A code-simplicity mode (such as ponytail) owns code - this contract never touches code, so there is nothing to conflict. Each mode keeps its own domain: terse chat, lazy code, human prose. No bold-for-emphasis inside prose sentences. No emojis in prose. Semicolons and parentheses where a writer would naturally use them.\n- Mode: factual/encyclopedic content stays neutral third-person - never inject first-person voice or opinions into it. Use when asked to verify a PR, confirm a fix works, test a change manually, check that a feature works, or validate local changes before pushing.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). ---
```text
CRITICAL!!: 
- NEVER EVER EVER create a branch!!! branches are FORBIDDEN!!!
- NEVER EVER EVER delete the `bugdrop-screenshots` branch, locally or remotely. It is protected project history.
- NEVER create a worktree, UNLESS the user has specifically asked for it!
- NEVER delete files unless the user has specifically asked for it. If you think a file is misplaced, unrelated, or stale, that is an entirely separate request — ask first. "Looks unrelated to this task" is not authorization to delete.

- When the user gives a UUID and asks what is going on with it, treat it as an OmniHarness conversation/session lookup: check `sqlite.db`, starting with the `runs` row for that UUID, then inspect related `workers`, `messages`, `execution_events`, queued messages, and validation/plan records as needed.
- To delete all conversations and associated persisted artifacts, use `scripts/delete-conversations.sh`

Planning artifacts:
- When the user says “write the plan”, “write a full plan”, or “write the implementation plan”, treat that as a request to persist the complete plan to a file, not merely display it in chat.
- If the user gives a path, write the plan there. Otherwise use `docs/superpowers/plans/YYYY-MM-DD-<kebab-case-feature-name>.md`, using the current date and a non-colliding filename.
- The saved file is the canonical full plan. The final response should link to it and summarize the result rather than replacing the file with a chat-only plan.
- This convention applies even when the current message omits the destination because “write the plan” is the project’s established shorthand for creating the plan artifact.
- If the same request explicitly says not to change the workspace or not to create a file, obey that more specific prohibition and ask for a destination when needed.

Testing:
- After any UI/frontend change, always run `pnpm build:interface:web` before considering the work complete.
- When testing the app, use the already-running process if one exists instead of starting another server.
- The normal runner URL is `http://localhost:3050`; the Vite development interface is usually at `http://localhost:5173`.
- Clean up any test sessions/conversations and their associated persisted artifacts before finishing.
- For lifecycle / chaos-style regressions (reconnect, restart, FK-on-delete, plan-review leftover state), use `pnpm test:lifecycle`. Scenarios live under `tests/lifecycle/scenarios/` and drive the control plane via HTTP/SSE — no Chromium. To debug a specific reported bug, mirror it as a new scenario file.
- Read `docs/architecture/lifecycle-observability-and-testing.md` before adding new server-side state transitions. It is the spec all new code is held to.
- Worker conversation content (direct-control and supervisor-driven worker turns) lives in the unified worker stream — one append-only JSONL per worker at `app-data/run-data/<runId>/<workerId>.jsonl`. Persist new worker content through `appendWorkerEntry` in `src/server/workers/output-store.ts`; render it through `WorkerEntriesManager` and the `entries` prop on `Terminal`. Do NOT add a parallel persistence layer (a sibling JSONL, a new `messages.kind`, an in-memory cache that the frontend reconciles against the stream). See `docs/architecture/worker-conversation-stream.md`.

Lifecycle observability rules (full doc: `docs/architecture/lifecycle-observability-and-testing.md`):
- Every server-side decision (spawn, reattach, recreate, give up, refuse, delete, fail) emits a typed named event via `emitNamedEvent` from `@/server/events/named-events`. Silent early returns and bare `catch {}` are bugs.
- User-relevant failures additionally emit `error.surfaced` with a stable `code` (typed union in `named-events.ts`), `surface`, and at least one relevant subject id such as `runId`/`workerId`/`conversationId`/`accountId`. Never funnel through a blanket wrapper in `api-errors.ts`.
- All SSE frames carry an `id:`. Clients reconnect with `Last-Event-ID`; the server replays from the ring buffer or emits `stream.resync_required`. Snapshot bootstrap is `GET /api/events?snapshot=1` (anchor id in the `x-omni-last-event-id` response header).
- Dev-only event log: `GET /api/events/log?since=<id>&runId=<id>` returns the ring buffer as JSON. Use this when triaging "X didn't happen" bug reports — if the event isn't there, the server didn't do the thing, and the next step is finding the silent branch.
- Chaos is a client-side concern. Never add fault-injection code paths to server code.

Frontend i18n:
- EVERY user-facing frontend string MUST live in `shared/locales/*.json` and be rendered with the `t()` function from `@/lib/i18n`.
- NEVER hardcode user-facing JSX text, button labels, dialog titles, aria-labels, titles, placeholders, empty states, status labels, help text, error fallback text, or visible option labels directly in components.
- For every new user-facing string, add the key to `shared/locales/en.json` and every other locale file in `shared/locales/` in the same change. Use stable dotted keys like `settings.runtime.recovery` or `conversation.sidebar.search`.
- Components that render translated strings and need to update when the language changes MUST subscribe to the i18n manager by calling `useI18nSnapshot()` from `@/lib/i18n`; then render text with the existing `t("key", params?)` function. The hook is only for re-rendering; `t()` is the translation API.
- Non-React modules that produce user-visible text must still use `t("key", params?)` at the boundary where text is produced. Protocol values, storage keys, API paths, CSS classes, model ids, and database values are not translation keys unless they are displayed as copy.
- Do not pass translated strings through transactions or persist translated UI copy. Store stable ids/values and translate at render time.

React Best Practices:
- MutableRefObject is deprecated in React; use RefObject instead.
- NEVER use require() to import modules. Use import instead.
- ALWAYS centralize all state in global single source of truth Manager classes. Components/listeners subscribe to updates from Managers and use Manager methods to update data.
- Always implement a custom data manager for any data structure. NEVER use separate state variables and arrays as the source of truth.
- NEVER send data around in transactions.
- Avoid using useEffect() to update state. Prefer useCallback() or Manager methods for explicit state transitions.
- Avoid callback hell, race conditions, and infinite loops.
- NEVER store string literals or UI settings for the frontend in the .env file. Use literals in files, inline arrays, or .json files instead.
- When debugging React: if a fix did not work, think hard about whether this is a race condition. Race conditions are common in React.
```

```text
CREATE TABLE process_sessions (
  run_id text PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
  worker_id text NOT NULL,
  cwd text NOT NULL,
  command_json text NOT NULL,
  command_preview text NOT NULL,
  env_policy text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'minimal',
  pid integer,
  status text NOT NULL,
  exit_code integer,
  signal text,
  started_at integer,
  exited_at integer,
  kill_escalated_at integer,
  last_error text,
  created_at integer NOT NULL,
  updated_at integer NOT NULL,
  FOREIGN KEY (run_id) REFERENCES runs(id) ON UPDATE no action ON DELETE no action,
  FOREIGN KEY (worker_id) REFERENCES workers(id) ON UPDATE no action ON DELETE no action
);
CREATE INDEX process_sessions_worker_idx ON process_sessions(worker_id);
CREATE INDEX process_sessions_status_idx ON process_sessions(status);
=== runs schema
CREATE TABLE runs (
  id text PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
  plan_id text NOT NULL,
  status text NOT NULL,
  created_at integer NOT NULL,
  updated_at integer NOT NULL, project_path text, title text, parent_run_id text, forked_from_message_id text, failed_at integer, last_error text, preferred_worker_type text, allowed_worker_types text, preferred_worker_model text, preferred_worker_effort text, mode text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'implementation', spec_path text, artifact_plan_path text, planner_artifacts_json text, archived_at integer, auto_commit_milestones integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, push_on_commit integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, git_baseline_json text, completion_commit_sha text, memory_metadata_revision integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, last_memory_consolidation_at integer, git_workspace_json text, planner_readiness_verdict_json text, session_type text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'omni', phase text, preferred_worker_account_id text, last_activity_at integer,
  FOREIGN KEY (plan_id) REFERENCES plans(id) ON UPDATE no action ON DELETE no action
);
CREATE INDEX runs_created_idx ON runs(created_at);
CREATE INDEX runs_archived_created_id_desc_idx ON runs(archived_at, created_at DESC, id DESC);
CREATE TRIGGER runs_activity_seed
AFTER INSERT ON runs
WHEN NEW.last_activity_at IS NULL
BEGIN
  UPDATE runs SET last_activity_at = NEW.created_at WHERE id = NEW.id;
END;
CREATE INDEX runs_archived_activity_id_desc_idx ON runs(archived_at, last_activity_at DESC, id DESC);
```

```text
CREATE TABLE execution_events (

);

  run_id text PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,

);
```

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-16T17:14:33.357Z

I found the key detail: the session limit message says it resets at 6pm Europe/Madrid, which checks out as 16:00 UTC since Madrid is on CEST. The resumeAt timestamp of 16:00:01Z also lines up correctly with 18:00:01 local time, confirming the parsing is accurate.

Now I'm tracing what happened around the wake time — the run's updated_at at 18:00:03 matches the scheduled wake, and the incident resolved at 18:15:45 with a run_terminal reason and done status. There's also an earlier, separate incident at 15:06:45 with a quota_recovery_refused failure that I need to keep distinct from this one.

I'm also noticing a massive spam of quota_wait_preserved events firing roughly once per second, which looks like a reload reconciliation bug on its own. I want to filter those out and query the events after 17:40 to see what's actually happening around the wake and resolution window.

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-16T17:15:19.311Z

So the worker actually did finish that interrupted turn at 18:15:45 local, producing the "Next step when you want it" response — but by the time it resumed, the run had already been marked done, and the harness rejected the recovery callback.

I'm trying to pin down who marked the run done and when. The updated_at timestamp of 18:00:03 matches the resume, but if something later flipped the status to done, that write either didn't touch updated_at or happened before the resume. Looking at the quota_wait_preserved events spanning 15:41 to 18:00:01, they all show previousStatus as running, which means the run stayed running through that whole window before the 18:00:03 resume — so the done marking must have happened sometime after that.

My working theory is that the worker going idle at 18:00:03 triggered the run to be marked done, since the resume likely reattached the session and saw the worker idle before the agent process had actually started emitting output, wrongly concluding the conversation had finished. That would explain why the real completion at 18:15:45 got refused as a late callback on a terminal run. I want to verify this against the actual code rather than just assert it, so I should look for where run status gets set to done and trace the quota recovery path, then report findings rather than attempt a fix since the user's asking a diagnostic question.

```text
34	function truncate(value: string, maxLength = 2_000) {
35	  return value.length > maxLength ? `${value.slice(0, maxLength)}...` : value;
36	}
37	
38	function isCancelledWorkerStatus(status: string | null | undefined) {
39	  const normalized = normalizeRunStatus(status);
40	  return normalized === "cancelled" || normalized === "canceled";
41	}
42	
43	function isTerminalRecoveryRunStatus(status: string | null | undefined) {
44	  return isTerminalRunStatus(status) || normalizeRunStatus(status) === "canceled";
45	}

60	      .where(and(
61	        eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, args.runId),
62	        eq(recoveryIncidents.kind, "quota_exhausted"),
63	        inArray(recoveryIncidents.status, ["open", "recovering"]),
64	        ...(args.incidentId ? [eq(recoveryIncidents.id, args.incidentId)] : []),
65	      ))
66	      .limit(1)
67	      .get()
68	    : null;
69	  const staleFailedRunOwnedByQuota = normalizeRunStatus(run?.status) === "failed" && Boolean(quotaIncident);
70	  const reason = !run
71	    ? "run_missing" as const
72	    : isTerminalRecoveryRunStatus(run.status) && !staleFailedRunOwnedByQuota
73	      ? "run_terminal" as const
74	      : isCancelledWorkerStatus(worker?.status)
75	        ? "worker_cancelled" as const
76	        : null;
77	  if (!reason) {
78	    return null;
79	  }
80	
81	  // Stop writes both the run and worker terminal barriers. If a quota callback
82	  // had already passed its first check and overwrote the worker afterward,
83	  // restore the worker side of that barrier before returning.
84	  if (
85	    run
86	    && reason === "run_terminal"
87	    && isCancelledWorkerStatus(run.status)
88	    && worker
89	    && !isCancelledWorkerStatus(worker.status)
90	  ) {
91	    const restored = await db.update(workers).set({
92	      status: "cancelled",
93	      updatedAt: args.now,
94	    }).where(and(
95	      eq(workers.id, worker.id),
96	      eq(workers.status, worker.status),
97	    )).returning({ id: workers.id });
98	    if (restored.length > 0) {
99	      emitNamedEvent({
100	        kind: "worker.status",
101	        runId: args.runId,
102	        workerId: worker.id,
103	        prev: worker.status,
104	        next: "cancelled",
105	      });
106	      emitNamedEvent({
107	        kind: "worker.terminal",
108	        runId: args.runId,
109	        workerId: worker.id,
110	        status: "cancelled",
111	      });
112	    }
113	  }
114	
115	  if (run && args.incidentId) {
116	    await markRecoveryIncidentResolved({
117	      incidentId: args.incidentId,
118	      runId: args.runId,
119	      workerId: args.workerId ?? null,
120	      summary: "Ignored quota recovery because the user-owned terminal state is authoritative.",
121	      details: { reason, runStatus: run.status },
122	    });
123	  }
124	
125	  if (run) {
126	    await insertQuotaEvent(args.runId, args.workerId ?? null, "quota_recovery_refused", {
127	      summary: "Ignored a late quota recovery callback after the conversation became terminal.",
128	      reason,
129	      runStatus: run.status,
130	      incidentId: args.incidentId ?? null,
131	    }, args.now);
132	  }
133	  emitNamedEvent({
134	    kind: "recovery.refused",
135	    runId: args.runId,
136	    workerId: args.workerId ?? null,
137	    reason,
138	    runStatus: run?.status ?? null,
139	  });
140	  return {
141	    state: "ignored",
142	    runId: args.runId,
143	    reason,
144	    runStatus: run?.status ?? null,
145	  };
146	}
147	
148	export async function transitionRunForQuotaRecovery(args: {
149	  runId: string;
150	  workerId?: string | null;
151	  incidentId: string;
152	  now: Date;
153	  updates: {
154	    status: string;
155	    failedAt?: Date | null;
156	    lastError?: string | null;
157	    updatedAt: Date;
158	  };
159	}): Promise<Extract<QuotaRecoveryResult, { state: "ignored" }> | null> {
160	  // Compare-and-swap the status that granted recovery ownership. If Stop wins
161	  // between the read and write, retrying observes the terminal state and emits
162	  // the refusal instead of overwriting it.
163	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 4; attempt += 1) {
164	    const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery(args);
165	    if (refused) {
166	      return refused;
167	    }
168	    const currentRun = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, args.runId)).get();
169	    if (!currentRun) {
170	      continue;
171	    }
172	    const transitioned = await db.update(runs).set(args.updates).where(and(
173	      eq(runs.id, args.runId),
174	      eq(runs.status, currentRun.status),
175	    )).returning({ id: runs.id });
176	    if (transitioned.length > 0) {
177	      return null;
178	    }
179	  }
180	
181	  const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery(args);
182	  if (refused) {
183	    return refused;
184	  }
185	  throw new Error(`Could not claim quota recovery ownership for run ${args.runId}.`);
186	}

188	async function insertQuotaEvent(
189	  runId: string,
190	  workerId: string | null,
191	  eventType: string,
192	  details: Record<string, unknown>,
193	  now: Date,
194	) {
195	  await recordExecutionEvent({
196	    runId,
197	    workerId,
198	    planItemId: null,
199	    eventType,
200	    details,
201	    createdAt: now,
202	  });
203	}
204	
205	function quotaIncidentDetails(args: {
206	  quota: QuotaResetInfo;
207	  resetAt: Date | null;
208	  resumeAt: Date | null;
209	  scheduledWakeAt?: Date | null;
210	  sourceType: "supervisor" | "worker";
211	  failoverPending?: boolean;
212	  accountId?: string | null;
213	}) {
214	  const details: Record<string, unknown> = {
215	    recoveryState: args.resumeAt ? "quota_waiting" : "needs_recovery",
216	    recommendedAction: args.resumeAt ? "wait_for_quota_reset" : "manual_resume",
217	    sourceType: args.sourceType,
218	    resetAt: args.resetAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
219	    resumeAt: args.resumeAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
220	    scheduledWakeAt: args.scheduledWakeAt?.toISOString() ?? args.resumeAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
221	    quotaResetSource: args.quota.source,
222	    quotaResetConfidence: args.quota.confidence,
223	    retryAfterMs: args.quota.retryAfterMs,
224	    provider: args.quota.provider ?? null,
225	    accountId: args.accountId ?? null,
226	    rawText: truncate(args.quota.rawText),
227	  };
228	  if (args.failoverPending) {
229	    details.failover_pending = true;
230	  }
231	  return details;
232	}
233	
234	async function computeResumeAt(quota: QuotaResetInfo, now: Date) {
235	  const policy = await getRecoveryPolicy();
236	  return {
237	    policy,
238	    resumeAt: normalizeQuotaResumeAt(quota, {
239	      now,
240	      quotaResetGraceMs: policy.quotaResetGraceMs,
241	      maxQuotaWaitMs: policy.maxQuotaWaitMs,
242	      allowQuotaWaitWithoutParsedReset: policy.allowQuotaWaitWithoutParsedReset,
243	    }),
244	  };
245	}
246	
247	/**
248	 * Step (a) of quota handling: mark the worker `cred-exhausted` and open
249	 * the recovery incident. Does NOT park the run. Used by the failover
250	 * path which wants to record the block before deciding whether to
251	 * actually transition the run.
252	 *
253	 * Optionally marks `details.failover_pending: true` on the incident so
254	 * the supervisor wake handler can detect that a failover attempt is
255	 * still owed for this incident.
256	 */
257	export async function recordWorkerQuotaBlock(args: {
258	  runId: string;
259	  workerId: string;
260	  text: string;
261	  provider?: string | null;
262	  now?: Date;
263	  failoverPending?: boolean;
264	}): Promise<RecordWorkerQuotaBlockResult> {
265	  const now = args.now ?? new Date();
266	  const quota = parseQuotaResetText(args.text, {
267	    now,
268	    provider: args.provider,
269	  });
270	  const { resumeAt } = await computeResumeAt(quota, now);
271	  const allocation = await db.select()
272	    .from(workerCredentialAllocations)
273	    .where(eq(workerCredentialAllocations.workerId, args.workerId))
274	    .get();
275	  const details = quotaIncidentDetails({
276	    quota,
277	    resetAt: quota.resetAt,
278	    resumeAt,
279	    sourceType: "worker",
280	    failoverPending: args.failoverPending ?? false,
281	    accountId: allocation?.accountId ?? null,
282	  });
283	
284	  return runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.runId, async () => {
285	
286	  const preMutationRefusal = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
287	    runId: args.runId,
288	    workerId: args.workerId,
289	    now,
290	  });
291	  if (preMutationRefusal) {
292	    return preMutationRefusal;
293	  }
294	
295	  // CAS the worker status that granted this callback authority. A concurrent
296	  // Stop either changes it first (so this update loses) or runs afterward and
297	  // cancels it during Stop's worker sweep.
298	  let workerClaimed = false;
299	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 4; attempt += 1) {
300	    const currentWorker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, args.workerId)).get();
301	    const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
302	      runId: args.runId,
303	      workerId: args.workerId,
304	      now,
305	    });
306	    if (refused) {
307	      return refused;
308	    }
309	    if (!currentWorker) {
310	      throw new Error(`Cannot record quota block for missing worker ${args.workerId}.`);
311	    }
312	    const claimed = await db.update(workers).set({
313	      status: "cred-exhausted",
314	      updatedAt: now,
315	    }).where(and(
316	      eq(workers.id, args.workerId),
317	      eq(workers.status, currentWorker.status),
318	    )).returning({ id: workers.id });
319	    if (claimed.length > 0) {
320	      workerClaimed = true;
321	      break;
322	    }
323	  }
324	  if (!workerClaimed) {
325	    const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({ runId: args.runId, workerId: args.workerId, now });
326	    if (refused) {
327	      return refused;
328	    }
329	    throw new Error(`Could not claim quota recovery ownership for worker ${args.workerId}.`);
330	  }
331	
332	  const postWorkerRefusal = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
333	    runId: args.runId,
334	    workerId: args.workerId,
335	    now,
336	  });
337	  if (postWorkerRefusal) {
338	    return postWorkerRefusal;
339	  }
340	
341	  const incident = await openRecoveryIncident({
342	    runId: args.runId,
343	    workerId: args.workerId,
344	    kind: "quota_exhausted",
345	    lastError: truncate(quota.rawText),
346	    details,
347	  });
348	
349	  const postIncidentRefusal = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
350	    runId: args.runId,
351	    workerId: args.workerId,
352	    incidentId: incident.id,
353	    now,
354	  });
355	  if (postIncidentRefusal) {
356	    return postIncidentRefusal;
357	  }
358	
359	  await insertQuotaEvent(args.runId, args.workerId, "quota_block_recorded", {
360	    summary: "Recorded worker quota block.",
361	    incidentId: incident.id,
362	    ...details,
363	  }, now);
364	
365	  return { incidentId: incident.id, quota, resumeAt, details };
366	  });
367	}
368	
369	/**
370	 * Step (b) of quota handling: transition the run into `quota_waiting`
371	 * (or `needs_recovery` if the reset cannot be scheduled) and schedule
372	 * the durable supervisor wake. Used after `recordWorkerQuotaBlock` when
373	 * failover gave up, OR directly when there is no worker context.
374	 *
375	 * Clears `failover_pending` on the incident — by the time we park, we
376	 * are no longer deferring to failover.
377	 */
378	export async function parkRunForQuotaWait(args: {
379	  runId: string;
380	  workerId?: string | null;
381	  incidentId: string;
382	  quota: QuotaResetInfo;
383	  now?: Date;
384	}): Promise<QuotaRecoveryResult> {
385	  const now = args.now ?? new Date();
386	  const policy = await getRecoveryPolicy();
387	  const resumeAt = normalizeQuotaResumeAt(args.quota, {
388	    now,
389	    quotaResetGraceMs: policy.quotaResetGraceMs,
390	    maxQuotaWaitMs: policy.maxQuotaWaitMs,
391	    allowQuotaWaitWithoutParsedReset: policy.allowQuotaWaitWithoutParsedReset,
392	  });
393	  const details = quotaIncidentDetails({
394	    quota: args.quota,
395	    resetAt: args.quota.resetAt,
396	    resumeAt,
397	    sourceType: args.workerId ? "worker" : "supervisor",
398	  });
399	
400	  return runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.runId, async () => {
401	
402	  if (!resumeAt || !policy.autoResumeAfterQuotaReset) {
403	    const refused = await transitionRunForQuotaRecovery({
404	      runId: args.runId,
405	      workerId: args.workerId,
406	      incidentId: args.incidentId,
407	      now,
408	      updates: {
409	        status: "needs_recovery",
410	        lastError: truncate(args.quota.rawText),
411	        updatedAt: now,
412	      },
413	    });
414	    if (refused) {
415	      return refused;
416	    }
417	    await markRecoveryIncidentNeedsUser({
418	      incidentId: args.incidentId,
419	      runId: args.runId,
420	      workerId: args.workerId ?? null,
421	      reason: "Quota reset time could not be scheduled automatically.",
422	      details,
423	    });
424	    const postIncidentRefusal = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
425	      runId: args.runId,
426	      workerId: args.workerId,
427	      incidentId: args.incidentId,
428	      now,
429	    });
430	    if (postIncidentRefusal) {
431	      return postIncidentRefusal;
432	    }
433	    await insertQuotaEvent(args.runId, args.workerId ?? null, "quota_wait_unschedulable", {
434	      summary: "Quota exhaustion requires manual recovery.",
435	      incidentId: args.incidentId,
436	      ...details,
437	    }, now);
438	    return {
439	      state: "needs_recovery",
440	      runId: args.runId,
441	      incidentId: args.incidentId,
442	      quota: args.quota,
443	    };
444	  }
445	
446	  const refused = await transitionRunForQuotaRecovery({
447	    runId: args.runId,
448	    workerId: args.workerId,
449	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
450	    now,
451	    updates: {
452	      status: "quota_waiting",
453	      failedAt: null,
454	      lastError: null,
455	      updatedAt: now,
456	    },
457	  });
458	  if (refused) {
459	    return refused;
460	  }
461	
462	  await scheduleDurableSupervisorWakeAt({
463	    runId: args.runId,
464	    wakeAt: resumeAt,
465	    reason: "quota_wait",
466	    source: args.quota.source,
467	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
468	    details: {
469	      ...details,
470	      incidentId: args.incidentId,
471	    },
472	  });
473	
474	  // Scheduling and Stop are separate storage operations. If Stop lands after
475	  // the run transition but before the wake insert completes, remove the wake
476	  // here; if it lands after this check, Stop's own cleanup removes it.
477	  const postScheduleRefusal = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
478	    runId: args.runId,
479	    workerId: args.workerId,
480	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
481	    now,
482	  });
483	  if (postScheduleRefusal) {
484	    await cancelDurableSupervisorWake(args.runId, "quota_wait");
485	    return postScheduleRefusal;
486	  }
487	
488	  await insertQuotaEvent(args.runId, args.workerId ?? null, "quota_wait_scheduled", {
489	    summary: "Scheduled supervisor resume after quota reset.",
490	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
491	    ...details,
492	    scheduledWakeAt: resumeAt.toISOString(),
493	  }, now);
494	
495	  return {
496	    state: "quota_wait",
497	    runId: args.runId,
498	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
499	    resumeAt,
500	    quota: args.quota,
501	  };
502	  });
503	}

505	async function handleSupervisorOnlyQuotaExhaustion(args: {
506	  runId: string;
507	  quota: QuotaResetInfo;
508	  now?: Date;
509	}) {
510	  const now = args.now ?? new Date();
511	  const { resumeAt } = await computeResumeAt(args.quota, now);
512	  const details = quotaIncidentDetails({
513	    quota: args.quota,
514	    resetAt: args.quota.resetAt,
515	    resumeAt,
516	    sourceType: "supervisor",
517	  });
518	  const incident = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.runId, async () => {
519	    const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({ runId: args.runId, now });
520	    if (refused) {
521	      return refused;
522	    }
523	    return openRecoveryIncident({
524	      runId: args.runId,
525	      workerId: null,
526	      kind: "quota_exhausted",
527	      lastError: truncate(args.quota.rawText),
528	      details,
529	    });
530	  });
531	  if ("state" in incident) {
532	    return incident;
533	  }
534	  return parkRunForQuotaWait({
535	    runId: args.runId,
536	    workerId: null,
537	    incidentId: incident.id,
538	    quota: args.quota,
539	    now,
540	  });
541	}
542	
543	export async function handleSupervisorQuotaExhaustion(args: {
544	  runId: string;
545	  error: unknown;
546	  provider?: string | null;
547	  now?: Date;
548	}) {
549	  const quota = extractQuotaResetInfo(args.error, {
550	    now: args.now,
551	    provider: args.provider,
552	  });
553	  return handleSupervisorOnlyQuotaExhaustion({
554	    runId: args.runId,
555	    quota,
556	    now: args.now,
557	  });
558	}
559	
560	/**
561	 * Backward-compatible wrapper: record the block and park the run. New
562	 * callers that want to attempt failover before parking should call
563	 * `recordWorkerQuotaBlock` then `parkRunForQuotaWait` themselves.
564	 */
565	export async function handleWorkerQuotaExhaustion(args: {
566	  runId: string;
567	  workerId: string;
568	  text: string;
569	  provider?: string | null;
570	  now?: Date;
571	}): Promise<QuotaRecoveryResult> {
572	  const now = args.now ?? new Date();
573	  const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
574	    runId: args.runId,
575	    workerId: args.workerId,
576	    now,
577	  });
578	  if (refused) {
579	    return refused;
580	  }
581	  const block = await recordWorkerQuotaBlock({
582	    runId: args.runId,
583	    workerId: args.workerId,
584	    text: args.text,
585	    provider: args.provider,
586	    now,
587	  });
588	  if ("state" in block) {
589	    return block;
590	  }
591	  return parkRunForQuotaWait({
592	    runId: args.runId,
593	    workerId: args.workerId,
594	    incidentId: block.incidentId,
595	    quota: block.quota,
596	    now,
597	  });
598	}
```

```text
62	const MISSING_SESSION_EVENT_REASONS: Record<DirectQuotaResumeSource, string> = {
63	  durable_wake: "quota_wait",
64	  watchdog_sweep: "watchdog_sweep",
65	  manual_resume: "manual_resume",
66	};
67	
68	const RESUMABLE_QUOTA_INCIDENT_STATUSES = ["open", "recovering"] as const;
69	
70	function isAgentAlreadyExistsError(error: unknown, workerId: string) {
71	  const message = formatErrorMessage(error).toLowerCase();
72	  return message.includes("agent already exists") && message.includes(workerId.toLowerCase());
73	}
74	
75	function appendWorkerOutput(existingLog: string | null | undefined, nextChunk: string) {
76	  if (!nextChunk) {
77	    return existingLog ?? "";
78	  }
79	
80	  if (!existingLog) {
81	    return nextChunk;
82	  }
83	
84	  const separator = existingLog.endsWith("\n") || nextChunk.startsWith("\n") ? "" : "\n";
85	  return `${existingLog}${separator}${nextChunk}`;
86	}
87	
88	function shouldPromptResumedWorker(state: string | null | undefined) {
89	  return !/\b(working|running|busy|starting|pending)\b/i.test(state ?? "");
90	}
91	
92	function buildQuotaResumePrompt(worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect) {
93	  const planHint = worker.initialPrompt?.trim()
94	    ? "Use your original assignment and the saved session history as the source of truth."
95	    : "Use the saved session history and current repository state as the source of truth.";
96	
97	  return [
98	    "Continue the interrupted work now that the quota wait has cleared.",
99	    planHint,
100	    "Review where you left off, continue implementation or verification, and report clearly if anything is still blocked.",
101	  ].join(" ");
102	}
103	
104	async function insertWorkerSessionResumedEvent(args: {
105	  runId: string;
106	  workerId: string;
107	  sessionId: string;
108	  incidentId: string;
109	}) {
110	  emitNamedEvent({ kind: "worker.reattached", runId: args.runId, workerId: args.workerId });
111	  await recordExecutionEvent({
112	    runId: args.runId,
113	    workerId: args.workerId,
114	    planItemId: null,
115	    eventType: "worker_session_resumed",
116	    details: {
117	      summary: `Resumed ${args.workerId} from saved session after quota reset.`,
118	      sessionId: args.sessionId,
119	      incidentId: args.incidentId,
120	      reason: "quota_wait",
121	    },
122	  });
123	}
124	
125	async function promptResumedQuotaWorker(args: {
126	  runId: string;
127	  worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect;
128	  incidentId: string;
129	  turnGeneration: number;
130	}) {
131	  const prompt = buildQuotaResumePrompt(args.worker);
132	  const response = await runWorkerTurn(args.worker.id, () => askAgent(
133	    args.worker.id,
134	    prompt,
135	    undefined,
136	    { expectedTurnGeneration: args.turnGeneration },
137	  ));
138	  const deliveryRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.runId, async () => {
139	    const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
140	      runId: args.runId,
141	      workerId: args.worker.id,
142	      incidentId: args.incidentId,
143	      now: new Date(),
144	    });
145	    if (refused) return refused;
146	    if (!await isWorkerTurnGenerationCurrent(args.worker.id, args.turnGeneration)) {
147	      return { state: "superseded" as const };
148	    }
149	    const deliveredAt = new Date();
150	    await recordSupervisorIntervention({
151	      runId: args.runId,
152	      workerId: args.worker.id,
153	      prompt,
154	      summary: "Prompted worker to continue after quota reset.",
155	      interventionType: "recovery",
156	    });
157	    await appendSupervisorInputOnDelivery({
158	      runId: args.runId,
159	      workerId: args.worker.id,
160	      text: prompt,
161	      deliveredAt,
162	    });
163	    return null;
164	  });
165	  if (deliveryRefusal) {
166	    if (deliveryRefusal.state === "ignored") return deliveryRefusal;
167	    throw new Error(`Worker turn was superseded by a newer worker turn: ${args.worker.id}`);
168	  }
169	  let snapshot: AgentRecord | null = null;
170	  try {
171	    snapshot = await getAgent(args.worker.id, { retryIndefinitely: false });
172	    await persistWorkerSnapshot(args.worker.id, snapshot);
173	  } catch {
174	    // The ask response is still the durable fallback when the bridge snapshot is unavailable.
175	  }
176	  await appendAskResponseFallbackEntry({
177	    runId: args.runId,
178	    workerId: args.worker.id,
179	    responseText: response.response,
180	    snapshot,
181	  });
182	  const latestWorker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, args.worker.id)).get();
183	
184	  const persistenceRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.runId, async () => {
185	    const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
186	      runId: args.runId,
187	      workerId: args.worker.id,
188	      incidentId: args.incidentId,
189	      now: new Date(),
190	    });
191	    if (refused) return refused;
192	    const updated = await db.update(workers).set({
193	      status: response.state,
194	      currentText: "",
195	      lastText: "",
196	      outputLog: appendWorkerOutput(latestWorker?.outputLog ?? args.worker.outputLog, response.response),
197	      updatedAt: new Date(),
198	    }).where(and(
199	      eq(workers.id, args.worker.id),
200	      eq(workers.turnGeneration, args.turnGeneration),
201	    )).returning({ id: workers.id });
202	    if (updated.length === 0) return { state: "superseded" as const };
203	    await recordExecutionEvent({
204	      runId: args.runId,
205	      workerId: args.worker.id,
206	      planItemId: null,
207	      eventType: "worker_prompted",
208	      details: {
209	        summary: `Sent quota recovery follow-up to ${args.worker.id}`,
210	        prompt,
211	        reason: "quota_wait",
212	      },
213	      createdAt: new Date(),
214	    });
215	    return null;
216	  });
217	  if (persistenceRefusal) {
218	    if (persistenceRefusal.state === "ignored") return persistenceRefusal;
219	    throw new Error(`Worker turn was superseded by a newer worker turn: ${args.worker.id}`);
220	  }
221	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
222	  return null;
223	}
224	
225	export async function resumeQuotaExhaustedWorkers(args: {
226	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect;
227	}): Promise<ResumeQuotaWorkersResult> {
228	  const initialRefusal = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({ runId: args.run.id, now: new Date() });
229	  if (initialRefusal) return initialRefusal;
230	  const incidents = await db.select().from(recoveryIncidents).where(and(
231	    eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, args.run.id),
232	    eq(recoveryIncidents.kind, "quota_exhausted"),
233	    inArray(recoveryIncidents.status, ["open", "recovering"]),
234	  ));
235	  const workerIncidents = incidents.filter((incident) => incident.workerId);
236	  if (workerIncidents.length === 0) {
237	    return { state: "none", resumedCount: 0 };
238	  }
239	
240	  let resumedCount = 0;
241	  const yoloModeEnabled = await readWorkerYoloModeEnabled();
242	  const { env: envParams } = await readRuntimeEnvFromSettings();
243	  for (const candidateIncident of workerIncidents) {
244	    const worker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, candidateIncident.workerId ?? "")).get();
245	    const sessionId = worker?.bridgeSessionId?.trim();
246	    if (!worker || !sessionId) {
247	      continue;
248	    }
249	
250	    const incident = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.run.id, async () => {
251	      const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
252	        runId: args.run.id,
253	        workerId: worker.id,
254	        incidentId: candidateIncident.id,
255	        now: new Date(),
256	      });
257	      if (refused) return refused;
258	      return claimRecoveryIncident({
259	        incidentId: candidateIncident.id,
260	        runId: args.run.id,
261	        workerId: worker.id,
262	        decision: "resume_quota_worker",
263	        details: { sessionId, reason: "quota_wait" },
264	      });
265	    });
266	    if (!incident) {
267	      continue;
268	    }
269	    if ("state" in incident) return incident;
270	
271	    const workerMode = resolveWorkerLaunchMode(worker.bridgeSessionMode, yoloModeEnabled);
272	    const launchSelection = resolveWorkerLaunchSelection(worker, args.run, {
273	      accountId: await readWorkerAllocatedAccountId(worker.id),
274	    });
275	    const turnGeneration = await readWorkerTurnGeneration(worker.id);
276	    try {
277	      let resumedWorker;
278	      try {
279	        resumedWorker = await spawnAgent({
280	          type: worker.type,
281	          cwd: worker.cwd,
282	          name: worker.id,
283	          ...(workerMode ? { mode: workerMode } : {}),
284	          env: envParams,
285	          ...(launchSelection.accountId ? { accountId: launchSelection.accountId } : {}),
286	          ...(launchSelection.model ? { model: launchSelection.model } : {}),
287	          ...(launchSelection.effort ? { effort: launchSelection.effort } : {}),
288	          resumeSessionId: sessionId,
289	        });
290	      } catch (error) {
291	        if (!isAgentAlreadyExistsError(error, worker.id)) {
292	          throw error;
293	        }
294	        resumedWorker = await getAgent(worker.id, { retryIndefinitely: false });
295	      }
296	
297	      const resumeRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.run.id, async () => {
298	        const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
299	          runId: args.run.id,
300	          workerId: worker.id,
301	          incidentId: incident.id,
302	          now: new Date(),
303	        });
304	        if (refused) return refused;
305	        const updated = await db.update(workers).set({
306	          status: resumedWorker.state,
307	          bridgeSessionId: resumedWorker.sessionId ?? sessionId,
308	          bridgeSessionMode: resumedWorker.sessionMode ?? worker.bridgeSessionMode ?? null,
309	          currentText: resumedWorker.currentText ?? "",
310	          lastText: resumedWorker.lastText ?? "",
311	          updatedAt: new Date(),
312	        }).where(and(
313	          eq(workers.id, worker.id),
314	          eq(workers.turnGeneration, turnGeneration),
315	        )).returning({ id: workers.id });
316	        if (updated.length === 0) {
317	          return { state: "superseded" as const };
318	        }
319	        await insertWorkerSessionResumedEvent({
320	          runId: args.run.id,
321	          workerId: worker.id,
322	          sessionId,
323	          incidentId: incident.id,
324	        });
325	      // Resolve before prompting, not after. The successful resume is already
326	      // proof the quota window reopened; `promptResumedQuotaWorker` then awaits
327	      // a full agent turn, and sequencing the resolve behind it left the
328	      // incident `open` — and the "Waiting for quota reset" banner up — for the
329	      // entire turn, while the worker was visibly working. If the window did
330	      // not actually reopen, the ask below throws and the catch reopens a fresh
331	      // incident via `handleWorkerQuotaExhaustion`.
332	        await markRecoveryIncidentResolved({
333	          incidentId: incident.id,
334	          runId: args.run.id,
335	          workerId: worker.id,
336	          summary: "Worker session resumed after quota reset.",
337	          details: {
338	            recoveryState: "quota_resumed",
339	            recommendedAction: "none",
340	            sessionId,
341	          },
342	        });
343	        return null;
344	      });
345	      if (resumeRefusal) {
346	        void cancelAgent(worker.id).catch(() => undefined);
347	        if (resumeRefusal.state === "ignored") return resumeRefusal;
348	        throw new Error(`Worker turn was superseded by a newer worker turn: ${worker.id}`);
349	      }
350	      resumedCount += 1;
351	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
352	      if (shouldPromptResumedWorker(resumedWorker.state)) {
353	        const promptRefusal = await promptResumedQuotaWorker({
354	          runId: args.run.id,
355	          worker: {
356	            ...worker,
357	            status: resumedWorker.state,
358	            bridgeSessionId: resumedWorker.sessionId ?? sessionId,
359	            bridgeSessionMode: resumedWorker.sessionMode ?? worker.bridgeSessionMode ?? null,
360	          },
361	          incidentId: incident.id,
362	          turnGeneration,
363	        });
364	        if (promptRefusal) return promptRefusal;
365	      }
366	    } catch (error) {
367	      const caughtRefusal = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
368	        runId: args.run.id,
369	        workerId: worker.id,
370	        incidentId: incident.id,
371	        now: new Date(),
372	      });
373	      if (caughtRefusal) return caughtRefusal;
374	      if (isWorkerTurnSupersededError(error) || isWorkerTurnAbortedError(error)) {
375	        const supersededRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.run.id, async () => {
376	          const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
377	            runId: args.run.id,
378	            workerId: worker.id,
379	            incidentId: incident.id,
380	            now: new Date(),
381	          });
382	          if (refused) return refused;
383	          emitNamedEvent({
384	            kind: "worker.recovery_continuation_superseded",
385	            runId: args.run.id,
386	            workerId: worker.id,
387	          });
388	          await recordExecutionEvent({
389	            runId: args.run.id,
390	            workerId: worker.id,
391	            planItemId: null,
392	            eventType: "quota_resume_prompt_superseded",
393	            details: {
394	              summary: `Stopped the automatic quota-resume prompt for ${worker.id} because a newer turn took over.`,
395	              incidentId: incident.id,
396	            },
397	          });
398	          await markRecoveryIncidentResolved({
399	            incidentId: incident.id,
400	            runId: args.run.id,
401	            workerId: worker.id,
402	            summary: "Quota recovery handed control to a newer worker turn.",
403	            details: {
404	              recoveryState: "quota_resumed",
405	              recommendedAction: "none",
406	              sessionId,
407	              continuationSuperseded: true,
408	            },
409	          });
410	          return null;
411	        });
412	        if (supersededRefusal) return supersededRefusal;
413	        resumedCount += 1;
414	        continue;
415	      }
416	      const quotaInfo = extractQuotaResetInfo(error, { provider: worker.type });
417	      if (quotaInfo.isQuotaError) {
418	        return handleWorkerQuotaExhaustion({
419	          runId: args.run.id,
420	          workerId: worker.id,
421	          text: quotaInfo.rawText,
422	          provider: worker.type,
423	        });
424	      }
425	
426	      const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
427	      if (isTransientSupervisorError(error)) {
428	        const transientRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.run.id, async () => {
429	          const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
430	            runId: args.run.id,
431	            workerId: worker.id,
432	            incidentId: incident.id,
433	            now: new Date(),
434	          });
435	          if (refused) return refused;
436	          await db.update(recoveryIncidents).set({
437	            status: "open",
438	            lastError: reason,
439	            updatedAt: new Date(),
440	          }).where(eq(recoveryIncidents.id, incident.id));
441	          return null;
442	        });
443	        if (transientRefusal) return transientRefusal;
444	        throw error;
445	      }
446	
447	      const failureRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.run.id, async () => {
448	        const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({
449	          runId: args.run.id,
450	          workerId: worker.id,
451	          incidentId: incident.id,
452	          now: new Date(),
453	        });
454	        if (refused) return refused;
455	        await db.update(runs).set({
456	          status: "needs_recovery",
457	          failedAt: null,
458	          lastError: reason,
459	          updatedAt: new Date(),
460	        }).where(eq(runs.id, args.run.id));
461	        await db.update(workers).set({
462	          status: "error",
463	          currentText: "",
464	          updatedAt: new Date(),
465	        }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
466	        await markRecoveryIncidentNeedsUser({
467	          incidentId: incident.id,
468	          runId: args.run.id,
469	          workerId: worker.id,
470	          reason,
471	          details: {
472	            resumeFailed: true,
473	            sessionId,
474	            errorType: "non_quota_resume_failure",
475	          },
476	        });
477	        await recordExecutionEvent({
478	          runId: args.run.id,
479	          workerId: worker.id,
480	          planItemId: null,
481	          eventType: "quota_resume_failed",
482	          details: {
483	            summary: `Quota recovery could not resume ${worker.id}.`,
484	            incidentId: incident.id,
485	            reason,
486	            sessionId,
487	          },
488	        });
489	        return null;
490	      });
491	      if (failureRefusal) return failureRefusal;
492	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
493	      return {
494	        state: "needs_recovery" as const,
495	        runId: args.run.id,
496	        incidentId: incident.id,
497	        quota: quotaInfo,
498	      };
499	    }
500	  }
501	
502	  if (resumedCount > 0) {
503	    for (const incident of incidents.filter((candidate) => !candidate.workerId)) {
504	      await markRecoveryIncidentResolved({
505	        incidentId: incident.id,
506	        runId: args.run.id,
507	        workerId: null,
508	        summary: "Quota wait resolved after worker session resume.",
509	        details: {
510	          recoveryState: "quota_resumed",
511	          recommendedAction: "none",
512	        },
513	      });
514	    }
515	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
516	    return { state: "resumed", resumedCount };
517	  }
518	  return { state: "none", resumedCount: 0 };
519	}
520	
521	/**
522	 * Resume a non-implementation (direct/commit) run once its quota window has
523	 * reopened, and settle the run status afterwards.
524	 *
525	 * Callers must decide *whether* to resume from the open `quota_exhausted`
526	 * incident — never from `runs.status`. Direct-run status is rewritten by
527	 * conversation sync and the worker-output resolver, neither of which knows
528	 * about quota, so a run parked as `quota_waiting` can be flipped to
529	 * `running`/`done` before the reset lands.
530	 */
531	export async function resumeDirectRunAfterQuotaReset(args: {
532	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect;
533	  source: DirectQuotaResumeSource;
534	}): Promise<ResumeQuotaWorkersResult> {
535	  const transitionRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.run.id, async () => {
536	    const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({ runId: args.run.id, now: new Date() });
537	    if (refused) return refused;
538	    await db.update(runs).set({
539	      status: "running",
540	      failedAt: null,
541	      lastError: null,
542	      updatedAt: new Date(),
543	    }).where(eq(runs.id, args.run.id));
544	    return null;
545	  });
546	  if (transitionRefusal) return transitionRefusal;
547	
548	  const result = await resumeQuotaExhaustedWorkers({ run: args.run });
549	  if (result.state !== "none" || result.resumedCount !== 0) {
550	    return result;
551	  }
552	
553	  const reason = MISSING_SESSION_REASONS[args.source];
554	  const missingSessionRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.run.id, async () => {
555	    const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({ runId: args.run.id, now: new Date() });
556	    if (refused) return refused;
557	    await db.update(runs).set({
558	      status: "needs_recovery",
559	      lastError: reason,
560	      updatedAt: new Date(),
561	    }).where(eq(runs.id, args.run.id));
562	    await recordExecutionEvent({
563	      runId: args.run.id,
564	      eventType: "quota_resume_missing_session",
565	      details: { summary: reason, reason: MISSING_SESSION_EVENT_REASONS[args.source] },
566	    });
567	    emitNamedEvent({
568	      kind: "error.surfaced",
569	      code: "recovery.needs_user",
570	      message: reason,
571	      surface: "banner",
572	      runId: args.run.id,
573	    });
574	    return null;
575	  });
576	  if (missingSessionRefusal) return missingSessionRefusal;
577	  return result;
578	}
579	
580	function incidentResumeAt(details: string | null | undefined) {
581	  if (!details) {
582	    return null;
583	  }
584	  try {
585	    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(details);
586	    const value = parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed)
587	      ? (parsed as { resumeAt?: unknown }).resumeAt
588	      : null;
589	    if (typeof value !== "string") {
590	      return null;
591	    }
592	    const resumeAt = new Date(value);
593	    return Number.isNaN(resumeAt.getTime()) ? null : resumeAt;
594	  } catch {
595	    return null;
596	  }
597	}
598	
599	export async function hasResumableQuotaIncident(runId: string) {
600	  const incident = await db.select().from(recoveryIncidents).where(and(
601	    eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, runId),
602	    eq(recoveryIncidents.kind, "quota_exhausted"),
603	    inArray(recoveryIncidents.status, [...RESUMABLE_QUOTA_INCIDENT_STATUSES]),
604	  )).limit(1).get();
605	  return Boolean(incident);
606	}
607	
608	/**
609	 * Safety net for direct runs whose durable quota wake never delivered a resume
610	 * — the wake row is consumed before the handler decides what to do, so any bail
611	 * after the claim destroys the only retry record. Sweeps every open
612	 * `quota_exhausted` incident whose reset has already elapsed and resumes it.
613	 *
614	 * Runs with a future durable wake are left alone; that wake is still the
615	 * primary path. `claimRecoveryIncident` debounces re-entry for 60s, and a
616	 * failed resume moves the incident to `needs_user`, so repeated sweeps cannot
617	 * spin on the same run.
618	 */
619	export async function resumeElapsedQuotaWaits(options: { now?: Date } = {}) {
620	  const now = options.now ?? new Date();
621	  const incidents = await db.select().from(recoveryIncidents).where(and(
622	    eq(recoveryIncidents.kind, "quota_exhausted"),
623	    inArray(recoveryIncidents.status, [...RESUMABLE_QUOTA_INCIDENT_STATUSES]),
624	  ));
625	
626	  const elapsedRunIds = Array.from(new Set<string>(incidents
627	    .filter((incident) => {
628	      const resumeAt = incidentResumeAt(incident.details);
629	      return resumeAt !== null && resumeAt.getTime() <= now.getTime();
630	    })
631	    .map((incident) => incident.runId)));
632	
633	  let sweptCount = 0;
634	  let clearedCount = 0;
635	  for (const runId of elapsedRunIds) {
636	    const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
637	    if (!run || run.archivedAt) {
638	      continue;
639	    }
640	    if (normalizeRunStatus(run.status) === "cancelled" || normalizeRunStatus(run.status) === "canceled") {
641	      continue;
642	    }
643	    if (await hasFutureDurableSupervisorWake(runId, now.getTime())) {
644	      continue;
645	    }
646	
647	    // Implementation runs park at `quota_waiting`, which the watchdog loop
648	    // below deliberately skips — so a lost wake strands them exactly like a
649	    // direct conversation. Re-arm a due wake rather than resuming here, so the
650	    // normal (well-tested) wake handler does the work. This converges: the
651	    // handler either resolves the incident or moves the run out of
652	    // `quota_waiting`, and either outcome stops matching on the next sweep.
653	    if (run.mode === "implementation") {
654	      if (isRunnableImplementationRun(run) && normalizeRunStatus(run.status) === "quota_waiting") {
655	        const sweepRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(runId, async () => {
656	          const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({ runId, now });
657	          if (refused) return refused;
658	          await scheduleDurableSupervisorWakeAt({
659	            runId,
660	            wakeAt: now,
661	            reason: "quota_wait",
662	            source: "watchdog-sweep",
663	            force: true,
664	          });
665	          emitNamedEvent({ kind: "supervisor.quota_wake_swept", runId, status: run.status, action: "rescheduled" });
666	          return null;
667	        });
668	        if (!sweepRefusal) sweptCount += 1;
669	      }
670	      continue;
671	    }
672	
673	    const runWorkers = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.runId, runId));
674	    const blockedWorker = runWorkers.find((worker) => (
675	      worker.status === "cred-exhausted" && Boolean(worker.bridgeSessionId?.trim())
676	    ));
677	    if (!blockedWorker) {
678	      // The worker already came back on its own (or has no resumable session),
679	      // so the incident is stale bookkeeping that only keeps the "waiting for
680	      // quota reset" banner up. Close it without touching the agent — resuming
681	      // a conversation the user considers finished would start unrequested work.
682	      const { resolvedCount } = await clearResolvedQuotaIncidents(runId, { now });
683	      if (resolvedCount > 0) {
684	        emitNamedEvent({ kind: "supervisor.quota_wake_swept", runId, status: run.status, action: "cleared" });
685	        clearedCount += 1;
686	      }
687	      continue;
688	    }
689	
690	    const resumeResult = await resumeDirectRunAfterQuotaReset({ run, source: "watchdog_sweep" });
691	    if (resumeResult.state === "ignored") {
692	      continue;
693	    }
694	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "supervisor.quota_wake_swept", runId, status: run.status, action: "resumed" });
695	    await recordExecutionEvent({
696	      runId,
697	      eventType: "quota_wait_swept",
698	      details: {
699	        summary: "Quota reset had elapsed with no scheduled wake; resumed from the open incident.",
700	        runStatus: run.status,
701	        workerId: blockedWorker.id,
702	        reason: "watchdog_sweep",
703	      },
704	    });
705	    sweptCount += 1;
706	  }
707	
708	  return { sweptCount, clearedCount };
709	}
710
```

```text
1	import { and, eq, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
2	import { db } from "@/server/db";

8	
9	const ACTIVE_QUOTA_INCIDENT_STATUSES = ["open", "recovering"] as const;
10	
11	type OutputEntryLike = {
12	  type?: string | null;
13	  text?: string | null;
14	  status?: string | null;
15	  raw?: unknown;
16	};
17	
18	type WorkerOutputSource = {
19	  workerStatus?: string | null;
20	  responseText?: string | null;
21	  renderedOutput?: string | null;
22	  currentText?: string | null;
23	  lastText?: string | null;
24	  outputLog?: string | null;
25	  outputEntries?: readonly OutputEntryLike[] | null;
26	  outputEntriesJson?: string | null;
27	  pendingPermissions?: readonly unknown[] | null;
28	  pendingElicitations?: readonly unknown[] | null;
29	};
30	
31	const ACTIVE_DIRECT_WORKER_STATUSES = new Set(["starting", "working", "stuck", "recovering"]);
32	
33	function normalizeWorkerStatus(value: string | null | undefined) {
34	  return value?.trim().toLowerCase().split(":")[0]?.trim() ?? "";
35	}
36	
37	function parseOutputEntriesJson(value: string | null | undefined): OutputEntryLike[] {
38	  if (!value?.trim()) {
39	    return [];
40	  }
41	
42	  try {
43	    const parsed = JSON.parse(value) as unknown;
44	    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((entry): entry is OutputEntryLike => {
45	      return typeof entry === "object" && entry !== null;
46	    }) : [];
47	  } catch {
48	    return [];
49	  }
50	}
51	
52	function visibleEntryText(entries: readonly OutputEntryLike[] | null | undefined) {
53	  return (entries ?? [])
54	    .filter((entry) => !entry.type || entry.type === "message")
55	    .map((entry) => entry.text ?? "")
56	    .filter((text) => text.trim().length > 0);
57	}
58	
59	function latestVisibleEntryText(entries: readonly OutputEntryLike[] | null | undefined) {
60	  return visibleEntryText(entries).at(-1) ?? "";
61	}
62	
63	function firstNonEmptyText(values: ReadonlyArray<string | null | undefined>) {
64	  return values.find((value) => typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0) ?? "";
65	}
66	
67	function isOpenHumanInputEntry(entry: OutputEntryLike) {
68	  if (entry.type !== "permission" && entry.type !== "elicitation") {
69	    return false;
70	  }
71	  const status = (entry.status ?? "pending").trim().toLowerCase();
72	  return !["answered", "approved", "cancelled", "canceled", "completed", "declined", "denied", "failed", "rejected", "skipped"].includes(status);
73	}
74	
75	function entryRequestId(entry: OutputEntryLike) {
76	  const raw = entry.raw;
77	  if (typeof raw !== "object" || raw === null) {
78	    return null;
79	  }
80	  const requestId = (raw as { requestId?: unknown }).requestId;
81	  return typeof requestId === "number" && Number.isFinite(requestId) ? requestId : null;
82	}
83	
84	function entriesHaveOpenHumanInput(entries: readonly OutputEntryLike[]) {
85	  const pendingByRequestId = new Map<string, boolean>();
86	
87	  for (const entry of entries) {
88	    if (entry.type !== "permission" && entry.type !== "elicitation") {
89	      continue;
90	    }
91	
92	    const open = isOpenHumanInputEntry(entry);
93	    const requestId = entryRequestId(entry);
94	    if (requestId === null) {
95	      if (open) {
96	        return true;
97	      }
98	      continue;
99	    }
100	
101	    pendingByRequestId.set(`${entry.type}:${requestId}`, open);
102	  }
103	
104	  return [...pendingByRequestId.values()].some(Boolean);
105	}
106	
107	export function directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput(source: WorkerOutputSource) {
108	  return (
109	    (source.pendingPermissions?.length ?? 0) > 0
110	    || (source.pendingElicitations?.length ?? 0) > 0
111	    || entriesHaveOpenHumanInput(source.outputEntries ?? [])
112	    || entriesHaveOpenHumanInput(parseOutputEntriesJson(source.outputEntriesJson))
113	  );
114	}
115	
116	// Trailing "?", tolerating closing quotes/brackets and trailing punctuation.
117	const QUESTION_TAIL_PATTERN = /\?["'”’)\]]*[.\s]*$/;
118	
119	function endsWithQuestion(text: string) {
120	  const lastLine = text
121	    .trim()
122	    .split(/\r?\n/)
123	    .map((line) => line.trim())
124	    .filter(Boolean)

126	  return Boolean(lastLine && QUESTION_TAIL_PATTERN.test(lastLine));
127	}
128	
129	/**
130	 * A worker can block on the user without ever raising a structured permission
131	 * or elicitation — it just asks in prose and goes idle. Treating that as "done"
132	 * marks the conversation finished in the UI while the worker is in fact waiting
133	 * for an answer, so the question is never surfaced and never answered.
134	 */
135	export function directWorkerOutputAsksBlockingQuestion(source: WorkerOutputSource) {
136	  // Deliberately entry-derived only. `outputLog`/`lastText` on a persisted
137	  // worker row are stale prose that outlive the turn, and reconciling an
138	  // already-finished conversation off them would flip it back to awaiting_user
139	  // forever. Message entries only exist for a turn that actually just spoke.
140	  const latestText = firstNonEmptyText([
141	    latestVisibleEntryText(source.outputEntries),
142	    latestVisibleEntryText(parseOutputEntriesJson(source.outputEntriesJson)),
143	  ]);

145	  return endsWithQuestion(latestText);
146	}
147	
148	export function resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(source: WorkerOutputSource) {
149	  if (directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput(source)) {
150	    return "awaiting_user";
151	  }
152	
153	  // A quota-blocked worker is idle, but the conversation is emphatically not
154	  // finished — it is parked waiting for the provider window to reopen. Letting
155	  // it fall through to "done" below silently cleared the run out of
156	  // `quota_waiting`, which hid the "waiting for quota reset" banner and (until

158	  if (normalizeWorkerStatus(source.workerStatus) === "cred-exhausted") {
159	    return "quota_waiting";
160	  }
161	
162	  if (ACTIVE_DIRECT_WORKER_STATUSES.has(normalizeWorkerStatus(source.workerStatus))) {
163	    return "running";
164	  }
165	
166	  // Only once the worker has stopped: a question mid-turn is just narration.
167	  return directWorkerOutputAsksBlockingQuestion(source) ? "awaiting_user" : "done";
168	}

170	export async function updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(args: WorkerOutputSource & {
171	  runId: string;
172	  workerId?: string | null;
173	}) {
174	  const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, args.runId)).get();
175	  if (!run || (run.mode !== "direct" && run.mode !== "commit")) {
176	    return null;
177	  }
178	
179	  const quotaIncident = await db.select({ id: recoveryIncidents.id })
180	    .from(recoveryIncidents)
181	    .where(and(
182	      eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, args.runId),
183	      eq(recoveryIncidents.kind, "quota_exhausted"),
184	      inArray(recoveryIncidents.status, [...ACTIVE_QUOTA_INCIDENT_STATUSES]),
185	    ))
186	    .limit(1)

201	      return run.status;
202	    }
203	
204	    if (run.status !== "quota_waiting" || run.failedAt || run.lastError) {
205	      const updated = await db.update(runs).set({

238	  const nextStatus = resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(args);

247	  }
248	
249	  if (nextStatus === "awaiting_user" && run.status !== "awaiting_user") {
250	    emitNamedEvent({

252	      runId: args.runId,
253	      workerId: args.workerId ?? undefined,
254	      reason: "worker_requested_input",
255	    });
256	    await recordExecutionEvent({
257	      runId: args.runId,
258	      workerId: args.workerId ?? null,
259	      planItemId: null,
260	      eventType: "direct_worker_awaiting_user",
261	      details: { reason: "worker_requested_input" },
262	      createdAt: now,
263	    });
264	  } else if (run.mode === "direct" && nextStatus === "done" && run.status !== "done") {

270	      latestVisibleEntryText(parseOutputEntriesJson(args.outputEntriesJson)),

279	  return nextStatus;
280	}
281	
282	export async function updateDirectRunAwaitingUserInputIfRequested(args: WorkerOutputSource & {
283	  runId: string;
284	  workerId?: string | null;
285	}) {
286	  if (!directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput(args)) {

290	  await updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(args);

292	}
```

```text
27	
28	function isDirectRunMode(mode: string | null | undefined) {
29	  return mode === "direct" || mode === "commit";
30	}
31	
32	function hasAgentOutput(agent: ReturnType<typeof normalizeAgentRecord>) {
33	  return Boolean(
34	    agent.renderedOutput?.trim()
35	    || agent.currentText.trim()
36	    || agent.lastText.trim()
37	    || agent.outputEntries?.some((entry) => entry.text.trim()),
38	  );
39	}
40	
41	function normalizedStatus(value: string | null | undefined) {
42	  return value?.trim().toLowerCase().split(":")[0]?.trim() ?? "";
43	}
44	
45	function isCompletedEntryStatus(value: string | null | undefined) {
46	  const status = normalizedStatus(value);
47	  return !status || [
48	    "approved",
49	    "cancelled",
50	    "canceled",
51	    "completed",
52	    "denied",
53	    "error",
54	    "failed",
55	    "success",
56	  ].includes(status);
57	}
58	
59	function isInputEntry(entry: NonNullable<AgentRecord["outputEntries"]>[number]) {
60	  const type = (entry as { type?: string | null }).type;
61	  return type === "user_input" || type === "supervisor_input";
62	}
63	
64	function isOpenWorkEntry(entry: NonNullable<AgentRecord["outputEntries"]>[number]) {
65	  if (entry.type !== "tool_call" && entry.type !== "tool_call_update" && entry.type !== "permission" && entry.type !== "elicitation") {
66	    return false;
67	  }
68	
69	  return !isCompletedEntryStatus(entry.status);
70	}
71	
72	function hasPendingElicitationSignal(snapshot: ReturnType<typeof normalizeAgentRecord> | null) {
73	  return Boolean(
74	    (snapshot?.pendingElicitations?.length ?? 0) > 0
75	    || snapshot?.outputEntries?.some((entry) => entry.type === "elicitation" && !isCompletedEntryStatus(entry.status)),
76	  );
77	}
78	
79	function directLiveAgentHasCompletedTurn(agent: ReturnType<typeof normalizeAgentRecord>) {
80	  const state = normalizedStatus(agent.state);
81	  if (state !== "working" && state !== "starting" && state !== "stuck") {
82	    return false;
83	  }
84	
85	  if (!agent.stopReason?.trim() && !isLongWorkerCompletionText(agent.currentText || agent.lastText || agent.renderedOutput)) {
86	    return false;
87	  }
88	
89	  if ((agent.pendingPermissions?.length ?? 0) > 0) {

92	
93	  if ((agent.pendingElicitations?.length ?? 0) > 0) {

97	  const entries = agent.outputEntries ?? [];

100	  }

103	  const turnEntries = entries.slice(lastInputIndex + 1);
104	  if (turnEntries.some(isOpenWorkEntry)) {

119	  return latestMeaningfulEntry?.type === "message";
120	}
121	
122	async function hasPersistedWorkerOutput(worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect) {
123	  if (
124	    worker.outputLog.trim()

137	
138	function resolveSyncedRunState(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect, agent: ReturnType<typeof normalizeAgentRecord>) {

140	    return "failed";
141	  }
142	
143	  if (isDirectRunMode(run.mode) && resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput({ ...agent, workerStatus: agent.state }) === "awaiting_user") {
144	    return "awaiting_user";

162	  return "running";

164	
165	async function resolvePersistedRunState(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect, worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect) {

168	  if (status === "error") {
169	    return "failed";

172	  if (isDirectRunMode(run.mode) && resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput({ ...worker, workerStatus: worker.status }) === "awaiting_user") {
173	    return "awaiting_user";
174	  }
175	
176	  if (
177	    ["stopped", "cancelled", "done", "completed"].includes(status)
178	    || (status === "idle" && await hasPersistedWorkerOutput(worker))
179	  ) {

181	  }
182	
183	  return "running";
184	}
185	
186	async function isEmptyIdlePersistedWorker(worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect) {
187	  const status = normalizedStatus(worker.status);
188	  return status === "idle" && !(await hasPersistedWorkerOutput(worker));

240	async function recordQueueDrainDecision(args: {
241	  runId: string;
242	  workerId: string;
243	  source: string;
244	  workerStatus: string;
245	  pendingCount: number;
246	  decision: "drain" | "skip";
247	  reason: string;
248	}) {
249	  emitNamedEvent({
250	    kind: "queue.drain_decision",
251	    runId: args.runId,
252	    workerId: args.workerId,
253	    source: args.source,
254	    workerStatus: args.workerStatus,
255	    pendingCount: args.pendingCount,
256	    decision: args.decision,
257	    reason: args.reason,
258	  });
259	  await recordExecutionEvent({
260	    runId: args.runId,
261	    workerId: args.workerId,
262	    eventType: "queue_drain_decision",
263	    details: {
264	      summary: args.decision === "drain"
265	        ? `Draining ${args.pendingCount} queued message(s) for ${args.workerId}.`
266	        : `Skipped queue drain for ${args.workerId}: ${args.reason}.`,
267	      source: args.source,
268	      workerStatus: args.workerStatus,
269	      pendingCount: args.pendingCount,
270	      decision: args.decision,
271	      reason: args.reason,
272	    },
273	  });
274	}
275	
276	async function drainQueuedWorkerMessagesWithObservation(args: {
277	  runId: string;
278	  workerId: string;
279	  workerStatus: string;
280	  source: string;
281	  snapshot?: ReturnType<typeof normalizeAgentRecord> | null;
282	}) {
283	  const pendingCount = await pendingWorkerQueueCount(args.runId, args.workerId);
284	  if (pendingCount === 0) {
285	    return 0;
286	  }
287	
288	  const snapshot = args.snapshot ?? null;
289	  const hasPendingElicitation = hasPendingElicitationSignal(snapshot);
290	  const drainable = hasPendingElicitation || isWorkerQueueDrainableStatus(args.workerStatus);
291	  await recordQueueDrainDecision({
292	    ...args,
293	    pendingCount,
294	    decision: drainable ? "drain" : "skip",
295	    reason: hasPendingElicitation
296	      ? "pending_elicitation"
297	      : drainable
298	        ? "worker_drainable"
299	        : "worker_not_drainable",
300	  });
301	  if (!drainable) {
302	    return 0;
303	  }
304	
305	  // Deliver on a background task rather than inline. Sync passes are
306	  // serialized through `liveSyncQueue`, and the drain runs a full worker turn:
307	  // awaiting it here parked the entire sync chain for the length of the turn.
308	  // When the agent raised an elicitation mid-turn that became a deadlock — the
309	  // turn cannot finish until the question is answered, and the question is
310	  // only surfaced by the snapshot writes in the very sync pass that is stuck
311	  // waiting for the turn. Claiming is atomic (`status = 'pending'` guard), and
312	  // an in-flight delivery leaves `pendingCount` at 0, so overlapping passes
313	  // return above instead of double-delivering.
314	  const delivery = trackConversationBackgroundTask((async () => {
315	    const deliveredCount = await drainQueuedWorkerMessages({
316	      runId: args.runId,
317	      workerId: args.workerId,
318	      snapshot,
319	    });
320	    emitNamedEvent({
321	      kind: "queue.drain_finished",
322	      runId: args.runId,
323	      workerId: args.workerId,
324	      source: args.source,
325	      pendingCount,
326	      deliveredCount,
327	    });
328	    await recordExecutionEvent({
329	      runId: args.runId,
330	      workerId: args.workerId,
331	      eventType: "queue_drain_finished",
332	      details: {
333	        summary: `Queue drain finished for ${args.workerId}: delivered ${deliveredCount} of ${pendingCount}.`,
334	        source: args.source,
335	        pendingCount,
336	        deliveredCount,
337	      },
338	    });
339	    return deliveredCount;
340	  })(), { runId: args.runId });
341	  delivery.catch((error) => {
342	    console.error(`Queued message drain failed for ${args.workerId}:`, error);
343	  });
344	  return 0;
345	}

390	function compareWorkersForFollowUp(a: typeof workers.$inferSelect, b: typeof workers.$inferSelect) {
391	  const workerNumberDiff = (b.workerNumber ?? 0) - (a.workerNumber ?? 0);
392	  if (workerNumberDiff !== 0) {
393	    return workerNumberDiff;
394	  }
395	
396	  const createdAtDiff = workerCreatedAtMs(b) - workerCreatedAtMs(a);
397	  if (createdAtDiff !== 0) {
398	    return createdAtDiff;
399	  }
400	
401	  return b.id.localeCompare(a.id);
402	}

424	let bridgeSyncInFlight: Promise<void> | null = null;

426	/**
427	 * Watchdog-driven reconciliation between the bridge runtime and persisted
428	 * worker/run state. Turn completions are normally persisted by the awaiting
429	 * send-message call, or by the SSE-driven sync that runs while a client is
430	 * connected — but both die with their in-flight request (server restart,
431	 * dropped ACP roundtrip, phone backgrounded the tab). A worker then sits at
432	 * status='working' forever while the bridge agent finished its turn long
433	 * ago, and the stuck-worker reaper keeps cancelling and re-delivering the
434	 * same prompt every timeout window. This sweep closes the gap from the 15s
435	 * watchdog without requiring any connected client.
436	 */
437	export function syncConversationSessionsFromBridge(): Promise<void> {
438	  if (bridgeSyncInFlight) {
439	    return bridgeSyncInFlight;
440	  }
441	
442	  bridgeSyncInFlight = (async () => {
443	    let rawAgents: unknown[];
444	    try {
445	      rawAgents = await listAgents({ retryIndefinitely: false });
446	    } catch {
447	      // Bridge down or unreachable — there is no live state to reconcile
448	      // against; the next sweep will retry.
449	      return;
450	    }
451	    await syncConversationSessions(rawAgents);
452	  })().finally(() => {
453	    bridgeSyncInFlight = null;
454	  });
455	  return bridgeSyncInFlight;
456	}

458	type SyncConversationSessionsOptions = {
459	  selectedRunId?: string | null;
460	  refreshPlanningArtifacts?: boolean;
461	};

463	let liveSyncQueue: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();

465	export function __resetSyncConversationSessionsQueueForTests() {
466	  liveSyncQueue = Promise.resolve();
467	}

469	export async function syncConversationSessions(rawAgents: unknown[], options: SyncConversationSessionsOptions = {}) {
470	  const runSync = () => syncConversationSessionsUnlocked(rawAgents, options);
471	  const nextSync = liveSyncQueue.then(runSync, runSync);
472	  liveSyncQueue = nextSync.catch(() => undefined);
473	  return nextSync;
474	}

476	async function syncConversationSessionsUnlocked(rawAgents: unknown[], options: SyncConversationSessionsOptions = {}) {

491	    && (incident.status === "open" || incident.status === "recovering")
```

```text
2013	              prompt: intervention.prompt,

2015	            })),
2016	          });
2017	          await runMilestoneAutoCommit(this.runId, summary);
2018	          await insertRunMessage(this.runId, "supervisor", completionSummary, "completion");
2019	          return { state: "completed" };
2020	        }
2021	
2022	        case "mark_failed": {
2023	          const reason = asString(action.args.reason, "reason");
2024	          const staleBinaryMissingReason = isWorkerBinaryMissingReason(reason);
2025	          const staleAuthMissingReason = isWorkerAuthenticationMissingReason(reason);
2026	          if (staleBinaryMissingReason || staleAuthMissingReason) {
2027	            const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, this.runId)).get();
2028	            const allowedWorkerTypes = parseAllowedWorkerTypes(run?.allowedWorkerTypes);
2029	            const requestedType = run?.preferredWorkerType ?? allowedWorkerTypes[0] ?? "codex";
2030	            try {
2031	              selectSpawnableWorkerType(requestedType, envParams, allowedWorkerTypes, {
2032	                cwd: run?.projectPath ?? undefined,
2033	                loginShellPathMode: "blocking",
2034	              });
2035	              const authInfo = staleAuthMissingReason
2036	                ? getWorkerAuthenticationInfo(requestedType, { env: envParams })
2037	                : null;
2038	              if (authInfo?.status === "not_authenticated") {
2039	                throw new Error(authInfo.message);
2040	              }
2041	              await insertExecutionEvent(this.runId, "supervisor_turn_stopped", {
2042	                summary: staleAuthMissingReason
2043	                  ? "Rejected stale supervisor failure because the requested worker is currently authenticated."
2044	                  : "Rejected stale supervisor failure because the requested worker binary is currently spawnable.",
2045	                actionName: action.name,
2046	                evidenceKey: staleAuthMissingReason ? "mark_failed:worker_auth_missing" : "mark_failed:worker_binary_missing",
2047	                rejectedReason: reason,
2048	                requestedType,
2049	                authentication: authInfo,
2050	              });
2051	              return { state: "wait", delayMs: 1_000 };
2052	            } catch {
2053	              // The worker is still genuinely unavailable; proceed with the failure.
2054	            }
2055	          }
2056	          await cancelRunWorkers(this.runId);
2057	          const failHalt = await this.haltResultIfRunCannotContinue();
2058	          if (failHalt) {
2059	            return failHalt;
2060	          }
2061	          if (memoryEnabled && !isInfraFailureReason(reason)) {
2062	            try {
2063	              await consolidateProjectMemory({
2064	                runId: this.runId,
2065	                trigger: "failure",
2066	                outcomeSummary: reason,
2067	              });
2068	            } catch (consolidationError) {
2069	              await insertExecutionEvent(this.runId, "supervisor_memory_consolidation_failed", {
2070	                summary: "Memory consolidation failed during run failure; continuing.",
2071	                trigger: "failure",
2072	                error: formatSupervisorError(consolidationError),
2073	              });
2074	            }
2075	          }
2076	          await persistRunFailure(this.runId, reason, {
2077	            surface: { code: "supervisor.gave_up" },
2078	          });
2079	          return { state: "failed" };
2080	        }
2081	
2082	        default:
2083	          throw new SupervisorProtocolError(`Unknown tool "${action.name}".`);
2084	      }
2085	    }
2086	
2087	    await insertExecutionEvent(this.runId, "supervisor_turn_step_limit_reached", {
2088	      summary: `Supervisor turn reached the ${turnStepLimit} step limit before choosing a turn-ending action.`,
2089	      turnStepLimit,
2090	    });
2091	    return { state: "wait", delayMs: 1_000 };
2092	  }
2093	}
```
