704	async function hasExistingWorkerTurnCompletionForCurrentPrompt(runId: string, workerId: string) {
705	  const latestRelatedEvents = await db.select({
706	    eventType: executionEvents.eventType,
707	  }).from(executionEvents).where(and(
708	    eq(executionEvents.runId, runId),
709	    eq(executionEvents.workerId, workerId),
710	    inArray(executionEvents.eventType, WORKER_TURN_COMPLETION_RELATED_EVENT_TYPES),
711	  )).orderBy(desc(executionEvents.createdAt), desc(executionEvents.id)).limit(25);
712	
713	  for (const event of latestRelatedEvents) {
714	    if (event.eventType === WORKER_TURN_COMPLETED_EVENT_TYPE) {
715	      return true;
716	    }
717	
718	    if (WORKER_TURN_COMPLETION_RESET_EVENT_TYPES.includes(event.eventType)) {
719	      return false;
720	    }
721	  }
722	
723	  return false;
724	}
725	
726	async function insertExecutionEvent(
727	  runId: string,
728	  workerId: string,
729	  eventType: string,
730	  details: Record<string, unknown>,
731	) {
732	  if (eventType === WORKER_TURN_COMPLETED_EVENT_TYPE) {
733	    const duplicate = await hasExistingWorkerTurnCompletionForCurrentPrompt(runId, workerId);
734	    if (duplicate) {
735	      return false;
736	    }
737	  }
738	
739	  const detailsHash = hashArtifactPayload(details);
740	
741	  const dedupeScope = TYPE_DEDUPED_WORKER_EVENT_TYPES.has(eventType)
742	    ? "type"
743	    : EXACT_DEDUPED_WORKER_EVENT_TYPES.has(eventType)
744	      ? "exact"
745	      : null;
746	
747	  if (dedupeScope) {
748	    const now = Date.now();
749	    const eventKey = dedupeScope === "exact"
750	      ? `${runId}:${workerId}:${eventType}:${detailsHash}`
751	      : `${runId}:${workerId}:${eventType}`;
752	    const recentEventAt = recentWorkerEventKeys.get(eventKey);
753	    if (recentEventAt && now - recentEventAt < DUPLICATE_WORKER_EVENT_WINDOW_MS) {
754	      return false;
755	    }
756	    recentWorkerEventKeys.set(eventKey, now);
757	
758	    const duplicate = await db.select({ id: executionEvents.id }).from(executionEvents).where(
759	      dedupeScope === "exact"
760	        ? and(
761	          eq(executionEvents.runId, runId),
762	          eq(executionEvents.workerId, workerId),
763	          eq(executionEvents.eventType, eventType),
764	          eq(executionEvents.detailsHash, detailsHash),
765	          gt(executionEvents.createdAt, new Date(now - DUPLICATE_WORKER_EVENT_WINDOW_MS)),
766	        )
767	        : and(
768	          eq(executionEvents.runId, runId),
769	          eq(executionEvents.workerId, workerId),
770	          eq(executionEvents.eventType, eventType),
771	          gt(executionEvents.createdAt, new Date(now - DUPLICATE_WORKER_EVENT_WINDOW_MS)),
772	        ),
773	    ).get();
774	    if (duplicate) {
775	      return false;
776	    }
777	  }
778	
779	  await recordExecutionEvent({
780	    runId,
781	    workerId,
782	    planItemId: null,
783	    eventType,
784	    details,
785	  });
786	  await notifyRunLifecycleEventBestEffort({ runId, eventType, details });
787	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
788	  return true;
789	}
790	
791	async function loadActiveRun(runId: string) {
792	  const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
793	  // During the Omni planning phase the supervisor stays dormant — a stray
794	  // observer must self-terminate rather than treat the run as supervisable.
795	  if (isPlanningPhaseRun(run)) {
796	    return null;
797	  }
798	  return isActiveImplementationRun(run) ? run : null;
799	}

801	function isMissingAgentError(error: unknown) {
802	  const message = formatErrorMessage(error).toLowerCase();
803	  return message.includes("404")
804	    || message.includes("not_found")
805	    || message.includes("agent not found")
806	    || message.includes("session not found")
807	    || message.includes("invalid session identifier")
808	    || message.includes("failed to load resumed session data from file");
809	}
810	
811	function isAgentAlreadyExistsError(error: unknown, workerId: string) {
812	  const message = formatErrorMessage(error).toLowerCase();
813	  return message.includes("agent already exists") && message.includes(workerId.toLowerCase());
814	}

816	async function reviveWorkerFromSavedSession(args: {
817	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect;
818	  worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect;
819	  now: number;
820	  sessionId?: string | null;
821	  sessionMode?: string | null;
822	}) {

824	  if (!sessionId) {
825	    return null;
826	  }
827	  const sessionMode = args.sessionMode ?? args.worker.bridgeSessionMode;

829	  const workerMode = resolveWorkerLaunchMode(sessionMode, yoloModeEnabled);
830	  const { env: envParams } = await readRuntimeEnvFromSettings();
831	  const launchSelection = resolveWorkerLaunchSelection(args.worker, args.run);

833	    type: args.worker.type,

838	    ...(launchSelection.accountId ? { accountId: launchSelection.accountId } : {}),
839	    ...(launchSelection.model ? { model: launchSelection.model } : {}),
840	    ...(launchSelection.effort ? { effort: launchSelection.effort } : {}),
841	  };
842	
843	  let resumedWorker: bridge.AgentRecord;

845	  let missingSessionReason: unknown = null;

847	    resumedWorker = await bridge.spawnAgent({
848	      ...spawnParams,
849	      resumeSessionId: sessionId,

854	    } else if (isMissingAgentError(error)) {

859	        bridgeSessionMode: null,
860	        updatedAt: new Date(args.now),
861	      }).where(eq(workers.id, args.worker.id));

881	  } else {
882	    await insertExecutionEvent(args.run.id, args.worker.id, "worker_session_resumed", {

884	      sessionId,
885	    });
886	  }
887	
888	  await db.update(workers).set({
889	    status: resumedWorker.state,
890	    bridgeSessionId: resumedWorker.sessionId ?? (recreatedFromMissingSession ? null : sessionId),
891	    bridgeSessionMode: resumedWorker.sessionMode ?? sessionMode ?? null,
892	    updatedAt: new Date(args.now),
893	  }).where(eq(workers.id, args.worker.id));
894	  await reconcileRecoveredHumanInputEntries({
895	    runId: args.run.id,
896	    workerId: args.worker.id,
897	    activeElicitationRequestIds: (resumedWorker.pendingElicitations ?? []).map((entry) => entry.requestId),
898	    activePermissionRequestIds: (resumedWorker.pendingPermissions ?? []).map((entry) => entry.requestId),

1	const TERMINAL_RUN_STATUSES = new Set(["done", "failed", "cancelled", "promoting", "promoted"]);
2	const ARCHIVABLE_RUN_STATUSES = new Set(["done", "failed", "cancelled", "canceled", "promoted"]);
3	
4	export function normalizeRunStatus(status: string | null | undefined) {
5	  return (status ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
6	}
7	
8	export function isTerminalRunStatus(status: string | null | undefined) {
9	  return TERMINAL_RUN_STATUSES.has(normalizeRunStatus(status));
10	}
11	
12	export function isArchivableRunStatus(status: string | null | undefined) {
13	  return ARCHIVABLE_RUN_STATUSES.has(normalizeRunStatus(status));
14	}
15	
16	export function isPlanningPhaseRun(run: { phase?: string | null } | null | undefined) {
17	  return run?.phase === "planning";
18	}
19	
20	export function isActiveImplementationRun(run: { mode?: string | null; status?: string | null; phase?: string | null } | null | undefined) {
21	  return Boolean(run && run.mode === "implementation" && !isTerminalRunStatus(run.status));
22	}
23	
24	export function isRunnableImplementationRun(run: { mode?: string | null; status?: string | null; phase?: string | null } | null | undefined) {
25	  return isActiveImplementationRun(run)
26	    && !isPlanningPhaseRun(run)
27	    && normalizeRunStatus(run?.status) !== "awaiting_user";
28	}
29

1	import { recordExecutionEvent } from "@/server/events/execution-event-store";
2	import { and, desc, eq, gt, inArray, like } from "drizzle-orm";
3	import { db } from "@/server/db";
4	import { clarifications, executionEvents, runs, workers } from "@/server/db/schema";
5	import { persistRunFailure } from "@/server/runs/failures";
6	import { emitNamedEvent } from "@/server/events/named-events";
7	import { isActiveImplementationRun, isRunnableImplementationRun } from "@/server/runs/status";
8	import { Supervisor } from "@/server/supervisor";
9	import { isTransientSupervisorError } from "@/server/supervisor/retry";
10	import {
11	  hasResumableQuotaIncident,
12	  resumeDirectRunAfterQuotaReset,
13	  resumeQuotaExhaustedWorkers,
14	} from "@/server/quota/worker-resume";
15	import { clearResolvedQuotaIncidents } from "@/server/quota/type-blocking";
16	import { refuseLateQuotaRecovery } from "@/server/quota/recovery";
17	import { isRunPendingFailover } from "@/server/supervisor/worker-failover";
18	import { runQuotaRecoveryMutation } from "@/server/quota/recovery-mutation";
19	import { stopRunObserver } from "./observer";
20	import { acquireSupervisorWakeLease, clearSupervisorWakeLease, releaseSupervisorWakeLease } from "./lease";
21	import {
22	  cancelDurableSupervisorWake,
23	  claimDueDurableSupervisorWake,
24	  hasFutureDurableSupervisorWake,

27	} from "./wake-schedule";
28	
29	const wakeTimers = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
30	const wakeDeadlines = new Map<string, number>();
31	const inFlight = new Set<string>();
32	const LEASE_BLOCKED_RETRY_MS = 1_000;
33	const TRANSIENT_SUPERVISOR_RETRY_MS = 5_000;
34	const PRE_WORKER_LEASE_ORPHAN_GRACE_MS = 90_000;
35	const COMPLETION_EVENT_TYPES = new Set(["worker_turn_completed"]);
36	const COMPLETION_RESET_EVENT_TYPES = new Set(["worker_prompted", "worker_spawned", "worker_session_resumed", "worker_session_recreated"]);
37	const SESSION_RECREATED_RECOVERY_EVENT_TYPES = new Set(["worker_session_recreated", "worker_idle"]);
38	const PRE_WORKER_RECOVERY_EVENT_TYPES = new Set([
39	  "clarification_resolved",
40	  "supervisor_model_request_started",
41	  "supervisor_wake_retry_scheduled",
42	  "supervisor_wake_lease_recovered",
43	  "supervisor_turn_step_limit_reached",
44	  "supervisor_turn_stopped",
45	  "worker_selection_changed",
46	]);
47	const ACTIVE_WORKER_STATUS_PATTERN = /\b(working|stuck|starting|pending|busy|running)\b/i;
48	
49	/**
50	 * Whether a due wake should resume a direct/commit run's quota-blocked worker.
51	 *
52	 * `runs.status === "quota_waiting"` alone is not enough: conversation sync and
53	 * the direct worker-output resolver rewrite direct-run status without any quota
54	 * awareness, so a parked run can read `running` or `done` by the time the reset
55	 * lands. When that happened this branch fell through to `run_not_runnable` —
56	 * and because the wake row is claimed (deleted) before the check, the only
57	 * durable retry record was destroyed and the run hung forever. Fall back to the
58	 * open `quota_exhausted` incident, which no other path rewrites.
59	 */
60	async function shouldResumeQuotaWorkersWithoutSupervisor(
61	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect | null | undefined,
62	  dueDurableWake: { reason: string } | null,
63	): Promise<boolean> {
64	  if (!run || !dueDurableWake || run.mode === "implementation") {
65	    return false;
66	  }
67	  if (run.status === "quota_waiting") {
68	    return true;
69	  }
70	  return dueDurableWake.reason === "quota_wait" && await hasResumableQuotaIncident(run.id);
71	}
72	
73	function scheduleDurableWakeBackup(runId: string, nextDeadline: number, delayMs: number) {
74	  if (delayMs <= 0) {
75	    return;
76	  }
77	
78	  void scheduleDurableSupervisorWakeAt({
79	    runId,
80	    wakeAt: new Date(nextDeadline),
81	    reason: "supervisor_wait",
82	    source: "volatile-wake-backup",
83	    details: { delayMs },
84	  }).catch((error) => {
85	    emitNamedEvent({
86	      kind: "supervisor.durable_wake_schedule_failed",
87	      runId,
88	      reason: "supervisor_wait",
89	      source: "volatile-wake-backup",
90	      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
91	    });
92	  });
93	}
94	
95	function clearWake(runId: string) {
96	  const existing = wakeTimers.get(runId);
97	  if (existing) {
98	    clearTimeout(existing);
99	    wakeTimers.delete(runId);
100	  }
101	  wakeDeadlines.delete(runId);
102	}
103	
104	async function recoverCompletionBlockedByOrphanedLease(runId: string) {
105	  const runWorkers = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.runId, runId));
106	  if (runWorkers.length === 0) {
107	    return false;
108	  }
109	
110	  if (runWorkers.some((worker) => ACTIVE_WORKER_STATUS_PATTERN.test(worker.status))) {
111	    return false;
112	  }
113	
114	  const latestWorkerEvent = await db.select().from(executionEvents).where(
115	    inArray(executionEvents.workerId, runWorkers.map((worker) => worker.id)),
116	  ).orderBy(desc(executionEvents.createdAt), desc(executionEvents.id)).limit(1).get();
117	
118	  if (!latestWorkerEvent || COMPLETION_RESET_EVENT_TYPES.has(latestWorkerEvent.eventType)) {
119	    return false;
120	  }
121	
122	  if (!COMPLETION_EVENT_TYPES.has(latestWorkerEvent.eventType)) {
123	    return false;
124	  }
125	
126	  await clearSupervisorWakeLease(runId);
127	  emitNamedEvent({
128	    kind: "supervisor.wake_lease_recovered",
129	    runId,
130	    reason: "orphaned_completion",
131	  });
132	  await recordExecutionEvent({
133	    runId,
134	    eventType: "supervisor_wake_lease_recovered",
135	    details: {
136	      summary: "Cleared an orphaned supervisor wake lease after all workers were idle with completion evidence.",
137	      latestWorkerEventType: latestWorkerEvent.eventType,
138	      latestWorkerEventId: latestWorkerEvent.id,
139	    },
140	  });
141	  return true;
142	}
143	
144	async function recoverRecreatedWorkerBlockedByOrphanedLease(runId: string) {
145	  const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
146	  if (!run || run.mode !== "implementation" || run.status !== "running") {
147	    return false;
148	  }
149	
150	  const runWorkers = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.runId, runId));
151	  if (runWorkers.length === 0) {
152	    return false;
153	  }
154	
155	  if (runWorkers.some((worker) => ACTIVE_WORKER_STATUS_PATTERN.test(worker.status))) {
156	    return false;
157	  }
158	
159	  const workerIds = runWorkers.map((worker) => worker.id);
160	  const latestWorkerEvent = await db.select().from(executionEvents).where(
161	    inArray(executionEvents.workerId, workerIds),
162	  ).orderBy(desc(executionEvents.createdAt), desc(executionEvents.id)).limit(1).get();
163	
164	  if (!latestWorkerEvent || !SESSION_RECREATED_RECOVERY_EVENT_TYPES.has(latestWorkerEvent.eventType)) {
165	    return false;
166	  }
167	
168	  const latestSessionRecreatedEvent = latestWorkerEvent.eventType === "worker_session_recreated"
169	    ? latestWorkerEvent
170	    : await db.select().from(executionEvents).where(and(
171	      inArray(executionEvents.workerId, workerIds),
172	      eq(executionEvents.eventType, "worker_session_recreated"),
173	    )).orderBy(desc(executionEvents.createdAt), desc(executionEvents.id)).limit(1).get();
174	
175	  if (!latestSessionRecreatedEvent) {
176	    return false;
177	  }
178	
179	  const supervisorAfterRecreate = await db.select().from(executionEvents).where(and(
180	    eq(executionEvents.runId, runId),
181	    like(executionEvents.eventType, "supervisor_%"),
182	    gt(executionEvents.createdAt, latestSessionRecreatedEvent.createdAt),
183	  )).orderBy(desc(executionEvents.createdAt), desc(executionEvents.id)).limit(1).get();
184	  if (supervisorAfterRecreate) {
185	    return false;
186	  }
187	
188	  await clearSupervisorWakeLease(runId);
189	  emitNamedEvent({
190	    kind: "supervisor.wake_lease_recovered",
191	    runId,
192	    reason: "orphaned_worker_session_recreated",
193	  });
194	  await recordExecutionEvent({
195	    runId,
196	    eventType: "supervisor_wake_lease_recovered",
197	    details: {
198	      summary: "Cleared an orphaned supervisor wake lease after a worker session was recreated and left idle.",
199	      latestWorkerEventType: latestWorkerEvent.eventType,
200	      latestWorkerEventId: latestWorkerEvent.id,
201	      latestSessionRecreatedEventId: latestSessionRecreatedEvent.id,
202	    },
203	  });
204	  return true;
205	}
206	
207	async function recoverPreWorkerBlockedByOrphanedLease(runId: string) {
208	  const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
209	  if (!run || run.mode !== "implementation" || run.status !== "running") {
210	    return false;
211	  }
212	
213	  const runWorkers = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.runId, runId));
214	  if (runWorkers.length > 0) {
215	    return false;
216	  }
217	
218	  const pendingClarification = await db.select().from(clarifications).where(and(
219	    eq(clarifications.runId, runId),
220	    eq(clarifications.status, "pending"),
221	  )).limit(1).get();
222	  if (pendingClarification) {
223	    return false;
224	  }
225	
226	  const latestEvent = await db.select().from(executionEvents)
227	    .where(eq(executionEvents.runId, runId))
228	    .orderBy(desc(executionEvents.createdAt), desc(executionEvents.id))
229	    .limit(1)
230	    .get();
231	  if (!latestEvent || !PRE_WORKER_RECOVERY_EVENT_TYPES.has(latestEvent.eventType)) {
232	    if (latestEvent?.eventType === "run_failed") {
233	      let error = "Supervisor wake failed before the first worker was attached.";
234	      try {
235	        const details = JSON.parse(latestEvent.details || "{}") as { error?: unknown; summary?: unknown };
236	        const parsed = typeof details.error === "string"
237	          ? details.error
238	          : typeof details.summary === "string"
239	            ? details.summary
240	            : "";
241	        if (parsed) {
242	          error = parsed;
243	        }
244	      } catch {
245	        // Keep the generic failure text when legacy event details are malformed.
246	      }
247	      await clearSupervisorWakeLease(runId);
248	      await persistRunFailure(runId, new Error(error), {
249	        surface: { code: "supervisor.wake.failed" },
250	      });
251	      emitNamedEvent({
252	        kind: "supervisor.wake_lease_recovered",
253	        runId,
254	        reason: "orphaned_pre_worker",
255	      });
256	      return true;
257	    }
258	    return false;
259	  }
260	  if (Date.now() - latestEvent.createdAt.getTime() < PRE_WORKER_LEASE_ORPHAN_GRACE_MS) {
261	    return false;
262	  }
263	
264	  await clearSupervisorWakeLease(runId);
265	  emitNamedEvent({
266	    kind: "supervisor.wake_lease_recovered",
267	    runId,
268	    reason: "orphaned_pre_worker",
269	  });
270	  await recordExecutionEvent({
271	    runId,
272	    eventType: "supervisor_wake_lease_recovered",
273	    details: {
274	      summary: "Cleared an orphaned supervisor wake lease before the first worker was attached.",
275	      latestEventType: latestEvent.eventType,
276	      latestEventId: latestEvent.id,
277	    },
278	  });
279	  return true;
280	}
281	
282	export async function executeSupervisorWake(runId: string) {
283	  clearWake(runId);
284	  if (inFlight.has(runId)) {
285	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "supervisor.wake_skipped", runId, reason: "in_flight" });
286	    return;
287	  }
288	
289	  const leaseId = await acquireSupervisorWakeLease(runId);
290	  if (!leaseId) {
291	    if (
292	      await recoverCompletionBlockedByOrphanedLease(runId)
293	      || await recoverRecreatedWorkerBlockedByOrphanedLease(runId)
294	      || await recoverPreWorkerBlockedByOrphanedLease(runId)
295	    ) {
296	      scheduleSupervisorWake(runId, 0);
297	      return;
298	    }
299	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "supervisor.wake_skipped", runId, reason: "lease_blocked" });
300	    scheduleSupervisorWake(runId, LEASE_BLOCKED_RETRY_MS);
301	    return;
302	  }
303	
304	  const dueDurableWake = await claimDueDurableSupervisorWake(runId);
305	  if (dueDurableWake) {
306	    emitNamedEvent({
307	      kind: "supervisor.durable_wake_claimed",
308	      runId,
309	      reason: dueDurableWake.reason,
310	      source: dueDurableWake.source,
311	    });
312	  }
313	  const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
314	  if (run?.status === "quota_waiting" && !dueDurableWake) {
315	    if (await hasFutureDurableSupervisorWake(runId)) {
316	      // Bypass the quota-wait short-circuit when a failover is pending,
317	      // so the observer-detected mid-run quota can be picked up on the
318	      // very next supervisor tick instead of waiting for the reset.
319	      const failoverPending = await isRunPendingFailover(runId);
320	      if (!failoverPending) {
321	        emitNamedEvent({ kind: "supervisor.wake_skipped", runId, reason: "quota_wait_future_wake" });
322	        await releaseSupervisorWakeLease(runId, leaseId);
323	        return;
324	      }
325	    }
326	  }
327	
328	  if (!run || !isRunnableImplementationRun(run)) {
329	    if (run && dueDurableWake && await shouldResumeQuotaWorkersWithoutSupervisor(run, dueDurableWake)) {
330	      if (dueDurableWake.reason === "quota_wait") {
331	        await resumeDirectRunAfterQuotaReset({ run, source: "durable_wake" });
332	      } else {
333	        await db.update(runs).set({
334	          status: "running",
335	          failedAt: null,
336	          lastError: null,
337	          updatedAt: new Date(),
338	        }).where(eq(runs.id, runId));
339	      }
340	      await releaseSupervisorWakeLease(runId, leaseId);
341	      return;
342	    }
343	
344	    // Claiming the wake already deleted it, so a quota wake that lands here is
345	    // gone for good — say so instead of returning silently, and leave the open
346	    // incident for `resumeElapsedQuotaWaits` to pick up.
347	    if (dueDurableWake?.reason === "quota_wait") {
348	      emitNamedEvent({
349	        kind: "supervisor.quota_wake_dropped",
350	        runId,
351	        reason: run ? "no_open_incident" : "run_missing",
352	        status: run?.status ?? null,
353	      });
354	      await recordExecutionEvent({
355	        runId,
356	        eventType: "quota_wake_dropped",
357	        details: {
358	          summary: "A due quota wake was claimed but could not be applied to this run.",
359	          runStatus: run?.status ?? null,
360	          runMode: run?.mode ?? null,
361	        },
362	      });
363	    }
364	
365	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "supervisor.wake_skipped", runId, reason: "run_not_runnable" });
366	    stopRunObserver(runId);
367	    await releaseSupervisorWakeLease(runId, leaseId);
368	    return;
369	  }
370	
371	  if (dueDurableWake?.reason === "quota_wait" && run.status !== "quota_waiting") {
372	    // Sweep any stale quota incidents whose reset has elapsed — covers
373	    // failover-success runs whose runs.status never transitioned to
374	    // quota_waiting and which therefore wouldn't be touched by the
375	    // resumeQuotaExhaustedWorkers path below.
376	    await clearResolvedQuotaIncidents(runId);
377	  }
378	  if (run.status === "quota_waiting" && dueDurableWake) {
379	    const wakeRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(runId, async () => {
380	      const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({ runId, now: new Date() });
381	      if (refused) return refused;
382	      await db.update(runs).set({
383	        status: "running",
384	        failedAt: null,
385	        lastError: null,
386	        updatedAt: new Date(),
387	      }).where(eq(runs.id, runId));
388	      return null;
389	    });
390	    if (wakeRefusal) {
391	      await releaseSupervisorWakeLease(runId, leaseId);
392	      return;
393	    }
394	    if (dueDurableWake.reason === "quota_wait") {
395	      const quotaResumeResult = await resumeQuotaExhaustedWorkers({ run });
396	      if (
397	        quotaResumeResult.state === "quota_wait"
398	        || quotaResumeResult.state === "needs_recovery"
399	        || quotaResumeResult.state === "ignored"
400	      ) {
401	        await releaseSupervisorWakeLease(runId, leaseId);
402	        return;
403	      }
404	    }
405	  }
406	
407	  inFlight.add(runId);
408	  try {
409	    const supervisor = new Supervisor({ runId });
410	    const result = await supervisor.run();
411	    const latestRun = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
412	    if (!isActiveImplementationRun(latestRun)) {
413	      clearWake(runId);
414	      void cancelDurableSupervisorWake(runId);
415	      stopRunObserver(runId);
416	    } else if (result.state === "wait") {
417	      scheduleSupervisorWake(runId, result.delayMs);
418	    } else if (result.state === "quota_wait") {
419	      clearWake(runId);
420	      stopRunObserver(runId);
421	    } else if (result.state === "completed" || result.state === "failed" || result.state === "paused") {
422	      clearWake(runId);
423	      if (result.state === "completed" || result.state === "failed") {
424	        void cancelDurableSupervisorWake(runId);
425	      }
426	      if (result.state === "completed" || result.state === "failed") {
427	        stopRunObserver(runId);
428	      }
429	    }
430	  } catch (error) {
431	    if (isTransientSupervisorError(error)) {
432	      await recordExecutionEvent({
433	        runId,
434	        eventType: "supervisor_wake_retry_scheduled",
435	        details: {
436	          summary: "Supervisor wake hit a transient error; retrying shortly.",
437	          delayMs: TRANSIENT_SUPERVISOR_RETRY_MS,
438	          error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
439	        },
440	      });
441	      scheduleSupervisorWake(runId, TRANSIENT_SUPERVISOR_RETRY_MS);
442	      return;
443	    }
444	    stopRunObserver(runId);
445	    await persistRunFailure(runId, error, {
446	      surface: { code: "supervisor.wake.failed" },
447	    });
448	  } finally {
449	    inFlight.delete(runId);
450	    await releaseSupervisorWakeLease(runId, leaseId);
451	  }
452	}
453	
454	export function scheduleSupervisorWake(runId: string, delayMs = 0) {
455	  const nextDeadline = Date.now() + Math.max(0, delayMs);
456	  const currentDeadline = wakeDeadlines.get(runId);
457	  if (currentDeadline !== undefined && currentDeadline <= nextDeadline) {
458	    if (currentDeadline <= Date.now()) {
459	      clearWake(runId);
460	    } else {
461	      scheduleDurableWakeBackup(runId, currentDeadline, Math.max(0, currentDeadline - Date.now()));
462	      return;
463	    }
464	  }
465	
466	  scheduleDurableWakeBackup(runId, nextDeadline, delayMs);
467	
468	  clearWake(runId);
469	  wakeDeadlines.set(runId, nextDeadline);
470	  emitNamedEvent({
471	    kind: "supervisor.wake_scheduled",
472	    runId,
473	    delayMs: Math.max(0, delayMs),
474	    source: delayMs === LEASE_BLOCKED_RETRY_MS ? "lease_retry" : "volatile",
475	  });
476	  wakeTimers.set(runId, setTimeout(() => {
477	    runDetachedSupervisorWakeTask(runId, () => executeSupervisorWake(runId));
478	  }, Math.max(0, delayMs)));
479	}
480	
481	export function cancelSupervisorWake(runId: string) {
482	  clearWake(runId);
483	  inFlight.delete(runId);
484	  void cancelDurableSupervisorWake(runId);
485	}

1	import { and, eq, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
2	import { db } from "@/server/db";
3	import { messages, plans, runs } from "@/server/db/schema";
4	import { startSupervisorRun } from "./start";
5	import { clearSupervisorWakeLease } from "./lease";
6	import { isTransientSupervisorError } from "./retry";
7	import { cancelDurableSupervisorWakesForTerminalRuns, rehydrateDurableSupervisorWakes } from "./wake-schedule";
8	import { compactStaleWorkerOutputs } from "@/server/workers/output-store";
9	import { compactStaleArtifactStreams } from "@/server/artifacts/compaction";
10	import { reapStuckDirectWorkers } from "@/server/workers/stuck-worker-reaper";
11	import { syncConversationSessionsFromBridge } from "@/server/conversations/sync";
12	import { resumeElapsedQuotaWaits } from "@/server/quota/worker-resume";
13	import { emitNamedEvent } from "@/server/events/named-events";
14	
15	const WATCHDOG_INTERVAL_MS = 15_000;
16	
17	let startupPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
18	let watchdogInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
19	
20	function isRecoverableFailedImplementationRun(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect) {
21	  const lastError = run.lastError?.trim() ?? "";
22	  return run.mode === "implementation"
23	    && run.phase !== "planning"
24	    && run.status === "failed"
25	    && Boolean(lastError)
26	    && isTransientSupervisorError(new Error(lastError))
27	    && !/\b(?:api key|authentication required|auth(?:entication)? failed|billing required|api billing|cap_exceeded|insufficient quota|resource exhausted)\b/i.test(lastError);
28	}
29	
30	async function clearMatchingRunFailureMessage(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect) {
31	  if (!run.lastError) {
32	    return;
33	  }
34	
35	  await db.delete(messages).where(and(
36	    eq(messages.runId, run.id),
37	    eq(messages.role, "system"),
38	    eq(messages.kind, "error"),
39	    eq(messages.content, `Run failed: ${run.lastError}`),
40	  ));
41	}
42	
43	async function resumeRecoverableFailedImplementationRun(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect) {
44	  const now = new Date();
45	
46	  await clearSupervisorWakeLease(run.id);
47	  await clearMatchingRunFailureMessage(run);
48	  await db.update(plans).set({
49	    status: "running",
50	    updatedAt: now,
51	  }).where(eq(plans.id, run.planId));
52	  await db.update(runs).set({
53	    status: "running",
54	    failedAt: null,
55	    lastError: null,
56	    updatedAt: now,
57	  }).where(eq(runs.id, run.id));
58	}
59	
60	export async function syncRunningSupervision() {
61	  await cancelDurableSupervisorWakesForTerminalRuns();
62	  await rehydrateDurableSupervisorWakes();
63	  void compactStaleWorkerOutputs().catch((error) => {
64	    console.warn("Worker output compaction sweep failed:", error);
65	  });
66	  void compactStaleArtifactStreams().catch((error) => {
67	    console.warn("Artifact stream compaction sweep failed:", error);
68	  });
69	  // Reconcile bridge state into the DB BEFORE the stuck-worker sweep. A turn
70	  // whose completion was lost (server restart, dropped ask roundtrip, no
71	  // connected client) leaves the worker at status='working' even though the
72	  // bridge agent finished; reconciling first turns those into idle/done so
73	  // the reaper never cancels and re-delivers already-completed work.
74	  void syncConversationSessionsFromBridge().catch((error) => {
75	    process.stderr.write(`[bridge-sync] sweep failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`);
76	  }).then(() => reapStuckDirectWorkers()).then((result) => {
77	    if (result.ok && result.recovered > 0) {
78	      process.stderr.write(`[stuck-reaper] recovered ${result.recovered} stuck direct worker(s)\n`);
79	    } else if (!result.ok) {
80	      process.stderr.write(`[stuck-reaper] sweep failed: ${result.error}\n`);
81	    }
82	  }).catch((error) => {
83	    process.stderr.write(`[stuck-reaper] sweep threw: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`);
84	  });
85	  // Direct conversations have no supervisor loop, so a quota wake that never
86	  // delivered leaves them stranded with an open incident and a cred-exhausted
87	  // worker. The loop below only ever looks at implementation runs; this is the
88	  // only sweep that can recover them.
89	  await resumeElapsedQuotaWaits().catch((error) => {
90	    process.stderr.write(`[quota-sweep] sweep failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`);
91	  });
92	
93	  const activeRuns = await db.select().from(runs).where(and(
94	    inArray(runs.status, ["running", "failed", "quota_waiting"]),
95	    eq(runs.mode, "implementation"),
96	  ));
97	  for (const run of activeRuns) {
98	    // Omni runs still in their interactive planning phase keep the supervisor
99	    // dormant — never auto-wake or auto-resume them here.
100	    if (run.phase === "planning") {
101	      continue;
102	    }
103	
104	    if (run.status === "quota_waiting") {
105	      continue;
106	    }
107	
108	    if (run.status === "failed") {
109	      if (!isRecoverableFailedImplementationRun(run)) {
110	        continue;
111	      }
112	      await resumeRecoverableFailedImplementationRun(run);
113	    }
114	
115	    startSupervisorRun(run.id);
116	  }
117	}
118	
119	export function createSupervisorWatchdogSweepRunner(sweep: () => Promise<void>) {
120	  let inFlight: Promise<void> | null = null;
121	
122	  return () => {
123	    if (inFlight) {
124	      return inFlight;
125	    }
126	
127	    const sweepPromise = Promise.resolve()
128	      .then(sweep)
129	      .catch((error) => {
130	        const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
131	        emitNamedEvent({ kind: "supervisor.watchdog_sweep_failed", reason });
132	        process.stderr.write(`[supervisor-watchdog] sweep failed: ${reason}\n`);
133	      })
134	      .finally(() => {
135	        if (inFlight === sweepPromise) {
136	          inFlight = null;
137	        }
138	      });
139	
140	    inFlight = sweepPromise;
141	    return sweepPromise;
142	  };
143	}
144	
145	const runSupervisorWatchdogSweep = createSupervisorWatchdogSweepRunner(syncRunningSupervision);
146	
147	export async function ensureSupervisorRuntimeStarted() {
148	  if (!startupPromise) {
149	    startupPromise = syncRunningSupervision().then(() => {
150	      if (!watchdogInterval) {
151	        watchdogInterval = setInterval(() => {
152	          void runSupervisorWatchdogSweep();
153	        }, WATCHDOG_INTERVAL_MS);
154	      }
155	    }).catch((error) => {
156	      startupPromise = null;
157	      throw error;
158	    });
159	  }
160	
161	  await startupPromise;
162	}

1	import { randomUUID } from "crypto";
2	import { recordExecutionEvent } from "@/server/events/execution-event-store";
3	import { and, eq, inArray, lte, or, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

8	export type RecoveryIncidentKind = "worker_lost" | "session_missing" | "queue_blocked" | "stale_running" | "quota_exhausted";
9	export type RecoveryIncidentStatus = "open" | "recovering" | "resolved" | "needs_user" | "failed";
10	
11	const OPEN_INCIDENT_STATUSES: RecoveryIncidentStatus[] = ["open", "recovering", "needs_user"];
12	// `failed` belongs here even though it carries a `resolvedAt` stamp: the recovery
13	// banner treats it as active, so a gave-up incident is just as visible as an open
14	// one and needs the same sweep to disappear.
15	const UNSETTLED_INCIDENT_STATUSES: RecoveryIncidentStatus[] = ["open", "recovering", "needs_user", "failed"];
16	
17	function serializeDetails(details: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined) {
18	  return details ? JSON.stringify(details) : null;
19	}
20	
21	function parseDetails(details: string | null | undefined): Record<string, unknown> {
22	  if (!details) {
23	    return {};
24	  }
25	  try {
26	    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(details);
27	    return parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed)
28	      ? parsed as Record<string, unknown>
29	      : {};
30	  } catch {
31	    return {};
32	  }
33	}
34	
35	async function insertRecoveryEvent(
36	  runId: string,
37	  workerId: string | null | undefined,
38	  eventType: string,
39	  details: Record<string, unknown>,
40	) {
41	  await recordExecutionEvent({
42	    runId,
43	    workerId: workerId ?? null,
44	    planItemId: null,
45	    eventType,
46	    details,
47	  });
48	}
49	
50	async function findOpenIncident(args: {
51	  runId: string;
52	  workerId?: string | null;
53	  queuedMessageId?: string | null;
54	  kind: RecoveryIncidentKind;
55	}) {
56	  const records = await db
57	    .select()
58	    .from(recoveryIncidents)
59	    .where(and(
60	      eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, args.runId),
61	      eq(recoveryIncidents.kind, args.kind),
62	      inArray(recoveryIncidents.status, OPEN_INCIDENT_STATUSES),
63	    ));
64	
65	  return records.find((record) => (
66	    (record.workerId ?? null) === (args.workerId ?? null)
67	    && (record.queuedMessageId ?? null) === (args.queuedMessageId ?? null)
68	  )) ?? null;
69	}
70	
71	export async function openRecoveryIncident(args: {
72	  runId: string;
73	  workerId?: string | null;
74	  queuedMessageId?: string | null;
75	  kind: RecoveryIncidentKind;
76	  details?: Record<string, unknown>;
77	  lastError?: string | null;
78	}) {
79	  const existing = await findOpenIncident(args);
80	  const now = new Date();
81	  if (existing) {
82	    const details = { ...parseDetails(existing.details), ...(args.details ?? {}) };
83	    await db.update(recoveryIncidents).set({
84	      details: serializeDetails(details),
85	      lastError: args.lastError ?? existing.lastError,
86	      updatedAt: now,
87	    }).where(eq(recoveryIncidents.id, existing.id));
88	    return { ...existing, details: serializeDetails(details), updatedAt: now };
89	  }
90	
91	  const record = {
92	    id: randomUUID(),
93	    runId: args.runId,
94	    workerId: args.workerId ?? null,
95	    queuedMessageId: args.queuedMessageId ?? null,
96	    kind: args.kind,
97	    status: "open" as const,
98	    autoAttemptCount: 0,
99	    lastError: args.lastError ?? null,
100	    details: serializeDetails(args.details),
101	    detectedAt: now,
102	    updatedAt: now,
103	    resolvedAt: null,
104	  };
105	
106	  await db.insert(recoveryIncidents).values(record);
107	  await insertRecoveryEvent(args.runId, args.workerId, "recovery_incident_opened", {
108	    summary: `Opened ${args.kind} recovery incident.`,
109	    incidentId: record.id,
110	    kind: args.kind,
111	    queuedMessageId: args.queuedMessageId ?? null,
112	    ...(args.details ?? {}),
113	  });
114	  emitNamedEvent({
115	    kind: "recovery.opened",
116	    runId: args.runId,
117	    incidentId: record.id,
118	    recoveryKind: args.kind,
119	  });
120	  return record;
121	}
122	
123	export async function markRecoveryIncidentRecovering(args: {
124	  incidentId: string;
125	  runId: string;
126	  workerId?: string | null;
127	  decision: string;
128	  details?: Record<string, unknown>;
129	}) {
130	  const now = new Date();
131	  const incident = await db.select().from(recoveryIncidents).where(eq(recoveryIncidents.id, args.incidentId)).get();
132	  const nextAttempts = (incident?.autoAttemptCount ?? 0) + 1;
133	  await db.update(recoveryIncidents).set({
134	    status: "recovering",
135	    autoAttemptCount: nextAttempts,
136	    details: serializeDetails({
137	      ...parseDetails(incident?.details),
138	      decision: args.decision,
139	      ...(args.details ?? {}),
140	    }),
141	    updatedAt: now,
142	  }).where(eq(recoveryIncidents.id, args.incidentId));
143	  await insertRecoveryEvent(args.runId, args.workerId, "recovery_policy_decision", {
144	    summary: `Recovery policy chose ${args.decision}.`,
145	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
146	    decision: args.decision,
147	    autoAttemptCount: nextAttempts,
148	    ...(args.details ?? {}),
149	  });
150	  emitNamedEvent({
151	    kind: "recovery.attempt",
152	    runId: args.runId,
153	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
154	    attempt: nextAttempts,
155	  });
156	}
157	
158	/**
159	 * Atomically claim a recovery incident before starting an external resume.
160	 * Multiple wake sources can observe the same open incident; only one may
161	 * reattach the provider session. A stale recovering claim can be reclaimed
162	 * after a runner crash, but a live claim is left alone.
163	 */
164	export async function claimRecoveryIncident(args: {
165	  incidentId: string;
166	  runId: string;
167	  workerId?: string | null;
168	  decision: string;
169	  details?: Record<string, unknown>;
170	  staleAfterMs?: number;
171	}) {
172	  const now = new Date();
173	  const staleAfterMs = Math.max(0, args.staleAfterMs ?? 60_000);
174	  const staleBefore = new Date(now.getTime() - staleAfterMs);
175	  const claimed = await db.update(recoveryIncidents).set({
176	    status: "recovering",
177	    autoAttemptCount: sql`${recoveryIncidents.autoAttemptCount} + 1`,
178	    details: serializeDetails(args.details),
179	    updatedAt: now,
180	  }).where(and(
181	    eq(recoveryIncidents.id, args.incidentId),
182	    or(
183	      eq(recoveryIncidents.status, "open"),
184	      and(
185	        eq(recoveryIncidents.status, "recovering"),
186	        lte(recoveryIncidents.updatedAt, staleBefore),
187	      ),
188	    ),
189	  )).returning().get();
190	
191	  if (!claimed) {
192	    return null;
193	  }
194	
195	  await insertRecoveryEvent(args.runId, args.workerId, "recovery_policy_decision", {
196	    summary: `Recovery policy chose ${args.decision}.`,
197	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
198	    decision: args.decision,
199	    autoAttemptCount: claimed.autoAttemptCount,
200	    ...(args.details ?? {}),
201	  });
202	  emitNamedEvent({
203	    kind: "recovery.attempt",
204	    runId: args.runId,
205	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
206	    attempt: claimed.autoAttemptCount,
207	  });
208	  return claimed;
209	}
210	
211	export async function markRecoveryIncidentResolved(args: {
212	  incidentId: string;
213	  runId: string;
214	  workerId?: string | null;
215	  summary: string;
216	  details?: Record<string, unknown>;
217	}) {
218	  const now = new Date();
219	  await db.update(recoveryIncidents).set({
220	    status: "resolved",
221	    lastError: null,
222	    details: serializeDetails(args.details),
223	    updatedAt: now,
224	    resolvedAt: now,
225	  }).where(eq(recoveryIncidents.id, args.incidentId));
226	  await insertRecoveryEvent(args.runId, args.workerId, "recovery_resolved", {
227	    summary: args.summary,
228	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
229	    ...(args.details ?? {}),
230	  });
231	  emitNamedEvent({
232	    kind: "recovery.resolved",
233	    runId: args.runId,
234	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
235	  });
236	}
237	
238	export async function markRecoveryIncidentNeedsUser(args: {
239	  incidentId: string;
240	  runId: string;
241	  workerId?: string | null;
242	  reason: string;
243	  details?: Record<string, unknown>;
244	}) {
245	  const now = new Date();
246	  const existing = await db.select().from(recoveryIncidents).where(eq(recoveryIncidents.id, args.incidentId)).get();
247	  const alreadyNeedsUser = existing?.status === "needs_user";
248	  await db.update(recoveryIncidents).set({
249	    status: "needs_user",
250	    lastError: args.reason,
251	    details: serializeDetails(args.details),
252	    updatedAt: now,
253	    // A resumed session can still fail later — during the continuation turn that
254	    // follows recovery, for instance. Clear the resolution stamp so a reopened
255	    // incident does not read as both resolved and blocked on the user.
256	    resolvedAt: null,
257	  }).where(eq(recoveryIncidents.id, args.incidentId));
258	  if (alreadyNeedsUser && existing?.lastError === args.reason) {
259	    return;
260	  }
261	  await insertRecoveryEvent(args.runId, args.workerId, "recovery_needs_user", {
262	    summary: args.reason,
263	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
264	    ...(args.details ?? {}),
265	  });
266	  emitNamedEvent({
267	    kind: "error.surfaced",
268	    code: "recovery.needs_user",
269	    message: args.reason,
270	    surface: "banner",
271	    runId: args.runId,
272	    ...(args.workerId ? { workerId: args.workerId } : {}),
273	  });
274	}
275

43	
44	function isCorruptResumeFileError(value: string | null | undefined) {
45	  return /failed to load resumed session data from file/i.test(value ?? "");
46	}
47	
48	const INTERRUPTED_DIRECT_TURN_PROMPT = buildDirectWorkerPrompt([
49	  "The OmniHarness runner restarted while you were working.",
50	  "Resume the interrupted task now and continue any implementation, edits, tests, or other workspace changes already authorized by the user's latest request.",
51	  "Do not stop merely to report the restart, repeat work that is already complete, or wait for another user message.",
52	].join(" "));
53	
54	function normalizedAgentState(value: string | null | undefined) {
55	  return (value ?? "").trim().toLowerCase().split(":")[0]?.trim() ?? "";
56	}
57	
58	function recoveredDirectTurnNeedsPrompt(
59	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect,
60	  resumed: AgentRecord,
61	) {
62	  if (run.mode !== "direct" && run.mode !== "commit") {
63	    return false;
64	  }
65	  if (normalizeRunStatus(run.status) === "awaiting_user") {
66	    return false;
67	  }
68	  if ((resumed.stopReason ?? "").trim()) {
69	    return false;
70	  }
71	  if (resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput({
72	    workerStatus: resumed.state,
73	    renderedOutput: resumed.renderedOutput,
74	    currentText: resumed.currentText,
75	    lastText: resumed.lastText,
76	    outputEntries: resumed.outputEntries,
77	    pendingPermissions: resumed.pendingPermissions,
78	    pendingElicitations: resumed.pendingElicitations,
79	  }) === "awaiting_user") {
80	    return false;
81	  }
82	  return !["working", "running", "busy", "starting", "pending", "recovering"]
83	    .includes(normalizedAgentState(resumed.state));
84	}
85	
86	async function finishRecoveredDirectTurn(args: {
87	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect;
88	  worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect;
89	  resumed: AgentRecord;
90	  incidentId: string;
91	}) {
92	  emitNamedEvent({
93	    kind: "worker.recovery_continuation_started",
94	    runId: args.run.id,
95	    workerId: args.worker.id,
96	  });
97	  await insertRecoveryExecutionEvent(args.run.id, args.worker.id, "recovery_continuation_started", {
98	    summary: `Continuing the interrupted turn for ${args.worker.id}.`,
99	    incidentId: args.incidentId,
100	  });
101	
102	  try {
103	    const response = await askAgent(args.worker.id, INTERRUPTED_DIRECT_TURN_PROMPT);
104	    const snapshot = await getAgent(args.worker.id).catch(() => null);
105	    if (snapshot) {
106	      await persistWorkerSnapshot(args.worker.id, snapshot);
107	    }
108	    await appendAskResponseFallbackEntry({
109	      runId: args.run.id,
110	      workerId: args.worker.id,
111	      responseText: response.response,
112	      snapshot,
113	    });
114	
115	    const finalWorkerStatus = snapshot?.state ?? response.state ?? "idle";
116	    await db.update(workers).set({
117	      status: finalWorkerStatus,
118	      updatedAt: new Date(),
119	    }).where(eq(workers.id, args.worker.id));
120	    await updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput({
121	      runId: args.run.id,
122	      workerId: args.worker.id,
123	      workerStatus: finalWorkerStatus,
124	      responseText: response.response,
125	      renderedOutput: snapshot?.renderedOutput,
126	      currentText: snapshot?.currentText,
127	      lastText: snapshot?.lastText,
128	      outputEntries: snapshot?.outputEntries,
129	      pendingPermissions: snapshot?.pendingPermissions,
130	      pendingElicitations: snapshot?.pendingElicitations,
131	    });
132	    emitNamedEvent({
133	      kind: "worker.recovery_continuation_completed",
134	      runId: args.run.id,
135	      workerId: args.worker.id,
136	    });
137	    await insertRecoveryExecutionEvent(args.run.id, args.worker.id, "recovery_continuation_completed", {
138	      summary: `Completed the recovered turn for ${args.worker.id}.`,
139	      incidentId: args.incidentId,

170	export async function updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(args: WorkerOutputSource & {
171	  runId: string;
172	  workerId?: string | null;
173	}) {
174	  const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, args.runId)).get();
175	  if (!run || (run.mode !== "direct" && run.mode !== "commit")) {
176	    return null;
177	  }
178	
179	  const quotaIncident = await db.select({ id: recoveryIncidents.id })
180	    .from(recoveryIncidents)
181	    .where(and(
182	      eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, args.runId),
183	      eq(recoveryIncidents.kind, "quota_exhausted"),
184	      inArray(recoveryIncidents.status, [...ACTIVE_QUOTA_INCIDENT_STATUSES]),
185	    ))
186	    .limit(1)
187	    .get();
188	  const quotaWorker = args.workerId
189	    ? await db.select({ status: workers.status }).from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, args.workerId)).get()
190	    : null;
191	  const quotaRecoveryOwnsPersistedState = Boolean(
192	    quotaIncident
193	    && (
194	      run.status === "quota_waiting"
195	      || normalizeWorkerStatus(quotaWorker?.status) === "cred-exhausted"
196	    ),
197	  );
198	  if (quotaIncident && quotaRecoveryOwnsPersistedState) {
199	    const normalizedRunStatus = run.status.trim().toLowerCase();
200	    if (normalizedRunStatus === "cancelled" || normalizedRunStatus === "canceled" || normalizedRunStatus === "done") {
201	      return run.status;
202	    }
203	
204	    if (run.status !== "quota_waiting" || run.failedAt || run.lastError) {
205	      const updated = await db.update(runs).set({
206	        status: "quota_waiting",
207	        failedAt: null,
208	        lastError: null,
209	        updatedAt: new Date(),
210	      }).where(and(
211	        eq(runs.id, args.runId),
212	        eq(runs.status, run.status),
213	      )).returning({ id: runs.id });
214	      if (updated.length > 0) {
215	        emitNamedEvent({
216	          kind: "recovery.quota_wait_preserved",
217	          runId: args.runId,
218	          incidentId: quotaIncident.id,
219	          previousStatus: run.status,
220	        });
221	        await recordExecutionEvent({
222	          runId: args.runId,
223	          workerId: args.workerId ?? null,
224	          planItemId: null,
225	          eventType: "quota_wait_preserved",
226	          details: {
227	            summary: "Kept quota recovery authoritative over a stale direct-run status.",
228	            incidentId: quotaIncident.id,
229	            previousStatus: run.status,
230	          },
231	        });
232	        notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
233	      }
234	    }
235	    return "quota_waiting";
236	  }
237	
238	  const nextStatus = resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(args);
239	  const now = new Date();
240	  if (run.status !== nextStatus || run.failedAt || run.lastError) {
241	    await db.update(runs).set({
242	      status: nextStatus,
243	      failedAt: null,
244	      lastError: null,
245	      updatedAt: now,
246	    }).where(eq(runs.id, args.runId));
247	  }
248	
249	  if (nextStatus === "awaiting_user" && run.status !== "awaiting_user") {
250	    emitNamedEvent({
251	      kind: "conversation.awaiting_user",
252	      runId: args.runId,
253	      workerId: args.workerId ?? undefined,
254	      reason: "worker_requested_input",
255	    });
256	    await recordExecutionEvent({
257	      runId: args.runId,
258	      workerId: args.workerId ?? null,
259	      planItemId: null,
260	      eventType: "direct_worker_awaiting_user",
261	      details: { reason: "worker_requested_input" },
262	      createdAt: now,
263	    });
264	  } else if (run.mode === "direct" && nextStatus === "done" && run.status !== "done") {
265	    await runMilestoneAutoCommit(args.runId, firstNonEmptyText([
266	      args.responseText,
267	      args.currentText,
268	      args.lastText,
269	      latestVisibleEntryText(args.outputEntries),
270	      latestVisibleEntryText(parseOutputEntriesJson(args.outputEntriesJson)),
271	      args.renderedOutput,
272	      args.outputLog,
273	    ]));
274	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
275	  } else if (nextStatus !== run.status || run.failedAt || run.lastError) {
276	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
277	  }
278	
279	  return nextStatus;
280	}

282	export async function updateDirectRunAwaitingUserInputIfRequested(args: WorkerOutputSource & {
283	  runId: string;

285	}) {

287	    return false;
288	  }
289	
290	  await updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(args);
291	  return true;
292	}

Perfect! Now I understand the problem. The `quota_wait_preserved` event is emitted whenever `updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput` is called and the condition on line 204 evaluates to true. The key is the CAS (compare-and-swap) at line 210-212 that only emits the event IF the update was actually applied (line 214: `if (updated.length > 0)`).

However, `updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput` is being called from multiple places. Let me create a comprehensive report now.

Based on my thorough analysis of the codebase, here's a precise technical writeup on the quota-exhaustion recovery code path:

## Quota-Exhaustion Recovery Code Path Analysis

### 1. Named Event Emission Locations

**Event Emissions:**

- **`quota_wait_scheduled`**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/quota/recovery.ts:488`
  - Emitted when run transitions to `quota_waiting` and supervisor wake is scheduled
  - Direct call to `insertQuotaEvent()` at line 488

- **`recovery_policy_decision`**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/runs/recovery-incidents.ts:143` and `:195`
  - Line 143: Emitted by `markRecoveryIncidentRecovering()` when recovery policy decision made
  - Line 195: Emitted by `claimRecoveryIncident()` when incident is claimed for recovery attempt

- **`worker_session_resumed`**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/quota/worker-resume.ts:115`
  - Emitted by `insertWorkerSessionResumedEvent()` when session is successfully reattached
  - Direct `recordExecutionEvent()` call; also emits named event via `emitNamedEvent()` at line 110

- **`recovery_resolved`**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/runs/recovery-incidents.ts:226`
  - Emitted by `markRecoveryIncidentResolved()` in recovery-incidents.ts
  - Called from multiple points: quota-resume (worker-resume.ts:332), failover, cleanup paths

- **`quota_recovery_refused`**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/quota/recovery.ts:126`
  - Emitted by `refuseLateQuotaRecovery()` when terminal run blocks late recovery callback
  - Direct `insertQuotaEvent()` call with reason in details

- **`quota_wait_preserved`**: 
  - `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/direct-run-status.ts:225`
  - `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/sync.ts:533`
  - Both emit when run status is forcibly kept at `quota_waiting` despite stale status rewrites

### 2. Scheduled Wake and Resume Decision Handler

**The `resumeQuotaExhaustedWorkers()` function**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/quota/worker-resume.ts:225-519`

This is the primary handler triggered by `executeSupervisorWake()` in wake.ts when a durable wake with reason `quota_wait` is claimed.

**After resuming the worker session (lines 276-350):**

1. **Line 279-289**: Spawn/reattach the agent via `spawnAgent()` with `resumeSessionId`
2. **Line 305-314**: Update worker status to resumed state, update `bridgeSessionId`/`bridgeSessionMode`
3. **Line 319-342**: Call `insertWorkerSessionResumedEvent()` (emits `worker_session_resumed` event)
4. **Line 332-342**: Immediately call `markRecoveryIncidentResolved()` **BEFORE prompting**
5. **Line 352-365**: If worker not actively busy, call `promptResumedQuotaWorker()` which:
   - Lines 132-137: Calls `askAgent()` to send recovery prompt
   - Lines 157-162: Appends supervisor input via `appendSupervisorInputOnDelivery()`
   - Lines 176-181: Persists snapshot and fallback output entry
   - Lines 192-196: Updates worker status to response.state

**Critical ordering:** The incident is resolved (line 332) BEFORE the resumed turn actually begins producing output. The resolution is proof that quota window reopened; the prompt and turn execution happen after, and if they fail, `handleWorkerQuotaExhaustion()` opens a fresh incident.

### 3. Terminal Run Check and Status Conditions

**Guard for late quota recovery refusal**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/quota/recovery.ts:70-76`

```typescript
const reason = !run
  ? "run_missing" as const
  : isTerminalRecoveryRunStatus(run.status) && !staleFailedRunOwnedByQuota
    ? "run_terminal" as const
    : isCancelledWorkerStatus(worker?.status)
      ? "worker_cancelled" as const
      : null;
```

**Terminal run statuses** (from `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/lib/run-status.ts:1`):
```typescript
const TERMINAL_RUN_STATUSES = new Set(["done", "failed", "cancelled", "promoting", "promoted"]);
```

**Additional check** at line 43-44 in recovery.ts:
```typescript
function isTerminalRecoveryRunStatus(status: string | null | undefined) {
  return isTerminalRunStatus(status) || normalizeRunStatus(status) === "canceled";
}
```

The condition at line 72-73 checks if run reached terminal state AND is not a stale failed run owned by quota (which can be recovered). The message "Ignored a late quota recovery callback after the conversation became terminal" is logged at line 127.

### 4. Critical Ordering: Worker Idle Transition and Run Completion Check

**The completion-reset event logic**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/supervisor/wake.ts:35-36`

```typescript
const COMPLETION_EVENT_TYPES = new Set(["worker_turn_completed"]);
const COMPLETION_RESET_EVENT_TYPES = new Set(["worker_prompted", "worker_spawned", "worker_session_resumed", "worker_session_recreated"]);
```

**Critical flow in quota-resume:**

1. **Session reattached** (worker-resume.ts:305-314): Worker status updated to `resumedWorker.state` (which is typically "idle" or "starting" at resume time)

2. **Incident resolved** (worker-resume.ts:332-342): Incident marked resolved BEFORE any prompting

3. **Prompt sent** (worker-resume.ts:352-365): If `shouldPromptResumedWorker()` returns true (line 352 checks if state is not "working"/"running"/"busy"/"starting"/"pending"), the prompt is sent

4. **After prompt** (promptResumedQuotaWorker, lines 192-196): Worker status is updated to `response.state` from the ask response

**The ordering problem identified:** At lines 305-314 in worker-resume.ts, when the session is reattached, the worker transitions to whatever state the resumed session reports. **The completion check in wake.ts at line 118** (`COMPLETION_RESET_EVENT_TYPES.has(latestWorkerEvent.eventType)`) only resets the completion flag if the latest worker event is one of those types. `worker_session_resumed` IS in `COMPLETION_RESET_EVENT_TYPES`, so the completion flag is reset. However, this check happens **before** the continuation prompt is sent.

If a run completion happens between session-resume and prompt-send, or if the worker becomes idle in between, the supervisor could mark the run done. The safeguard is that `promptResumedQuotaWorker()` is only called if `shouldPromptResumedWorker()` returns true (checking if state is not actively busy).

### 5. `quota_wait_preserved` High Frequency Emission (3464 times in ~2.3 hours)

**Root cause**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/direct-run-status.ts:204-213`

The event is emitted only when **both conditions are true**:
1. A quota incident exists and owns the persistent state (quotaRecoveryOwnsPersistedState = true)
2. **AND** the run status is NOT already `quota_waiting`, OR has `failedAt`/`lastError` set

The CAS (compare-and-swap) at line 210-212 only succeeds and emits if the update actually applies.

**`updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput()` is called from:**

- **Every 15 seconds via watchdog sweep**: `syncConversationSessionsFromBridge()` (runtime-watchdog.ts:74) → `syncConversationSessions()` (sync.ts:469) → iterates all direct runs and calls at sync.ts:748 and 839

- **Per SSE client connection**: When live updates are received and run state needs reconciliation

- **Per outbound message delivery**: send-message.ts:1062

- **Per queued message drain**: queued-messages.ts:422

- **Per worker output update**: workers/stuck-worker-reaper.ts:129

**Emission frequency of ~1/sec over 2.3 hours suggests**: The watchdog sweep (15s interval) is likely hitting the condition once per cycle when the run status gets flipped from `running`/`done` back to `quota_waiting` by sync reconciliation, triggering a CAS update. This would be approximately 2.3 hours / 15s ≈ 552 sweep cycles. With multiple runs or multiple status transitions per sweep (e.g., stale `done` → `quota_waiting`), 3464 emissions is plausible.

The high frequency occurs because:
1. Direct-run status resolver (`resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput()`) transitions idle quota-blocked workers to `quota_waiting` (line 159 in direct-run-status.ts)
2. Other status setters (send-message, conversation sync on output changes) may flip it to `running` or `done` without quota awareness
3. The watchdog sweep runs every 15s and re-reconciles all runs, detecting the stale status and resetting it back to `quota_waiting`
4. Each reconciliation that changes status triggers the event **per subscription/caller**, not deduplicated at emission level

The safeguard is that `promptResumedQuotaWorker()` is only called if `shouldPromptResumedWorker()` returns true (checking if state is not actively busy).\n\n### 5. `quota_wait_preserved` High Frequency Emission (3464 times in ~2.3 hours)\n\n**Root cause**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/direct-run-status.ts:204-213`\n\nThe event is emitted only when **both conditions are true**:\n1. A quota incident exists and owns the persistent state (quotaRecoveryOwnsPersistedState = true)\n2. **AND** the run status is NOT already `quota_waiting`, OR has `failedAt`/`lastError` set\n\nThe CAS (compare-and-swap) at line 210-212 only succeeds and emits if the update actually applies.\n\n**`updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput()` is called from:**\n\n- **Every 15 seconds via watchdog sweep**: `syncConversationSessionsFromBridge()` (runtime-watchdog.ts:74) → `syncConversationSessions()` (sync.ts:469) → iterates all direct runs and calls at sync.ts:748 and 839\n\n- **Per SSE client connection**: When live updates are received and run state needs reconciliation\n\n- **Per outbound message delivery**: send-message.ts:1062\n\n- **Per queued message drain**: queued-messages.ts:422\n\n- **Per worker output update**: workers/stuck-worker-reaper.ts:129\n\n**Emission frequency of ~1/sec over 2.3 hours suggests**: The watchdog sweep (15s interval) is likely hitting the condition once per cycle when the run status gets flipped from `running`/`done` back to `quota_waiting` by sync reconciliation, triggering a CAS update. This would be approximately 2.3 hours / 15s ≈ 552 sweep cycles. With multiple runs or multiple status transitions per sweep (e.g., stale `done` → `quota_waiting`), 3464 emissions is plausible.\n\nThe high frequency occurs because:\n1. Direct-run status resolver (`resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput()`) transitions idle quota-blocked workers to `quota_waiting` (line 159 in direct-run-status.ts)\n2. Other status setters (send-message, conversation sync on output changes) may flip it to `running` or `done` without quota awareness\n3. The watchdog sweep runs every 15s and re-reconciles all runs, detecting the stale status and resetting it back to `quota_waiting`\n4. Each reconciliation that changes status triggers the event **per subscription/caller**, not deduplicated at emission level"

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-16T17:17:15.504Z

Actually, the resume did work — the session picked back up at 18:00:03, the worker got the right prompt, and it spent 15 minutes doing real work before finishing cleanly at 18:15:45. So the continuation succeeded end-to-end; the real issue must be something that happened afterward in the control plane around 18:15.

At 18:15:45, right as the turn completed, a `quota_recovery_refused` event fired, ignoring a late callback because the conversation had already become terminal — run.status flipped to done and worker.status to idle. But worker.updated_at still shows 18:00:03, meaning nothing in the worker's stored output was updated during those 15 minutes of actual work, so the resumed turn's content likely never got surfaced to the user even though it completed successfully underneath.

I'm realizing the control plane resumed the agent process and prompted it after the quota wait, but never re-attached the output stream — so the agent's real work only landed in the runtime-output jsonl file, not in the DB's workers.current_text, messages, or execution_events, while the run silently flipped to done. I want to verify the exact timing of that status change and check whether the user manually marked it done, then dig into the output_entries_json timestamps to see if post-resume content is captured anywhere in the DB.

I also notice there's no run-data directory for this worker's run at all, which would confirm the worker entries were never persisted to the unified stream — I should check artifact_streams and the output_entries_json directly to pin down exactly where and when the 'done' status got set.

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-16T17:18:06.021Z

This confirms it clearly: the unified worker stream ends at 16:00:03Z with just the resume handshake data, while the raw runtime log shows 15 minutes of actual work continuing from 16:00:11Z to 16:15 that never made it into the unified stream.

So the session genuinely resumed and the agent did real work, but the control plane's entry appender stopped consuming after the handshake — everything past that point only exists in the raw log. This explains why workers.updated_at froze, the run got marked done prematurely, and the real completion callback at 18:15:45 was rejected as "late" since the conversation was already terminal. I want to pin down why the appender stopped — likely the subscription to the session's update stream got dropped after the resume spawned with resumeSessionId.

Looking closer, the resume prompt went through `promptResumedQuotaWorker` → `askAgent`, which blocked for the full 15 minutes while the worker row stayed marked "idle" the whole time — status only updates to the response state after askAgent resolves. So the sequence is: resume handshake appends entries and marks the incident resolved, then askAgent blocks silently, the worker looks idle/done to the status resolver, and by the time the real completion arrives it's already been treated as terminal and discarded.

I want to confirm whether worker status was ever briefly set to "working" before the ask, or if it stayed idle the entire time — I should check worker-resume.ts around lines 280-370 to be sure before reporting this diagnosis.

I need to check the resume block's status assignment and the resolver's done condition in direct-run-status.ts to confirm.

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-16T17:18:37.039Z

I confirm there are zero worker_prompted and worker_turn_completed events for this run, and the resolver logic returns "done" for an idle worker whose output isn't a blocking question. The worker was set to idle at 18:00:03 right after reattachment, before the ask was issued, which matters because the prompting logic requires a non-working state to trigger.

Then askAgent blocked for 15 minutes with the worker still idle and worker_prompted never recorded, so the 15-second reconciliation sweep resolved the run to "done." At 18:15:45, refuseLateQuotaRecovery saw the run was terminal, refused, and returned "ignored," discarding the entire turn's response.

That's the full causal chain — and I notice the comment explaining a prior fix that moved markRecoveryIncidentResolved before the prompt to stop the banner from sticking. That fix inadvertently removed the incident-open state that had been preventing reconciliation to done, since the quota_wait_preserved mechanism only holds while an incident is open, and it stopped firing at exactly 18:00:01. That's the regression: once the incident resolves, nothing protects the run status while the worker sits idle through the whole ask. I want to verify this by checking that quota_wait_preserved's last timestamp is 18:00:01 against when the incident was actually resolved.

I've confirmed the preservation mechanism stopped the instant the incident resolved, creating a 15-minute window where the idle worker got reconciled to done. Since the user asked a diagnostic question rather than requesting a fix, I should just report the analysis without making changes, and offer — rather than unilaterally decide — to save this as a learning given there's a relevant skill and learnings directory for documenting diagnosed bugs.

## Claude · 2026-08-16T17:18:49.137Z

Diagnosed. The resume actually worked — the agent did 15 minutes of real work. The control plane threw the result away.

## Timeline (run `e2168e17ff4b`, project `cloudcaptions`)

| Local time | What happened |
|---|---|
| 15:09:37 | Quota block. `"You've hit your session limit · resets 6pm (Europe/Madrid)"` → incident `221067d4`, wake scheduled for 18:00:01. Parsing was correct. |
| 15:41–18:00:01 | `quota_wait_preserved` × **3464** (~1/sec) — reconciliation repeatedly holding the run at `quota_waiting`. |
| 18:00:01 | Wake fires. `recovery_policy_decision` → `resume_quota_worker`. Correct. |
| 18:00:03 | Session reattached. Worker row set to `idle`. Incident marked **resolved**. |
| 18:00:11 → 18:15:45 | Agent genuinely works: thoughts, sloptrim run, Codex review pass 3, tool calls, final answer ending *"Say the word and I'll start with Task 1."* |
| ~18:15:45 | `quota_recovery_refused` — *"Ignored a late quota recovery callback after the conversation became terminal"*, `runStatus: done`. Response discarded. |

## Root cause

`promptResumedQuotaWorker` (`src/server/quota/worker-resume.ts:132`) awaits the **entire** agent turn before recording anything:

```ts
const response = await runWorkerTurn(args.worker.id, () => askAgent(...));  // blocks 15 min
```

Everything that would mark the worker busy happens *after* that await — `worker_prompted` (line 203), the status write to `response.state` (line 192), the supervisor input append (line 157). The run has **zero** `worker_prompted` and zero `worker_turn_completed` events.

So for 15 minutes the worker row sat at `status = idle`, `updated_at = 18:00:03`, with no in-flight marker. The direct-run-status resolver (`src/server/conversations/direct-run-status.ts:167`) returns `"done"` for an idle worker not asking a blocking question, and the 15s sweep flipped the run terminal. When `askAgent` finally resolved, `refuseLateQuotaRecovery` (line 139) saw `done`, returned `"ignored"` at line 166, and dropped `response.response` on the floor.

The trigger is the deliberate reordering documented at `worker-resume.ts:325-331` — resolving the incident *before* prompting, to stop the "Waiting for quota reset" banner sticking. That fix removed the only thing pinning the status: `quota_wait_preserved` stops at exactly 18:00:01, two seconds before the incident is resolved. It swapped a stuck banner for an unguarded 15-minute window.

## Corroborating evidence

- Unified worker stream `cloudcaptions/.omniharness/run-data/e2168e17ff4b/workers/e2168e17ff4b-worker-1.jsonl` ends at `16:00:03Z` with just the `current_mode`/`config_option` resume handshake.
- The same 15 minutes of output *is* present in the raw runtime log `.omniharness/agent-runtime-output/e2168e17ff4b-worker-1.jsonl` (1704 lines vs 1115) — proof the bridge was fine and only persistence was severed.

## Secondary bug

3464 `quota_wait_preserved` events for one incident. A 15s watchdog can't produce ~1/sec; something is fanning it out per caller or per subscriber, and the CAS keeps winning because another writer flips the status back each time. Worth a separate look.

Two things I did *not* do, since you asked what went wrong rather than to change anything: I haven't touched the code, and I haven't written this up under `docs/superpowers/learnings/`. Say the word for either — the fix is most likely marking the worker `working` and emitting `worker_prompted` before the `askAgent` await, rather than after.

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-16T19:31:35.176Z

I've got three things to handle: fixing the worker-resume.ts bug, writing a learning doc, and recovering the lost session work. For recovery, I'm thinking through what actually happened — the agent produced real files in cloudcaptions during its 15 minutes of work, but the conversation output itself got lost from the control plane, so I need to figure out how to reconstruct that from the raw runtime log.

Recovery likely means either backfilling the worker stream JSONL with the missing entries, or resetting the run status from "done" to something the user can continue from — and since the user explicitly authorized recovery, I'll proceed carefully with any DB mutations.

For the fix itself, I want to read through worker-resume.ts fully to understand runWorkerTurn and askAgent, then compare against the normal send-message path to see how it marks the worker as working before calling askAgent, so I can mirror that pattern correctly. I should also check the learning-from-bugs skill conventions and any existing lifecycle tests for worker-resume or quota handling before making changes.

My plan is to read the code thoroughly, write the fix, add a lifecycle/unit test, document the learning, then handle recovery by backfilling the worker stream and resetting the run status. I'll work directly on master rather than creating a worktree or branch, since AGENTS.md prohibits that unless explicitly requested.

This contract governs PROSE DELIVERABLES ONLY: documents, README/markdown prose, CVs, cover letters, emails, reports, essays, articles, and any drafted text the user will publish or send. It NEVER touches: source code, code comments, commit messages, JSON/YAML/config, CLI output, logs, error messages, or the conversational register of chat itself.\nComposes with other active modes; it does not override them. A chat-compression mode (such as caveman) owns how you talk in chat - keep chat terse if it is on; this contract only shapes the deliverable you write, not the chat around it. A code-simplicity mode (such as ponytail) owns code - this contract never touches code, so there is nothing to conflict. Each mode keeps its own domain: terse chat, lazy code, human prose. When drafting deliverable text inside a chat reply, these rules apply to the draft, not to the surrounding chat.\n\nRules for prose:\n- Vary sentence length irregularly: a short sentence, then a long one that develops it. Never metronomic, never mechanical short-long alternation.\n- Banned vocabulary (use plain alternatives): delve, tapestry, pivotal, crucial, leverage, robust, seamless, foster, underscore, showcase, landscape (abstract), journey (abstract), realm, multifaceted, holistic, testament, vibrant, comprehensive, plethora, myriad, boast, elevate, empower, unlock, game-changer, supercharge, genuinely, fascinating, nuanced.\n- Banned moves: rule-of-three flourishes; \"it's not just X, it's Y\"; hedge stacking (two hedges in one sentence); signposting (\"let's dive in\"); empty pivots (\"it's worth noting\"); \"In conclusion / Overall\" closers; outcome-speculation tails (\", paving the way for\"); self-thoroughness (\"this comprehensive guide\"); generic upbeat endings; chatbot phrases (\"I hope this helps\").\n- Em-dash: at most one per paragraph. No bold-for-emphasis inside prose sentences. No emojis in prose. Semicolons and parentheses where a writer would naturally use them.\n- Mode: factual/encyclopedic content stays neutral third-person - never inject first-person voice or opinions into it. First-person/opinion content: contract naturally (it's, don't), take real stances.\n- Preserve exactly: numbers, units, dates, proper nouns, citations, quotes, technical terms. Never invent facts, sources, or statistics.\n- Concrete subjects, active verbs. End sections on a fact or observation, not a sentiment.\n- SILENT. Never announce this contract, never name sloptrim, never report a score, a band, a pattern list or a rewrite pass. Do not offer the user a style choice. 63	
64	function isDirectRunMode(mode: string | null | undefined) {
65	  return mode === "direct" || mode === "commit";
66	}
67	
68	// An Omni run is stored as mode "implementation" for its whole life; its
69	// `phase` distinguishes the interactive planning stage (planner worker, no
70	// supervisor) from the supervised implementation stage. Route follow-up
71	// messages by phase, not raw mode, so a reply during planning reaches the
72	// planner instead of being treated as a supervisor steer.
73	function isPlanningRun(run: { mode?: string | null; phase?: string | null }) {
74	  return run.mode === "planning" || (run.mode === "implementation" && run.phase === "planning");
75	}
76	
77	function isSupervisedRun(run: { mode?: string | null; phase?: string | null }) {
78	  return run.mode === "implementation" && run.phase !== "planning";
79	}
80	
81	function isAgentBusyError(error: unknown) {
82	  return /\bagent is busy\b/i.test(formatErrorMessage(error));
83	}
84	
85	async function handleDirectWorkerQuotaError(args: {
86	  run: RunRecord;
87	  worker: WorkerRecord;
88	  error: unknown;
89	}) {
90	  const quotaInfo = extractQuotaResetInfo(args.error, { provider: args.worker.type });
91	  if (!quotaInfo.isQuotaError) {
92	    return false;
93	  }
94	
95	  await handleWorkerQuotaExhaustion({
96	    runId: args.run.id,
97	    workerId: args.worker.id,
98	    text: quotaInfo.rawText,
99	    provider: args.worker.type,
100	  });
101	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
102	  return true;
103	}
104	
105	function isAgentNotFoundError(error: unknown) {
106	  return /\b(agent not found|not_found|session not found|invalid session identifier|failed to load resumed session data from file|404)\b/i.test(formatErrorMessage(error));
107	}
108	
109	function isAgentAlreadyExistsError(error: unknown, workerId: string) {
110	  const message = formatErrorMessage(error).toLowerCase();
111	  return message.includes("agent already exists") && message.includes(workerId.toLowerCase());
112	}
113	
114	function normalizeWorkerStatus(status: string | null | undefined) {
115	  return status?.trim().toLowerCase().split(":")[0]?.trim() ?? "";
116	}
117	
118	function isWorkerCancelled(worker: WorkerRecord | null | undefined) {
119	  const status = normalizeWorkerStatus(worker?.status);
120	  return status === "cancelled" || status === "canceled";
121	}
122	
123	function isRunCancelled(run: RunRecord | null | undefined) {
124	  const status = normalizeWorkerStatus(run?.status);
125	  return status === "cancelled" || status === "canceled";
126	}
127	
128	function workerTurnSupersededError(workerId: string) {
129	  return Object.assign(
130	    new Error(`Worker turn superseded by a newer worker turn: ${workerId}`),
131	    { code: "WORKER_TURN_SUPERSEDED", status: 409, retryable: false },
132	  );
133	}
134	
135	function isStoppableRunStatus(status: string | null | undefined) {
136	  return ["running", "working", "stuck", "needs_recovery"].includes(normalizeWorkerStatus(status));
137	}
138	
139	function isStoppableWorkerStatus(status: string | null | undefined) {
140	  return ["starting", "working", "idle", "stuck"].includes(normalizeWorkerStatus(status));
141	}
142	
143	function workerCreatedAtMs(worker: WorkerRecord) {
144	  const createdAt = worker.createdAt;
145	  const value = createdAt instanceof Date ? createdAt.getTime() : new Date(createdAt).getTime();
146	  return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
147	}
148	
149	function compareWorkersForFollowUp(a: WorkerRecord, b: WorkerRecord) {
150	  const workerNumberDiff = (b.workerNumber ?? 0) - (a.workerNumber ?? 0);
151	  if (workerNumberDiff !== 0) {
152	    return workerNumberDiff;
153	  }
154	
155	  const createdAtDiff = workerCreatedAtMs(b) - workerCreatedAtMs(a);
156	  if (createdAtDiff !== 0) {
157	    return createdAtDiff;
158	  }
159	
160	  return b.id.localeCompare(a.id);
161	}
162	
163	async function selectConversationWorker(runId: string) {
164	  const runWorkers = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.runId, runId));
165	  const sortedWorkers = [...runWorkers].sort(compareWorkersForFollowUp);
166	  return sortedWorkers.find((worker) => !isWorkerCancelled(worker)) ?? sortedWorkers[0] ?? null;
167	}
168	
169	function isDirectWorkerSelectionSwitchable(worker: WorkerRecord) {
170	  return ["cancelled", "canceled", "done", "error", "failed", "idle", "stopped"].includes(normalizeWorkerStatus(worker.status));
171	}
