179	  const requestedModel = args.run.preferredWorkerModel?.trim() || null;
180	  const requestedEffort = args.run.preferredWorkerEffort?.trim().toLowerCase() || null;
181	  const requestedAccountId = args.run.preferredWorkerAccountId?.trim() || null;
182	  const currentAllocation = await db
183	    .select()
184	    .from(workerCredentialAllocations)
185	    .where(eq(workerCredentialAllocations.workerId, args.worker.id))
186	    .get();
187	  const currentModel = args.worker.effectiveLaunchModel?.trim() || null;
188	  const currentEffort = args.worker.effectiveLaunchEffort?.trim().toLowerCase() || null;
189	  const typeChanged = normalizeWorkerType(args.worker.type) !== normalizeWorkerType(requestedType);
190	  const modelChanged = Boolean(currentModel && requestedModel && currentModel !== requestedModel);
191	  const effortChanged = Boolean(currentEffort && requestedEffort && currentEffort !== requestedEffort);
192	  const accountChanged = Boolean(
193	    requestedAccountId
194	    && currentAllocation?.accountId
195	    && requestedAccountId !== currentAllocation.accountId,
196	  );
197	
198	  if (!typeChanged && !modelChanged && !effortChanged && !accountChanged) {
199	    return null;
200	  }
201	
202	  if (!isDirectWorkerSelectionSwitchable(args.worker)) {
203	    await recordExecutionEvent({
204	      runId: args.run.id,
205	      workerId: args.worker.id,
206	      planItemId: null,
207	      eventType: "worker_selection_deferred",
208	      details: {
209	        summary: `Deferred the ${requestedType} selection until ${args.worker.id} is idle.`,
210	        previousWorkerType: args.worker.type,
211	        nextWorkerType: requestedType,
212	        reason: "worker_turn_active",
213	      },
214	    });
215	    emitNamedEvent({
216	      kind: "worker.selection_deferred",
217	      runId: args.run.id,
218	      workerId: args.worker.id,
219	      requestedType,
220	      reason: "worker_turn_active",
221	    });
222	    return null;
223	  }
224	
225	  const selection: WorkerRecreationSelection = {
226	    type: requestedType,
227	    model: requestedModel,
228	    effort: requestedEffort,
229	    accountId: requestedAccountId,
230	    credentialSource: decodeClaudeGatewayModel(requestedModel) ? "gateway" : "account",
231	  };
232	
233	  try {
234	    const recreated = await recreateWorkerFromTranscript({
235	      run: args.run,
236	      worker: args.worker,
237	      nextUserPrompt: args.nextUserPrompt,
238	      source: "direct-follow-up",
239	      reason: "direct_worker_selection_changed",
240	      selection,
241	      expectedTurnGeneration: args.worker.turnGeneration,
242	    });
243	    await recordExecutionEvent({
244	      runId: args.run.id,
245	      workerId: args.worker.id,
246	      planItemId: null,
247	      eventType: "worker_selection_reconciled",
248	      details: {
249	        summary: `Recreated ${args.worker.id} to honor the selected ${requestedType} worker.`,
250	        previousWorkerType: args.worker.type,
251	        nextWorkerType: requestedType,
252	        previousWorkerModel: currentModel,
253	        nextWorkerModel: requestedModel,
254	        previousWorkerEffort: currentEffort,
255	        nextWorkerEffort: requestedEffort,
256	        previousAccountId: currentAllocation?.accountId ?? null,
257	        nextAccountId: requestedAccountId,
258	      },
259	    });
260	    const worker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, args.worker.id)).get();
261	    return {
262	      worker: worker ?? args.worker,
263	      replayPrompt: recreated.replayPrompt,
264	    };
265	  } catch (error) {
266	    if (isWorkerTurnSupersededError(error)) {
267	      throw error;
268	    }
269	    await db.update(workers).set({
270	      status: "error",
271	      updatedAt: new Date(),
272	    }).where(eq(workers.id, args.worker.id));
273	    await persistRunFailure(args.run.id, error);
274	    emitNamedEvent({
275	      kind: "error.surfaced",
276	      code: "worker.resume.failed",
277	      message: `Could not switch ${args.worker.id} to the selected ${requestedType} worker: ${formatErrorMessage(error)}`,
278	      surface: "banner",
279	      runId: args.run.id,
280	      workerId: args.worker.id,
281	      cause: error instanceof Error ? { name: error.name, message: error.message } : null,
282	    });
283	    throw error;
284	  }
285	}
286	
287	function elicitationAnswerContent(text: string, requestedSchema: ElicitationSchema | null | undefined): ElicitationContent {
288	  const properties = requestedSchema?.properties ?? {};
289	  const propertyNames = Object.keys(properties);
290	  if (propertyNames.includes("customAnswer")) {
291	    return { customAnswer: text };
292	  }
293	
294	  const nonCustomFields = propertyNames.filter((name) => name !== "customAnswer");
295	  if (nonCustomFields.length === 1 && nonCustomFields[0]) {
296	    return { [nonCustomFields[0]]: text };
297	  }
298	
299	  return { response: text };
300	}
301	
302	async function answerDirectWorkerElicitation(args: {
303	  run: RunRecord;
304	  worker: WorkerRecord;
305	  userText: string;
306	  workerText: string;
307	  attachments: ChatAttachment[];
308	  attachmentsJson: string | null;
309	  messageId: string;
310	}) {
311	  const snapshot = await Promise.resolve(getAgent(args.worker.id)).catch(() => null);
312	  const elicitation = snapshot?.pendingElicitations?.[0] ?? null;
313	  if (!elicitation) {
314	    return null;
315	  }
316	
317	  const createdAt = new Date();
318	  const userMessage = {
319	    id: args.messageId,
320	    runId: args.run.id,
321	    role: "user",
322	    kind: "checkpoint",
323	    content: args.userText,
324	    attachmentsJson: args.attachmentsJson,
325	    createdAt,
326	  };
327	
328	  await activateRunAndPersistMessage({
329	    run: args.run,
330	    nextStatus: "running",
331	    activatedAt: createdAt,
332	    workerFence: {
333	      workerId: args.worker.id,
334	      expectedTurnGeneration: args.worker.turnGeneration,
335	      allowCancelled: false,
336	    },
337	    persist: async () => {
338	      await appendUserInputOnDelivery({
339	        id: userMessage.id,
340	        runId: args.run.id,
341	        workerId: args.worker.id,
342	        text: args.userText,
343	        deliveredAt: createdAt,
344	        attachments: args.attachments.map((attachment) => ({
345	          id: attachment.id,
346	          filename: attachment.name,
347	          mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
348	          sizeBytes: attachment.size,
349	          storagePath: attachment.storagePath,
350	        })),
351	      });
352	      await db.insert(messages).values(userMessage);
353	      await respondElicitation(args.worker.id, {
354	        action: "accept",
355	        content: elicitationAnswerContent(args.workerText || args.userText, elicitation.requestedSchema),
356	      });
357	      await db.update(workers).set({
358	        status: "working",
359	        updatedAt: createdAt,
360	      }).where(and(
361	        eq(workers.id, args.worker.id),
362	        eq(workers.turnGeneration, args.worker.turnGeneration),
363	      ));
364	    },
365	  });
366	  emitNamedEvent({
367	    kind: "worker.status",
368	    runId: args.run.id,
369	    workerId: args.worker.id,
370	    prev: args.worker.status,
371	    next: "working",
372	  });
373	  await recordExecutionEvent({
374	    runId: args.run.id,
375	    workerId: args.worker.id,
376	    planItemId: null,
377	    eventType: "direct_worker_elicitation_answered",
378	    details: {
379	      summary: `Answered pending worker question for ${args.worker.id}.`,
380	      requestId: elicitation.requestId,
381	    },
382	    createdAt,
383	  });
384	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
385	  return userMessage;
386	}

388	async function retireMatchingQueuedDirectAnswers(args: {
389	  runId: string;
390	  workerId: string;
391	  content: string;
392	  attachmentsJson: string | null;
393	  answeredAt: Date;
394	}) {
395	  const expectedAttachmentsJson = serializeChatAttachments(parseChatAttachmentsJson(args.attachmentsJson));
396	  const queuedRows = await db
397	    .select()
398	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
399	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.runId, args.runId));
400	  const matchingRows = queuedRows.filter((row) => (
401	    (row.status === "pending" || row.status === "delivering")
402	    && (row.targetWorkerId === args.workerId || row.targetWorkerId === null)
403	    && row.content === args.content
404	    && serializeChatAttachments(parseChatAttachmentsJson(row.attachmentsJson)) === expectedAttachmentsJson
405	  ));
406	
407	  for (const row of matchingRows) {
408	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
409	      status: "cancelled",
410	      lastError: "Answered directly before queued delivery.",
411	      updatedAt: args.answeredAt,
412	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, row.id));
413	    await recordExecutionEvent({
414	      runId: args.runId,
415	      workerId: row.targetWorkerId ?? args.workerId,
416	      planItemId: null,
417	      eventType: "queued_message_cancelled",
418	      details: {
419	        summary: "Cancelled queued duplicate after the answer was delivered directly.",
420	        queuedMessageId: row.id,
421	        reason: "direct_answer_delivered",
422	      },
423	      createdAt: args.answeredAt,
424	    });
425	  }
426	}

428	/**
429	 * Reconcile the DB user-message list against the worker output stream.
430	 *
431	 * Historically this asserted the invariant and threw a 409 if any DB row
432	 * was missing from the stream. That surfaced as "Previous message is
433	 * still being persisted..." in the UI whenever the send-message flow
434	 * crashed between the DB insert and the stream write — and the only way
435	 * out was to hand-edit the DB.
436	 *
437	 * New contract: if a row is missing, silently backfill the stream entry
438	 * from the DB row. The stream is append-only and the user_input entry is
439	 * keyed by the message id, so the write is idempotent. We never block
440	 * the caller on this — at worst we log the failure and continue.
441	 */
442	export async function reconcileWorkerUserMessagesInStream(runId: string, workerId: string) {
443	  const [storedUserMessages, entries] = await Promise.all([
444	    db
445	      .select()
446	      .from(messages)
447	      .where(and(eq(messages.runId, runId), eq(messages.role, "user")))
448	      .orderBy(asc(messages.createdAt), asc(messages.id)),
449	    readWorkerOutputEntries(runId, workerId),
450	  ]);
451	  const streamUserInputIds = new Set(
452	    entries
453	      .filter((entry) => (entry as { type?: string }).type === "user_input")
454	      .map((entry) => entry.id),
455	  );
456	  for (const message of storedUserMessages) {
457	    if (streamUserInputIds.has(message.id)) continue;
458	    try {
459	      await appendUserInputOnDelivery({
460	        id: message.id,
461	        runId,
462	        workerId,
463	        text: message.content ?? "",
464	        deliveredAt: message.createdAt instanceof Date
465	          ? message.createdAt
466	          : new Date(message.createdAt as unknown as string | number),
467	        attachments: parseChatAttachmentsJson(message.attachmentsJson).map((attachment) => ({
468	          id: attachment.id,
469	          filename: attachment.name,
470	          mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
471	          sizeBytes: attachment.size,
472	          storagePath: attachment.storagePath,
473	        })),
474	      });
475	    } catch (error) {
476	      process.stderr.write(
477	        `[send-message] failed to backfill user_input for message ${message.id} on worker ${workerId}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}\n`,
478	      );
479	    }
480	  }
481	}
482	
483	export async function resumeMissingDirectWorker(
484	  run: RunRecord,
485	  worker: WorkerRecord,
486	  expectedTurnGeneration?: number,
487	) {
488	  const workerPredicate = expectedTurnGeneration === undefined
489	    ? eq(workers.id, worker.id)
490	    : and(
491	      eq(workers.id, worker.id),
492	      eq(workers.turnGeneration, expectedTurnGeneration),
493	    );
494	  let sessionId = worker.bridgeSessionId?.trim();
495	  let sessionMode = worker.bridgeSessionMode?.trim();
496	  if (!sessionId) {
497	    const metadata = await readWorkerSessionMetadata(worker.runId, worker.id);
498	    if (metadata) {
499	      sessionId = metadata.sessionId;
500	      sessionMode = metadata.sessionMode ?? sessionMode;
501	      await db.update(workers).set({
502	        bridgeSessionId: metadata.sessionId,
503	        bridgeSessionMode: metadata.sessionMode ?? worker.bridgeSessionMode,
504	        updatedAt: new Date(),
505	      }).where(workerPredicate);
506	      emitNamedEvent({
507	        kind: "worker.session_metadata_repaired",
508	        runId: worker.runId,
509	        workerId: worker.id,
510	      });
511	    }
512	  }
513	  if (!sessionId) {
514	    const message = `Direct worker ${worker.id} is missing persisted ACP session metadata.`;
515	    emitNamedEvent({
516	      kind: "error.surfaced",
517	      code: "worker.resume.failed",
518	      message,
519	      surface: "banner",
520	      runId: run.id,
521	      workerId: worker.id,
522	      cause: null,
523	    });
524	    throw Object.assign(new Error(message), { status: 500 });
525	  }
526	
527	  const yoloModeEnabled = await readWorkerYoloModeEnabled();
528	  const workerMode = resolveWorkerLaunchMode(sessionMode, yoloModeEnabled);
529	  const { env: envParams } = await readRuntimeEnvFromSettings();
530	  const launchSelection = resolveWorkerLaunchSelection(worker, run, {
531	    accountId: await readWorkerAllocatedAccountId(worker.id),
532	  });
533	  const spawnParams = {
534	    type: worker.type,
535	    cwd: worker.cwd,
536	    name: worker.id,
537	    ...(workerMode ? { mode: workerMode } : {}),
538	    env: envParams,
539	    ...(launchSelection.accountId ? { accountId: launchSelection.accountId } : {}),
540	    ...(launchSelection.model ? { model: launchSelection.model } : {}),
541	    ...(launchSelection.effort ? { effort: launchSelection.effort } : {}),
542	  };
543	  let resumedWorker;
544	  let recreatedFromRejectedEmptySession = false;
545	  let transcriptReplayRequired = false;
546	  try {
547	    resumedWorker = await spawnAgent({
548	      ...spawnParams,
549	      resumeSessionId: sessionId,
550	    });
551	  } catch (error) {
552	    if (isAgentAlreadyExistsError(error, worker.id)) {
553	      resumedWorker = await getAgent(worker.id);
554	    } else if (
555	      isRejectedSavedSessionErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(error))
556	      && await canRecreateRejectedSavedSession(run.id, worker.id)
557	    ) {
558	      await recordExecutionEvent({
559	        runId: run.id,
560	        workerId: worker.id,
561	        planItemId: null,
562	        eventType: "worker_session_missing",
563	        details: {
564	          summary: `Saved bridge session for ${worker.id} is not recoverable, and the worker stream has no provider transcript.`,
565	          sessionId,
566	          reason: formatErrorMessage(error),
567	        },
568	      });
569	      const startingWorker = await db.update(workers).set({
570	        status: "starting",
571	        bridgeSessionId: null,
572	        bridgeSessionMode: null,
573	        updatedAt: new Date(),
574	      }).where(workerPredicate).returning({ id: workers.id }).get();
575	      if (!startingWorker) {
576	        throw workerTurnSupersededError(worker.id);
577	      }
578	      resumedWorker = await spawnAgent(spawnParams);
579	      recreatedFromRejectedEmptySession = true;
580	    } else if (isRejectedSavedSessionErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(error))) {
581	      const materialized = await materializeProviderSessionFromWorkerStream({
582	        runId: run.id,
583	        workerId: worker.id,
584	        type: worker.type,
585	        sessionId,
586	        cwd: worker.cwd,
587	        errorMessage: formatErrorMessage(error),
588	        env: envParams,
589	      });
590	      if (materialized) {
591	        await recordExecutionEvent({
592	          runId: run.id,
593	          workerId: worker.id,
594	          planItemId: null,
595	          eventType: "worker_session_materialized",
596	          details: {
597	            summary: `Materialized ${materialized.provider} session ${sessionId} from the saved OmniHarness transcript.`,
598	            provider: materialized.provider,
599	            sessionId,
600	            filePath: materialized.filePath,
601	            messageCount: materialized.messageCount,
602	          },
603	        });
604	        try {
605	          resumedWorker = await spawnAgent({
606	            ...spawnParams,
607	            resumeSessionId: sessionId,
608	          });
609	        } catch (materializedResumeError) {
610	          await recordExecutionEvent({
611	            runId: run.id,
612	            workerId: worker.id,
613	            planItemId: null,
614	            eventType: "worker_session_materialized_resume_failed",
615	            details: {
616	              summary: `Materialized ${materialized.provider} session ${sessionId}, but ACP resume still failed.`,
617	              provider: materialized.provider,
618	              sessionId,
619	              filePath: materialized.filePath,
620	              reason: formatErrorMessage(materializedResumeError),
621	            },
622	          });
623	        }
624	      }
625	      if (!resumedWorker) {
626	        await recordExecutionEvent({
627	          runId: run.id,
628	          workerId: worker.id,
629	          planItemId: null,
630	          eventType: "worker_session_missing",
631	          details: {
632	            summary: `Saved bridge session for ${worker.id} is not recoverable; continuing from the saved OmniHarness transcript.`,
633	            sessionId,
634	            reason: formatErrorMessage(error),
635	            transcriptReplay: true,
636	          },
637	        });
638	        const startingWorker = await db.update(workers).set({
639	          status: "starting",
640	          bridgeSessionId: null,
641	          bridgeSessionMode: null,
642	          updatedAt: new Date(),
643	        }).where(workerPredicate).returning({ id: workers.id }).get();
644	        if (!startingWorker) {
645	          throw workerTurnSupersededError(worker.id);
646	        }
647	        resumedWorker = await spawnAgent(spawnParams);
648	        transcriptReplayRequired = true;
649	      }
650	    } else {
651	      emitNamedEvent({
652	        kind: "error.surfaced",
653	        code: "worker.resume.failed",
654	        message: formatErrorMessage(error),
655	        surface: "banner",
656	        runId: run.id,
657	        workerId: worker.id,
658	        cause: error instanceof Error ? { name: error.name, message: error.message } : null,
659	      });
660	      throw error;
661	    }
662	  }
663	
664	  const persistedWorker = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(run.id, async () => {
665	    const currentRun = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, run.id)).get();
666	    if (!currentRun || isRunCancelled(currentRun)) {
667	      return false;
668	    }
669	    return Boolean(await db.update(workers).set({
670	      status: resumedWorker.state,
671	      bridgeSessionId: resumedWorker.sessionId ?? (recreatedFromRejectedEmptySession ? null : sessionId),
672	      bridgeSessionMode: resumedWorker.sessionMode ?? sessionMode ?? null,
673	      updatedAt: new Date(),
674	    }).where(workerPredicate).returning({ id: workers.id }).get());
675	  });
676	  if (!persistedWorker) {
677	    await cancelAgent(worker.id).catch(() => undefined);
678	    throw workerTurnSupersededError(worker.id);
679	  }
680	
681	  await recordExecutionEvent({
682	    runId: run.id,
683	    workerId: worker.id,
684	    planItemId: null,
685	    eventType: transcriptReplayRequired
686	      ? "worker_session_recreated_from_transcript"
687	      : recreatedFromRejectedEmptySession ? "worker_session_recreated" : "worker_session_resumed",
688	    details: {
689	      summary: transcriptReplayRequired
690	        ? `Started a fresh runtime worker for ${worker.id} and continued from the saved OmniHarness transcript.`
691	        : recreatedFromRejectedEmptySession
692	        ? `Started a fresh runtime worker for ${worker.id} after its empty saved session was rejected.`
693	        : `Resumed ${worker.id} from saved session`,
694	      rejectedSessionId: recreatedFromRejectedEmptySession || transcriptReplayRequired ? sessionId : null,
695	      sessionId: recreatedFromRejectedEmptySession || transcriptReplayRequired ? resumedWorker.sessionId ?? null : sessionId,
696	      transcriptReplay: transcriptReplayRequired,
697	    },
698	  });
699	  await reconcileRecoveredHumanInputEntries({
700	    runId: run.id,
701	    workerId: worker.id,
702	    activeElicitationRequestIds: (resumedWorker.pendingElicitations ?? []).map((entry) => entry.requestId),
703	    activePermissionRequestIds: (resumedWorker.pendingPermissions ?? []).map((entry) => entry.requestId),
704	    reason: "the worker was resumed for a follow-up and the recovered runtime no longer owns this request",
705	  });
706	  emitNamedEvent({
707	    kind: recreatedFromRejectedEmptySession || transcriptReplayRequired ? "worker.recreated" : "worker.reattached",
708	    runId: run.id,
709	    workerId: worker.id,
710	  });
711	
712	  await appendWorkerSessionMetadata({
713	    runId: run.id,
714	    workerId: worker.id,
715	    sessionId: resumedWorker.sessionId ?? (recreatedFromRejectedEmptySession ? null : sessionId),
716	    sessionMode: resumedWorker.sessionMode ?? sessionMode ?? null,
717	    source: "direct-follow-up",
718	  });
719	
720	  await persistWorkerSnapshot(worker.id, resumedWorker, { expectedTurnGeneration });
721	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
722	  return { ...resumedWorker, transcriptReplayRequired };
723	}
724	
725	// A provider that blames the credential is usually just having a bad second.
726	// Retry the turn a couple of times before anyone concludes the account is dead.
727	const AUTH_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS = [2_000, 6_000];
728	
729	function sleep(ms: number) {
730	  return new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
731	}
732	
733	async function resolveWorkerAccountId(run: RunRecord, worker: WorkerRecord) {
734	  const allocation = await db
735	    .select()
736	    .from(workerCredentialAllocations)
737	    .where(eq(workerCredentialAllocations.workerId, worker.id))
738	    .get();
739	  return allocation?.accountId ?? run.preferredWorkerAccountId?.trim() ?? null;
740	}
741	
742	/**
743	 * Decide whether an auth-shaped failure is really the account's fault.
744	 *
745	 * Returns the message to persist: annotated as verified-live when the
746	 * credential provably still works (so recovery stays armed), as verified-dead
747	 * when the probe was rejected too (so the UI asks for a re-login instead of
748	 * another send), or untouched when the probe could not run at all.
749	 */
750	async function resolveAuthFailureMessage(run: RunRecord, worker: WorkerRecord, message: string) {
751	  if (!isAuthShapedProviderFailure(message) || !supportsCredentialLivenessProbe(worker.type)) {
752	    return message;
753	  }
754	
755	  const accountId = await resolveWorkerAccountId(run, worker);
756	  const verification = await verifyAccountCredentialLiveness({
757	    workerType: worker.type,
758	    accountId,
759	    cwd: worker.cwd || run.projectPath || process.cwd(),
760	  });
761	
762	  await recordExecutionEvent({
763	    runId: run.id,
764	    workerId: worker.id,
765	    eventType: "worker_credential_verified",
766	    details: {
767	      summary: verification.liveness === "live"
768	        ? "Provider reported an auth failure but the credential still works; treating it as transient."
769	        : `Credential verification returned "${verification.liveness}"; the failure stands.`,
770	      accountId,
771	      liveness: verification.liveness,
772	      detail: verification.detail,
773	      providerError: message,
774	    },
775	  });
776	
777	  if (verification.liveness === "live") {
778	    return annotateVerifiedLiveCredential(message);
779	  }
780	
781	  if (verification.liveness === "dead") {
782	    // Two independent requests agreed the credential is rejected. Nothing the
783	    // run can do fixes that, so say so instead of leaving the UI to guess.
784	    emitNamedEvent({
785	      kind: "account.login_required",
786	      accountId: accountId ?? "default",
787	      workerType: worker.type,
788	      reason: verification.detail,
789	    });
790	    return annotateVerifiedDeadCredential(message, accountId);
791	  }
792	
793	  // "unknown" — the probe itself could not run. Callers still treat this as
794	  // permanent, but we have not proven the credential is dead, so the message
795	  // stays unmarked and the UI keeps its generic failure copy.
796	  return message;
797	}
798	
799	/**
800	 * A 403/401 on a prompt aborts the turn before any work happens, so replaying
801	 * it is safe — and it is the single cheapest way to shake off the provider's
802	 * intermittent "Account suspended" answers.
803	 */
804	async function askAgentWithAuthRetry(
805	  workerId: string,
806	  prompt: string,
807	  imageAttachments?: Array<{ path: string; mimeType: string }>,
808	  expectedTurnGeneration?: number,
809	) {
810	  for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt += 1) {
811	    try {
812	      return await (imageAttachments?.length
813	        ? askAgent(workerId, prompt, imageAttachments, { expectedTurnGeneration })
814	        : askAgent(workerId, prompt, undefined, { expectedTurnGeneration }));
815	    } catch (error) {
816	      const exhausted = attempt >= AUTH_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS.length;
817	      if (exhausted || !isAuthShapedProviderFailure(formatErrorMessage(error))) {
818	        throw error;
819	      }
820	      await sleep(AUTH_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS[attempt]);
821	    }
822	  }
823	}
824	
825	async function askDirectWorkerWithResume(
826	  run: RunRecord,
827	  worker: WorkerRecord,
828	  content: string,
829	  imageAttachments?: Array<{ path: string; mimeType: string }>,
830	  promptOverride?: string | null,
831	  expectedTurnGeneration?: number,
832	) {
833	  const workerPrompt = promptOverride ?? (isDirectRunMode(run.mode) ? buildDirectWorkerPrompt(content) : content);
834	  try {
835	    return await askAgentWithAuthRetry(worker.id, workerPrompt, imageAttachments, expectedTurnGeneration);
836	  } catch (error) {
837	    if (isProviderSessionDiagnosticErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(error))) {
838	      const currentWorker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id)).get();
839	      const recreated = await recreateWorkerFromTranscript({
840	        run,
841	        worker: currentWorker ?? worker,
842	        nextUserPrompt: workerPrompt,
843	        source: "direct-follow-up",
844	        reason: "provider_session_diagnostic_after_direct_follow_up",
845	        expectedTurnGeneration,
846	      });
847	      await db.update(workers).set({
848	        status: "working",
849	        updatedAt: new Date(),
850	      }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
851	      await db.update(runs).set({
852	        status: "running",
853	        failedAt: null,
854	        lastError: null,
855	        updatedAt: new Date(),
856	      }).where(eq(runs.id, run.id));
857	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
858	
859	      try {
860	        return imageAttachments?.length
861	          ? await askAgent(worker.id, recreated.replayPrompt, imageAttachments, { expectedTurnGeneration })
862	          : await askAgent(worker.id, recreated.replayPrompt, undefined, { expectedTurnGeneration });
863	      } catch (retryError) {
864	        if (isProviderSessionDiagnosticErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(retryError))) {
865	          throw new Error(userFacingProviderSessionErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(retryError)));
866	        }
867	        throw retryError;
868	      }
869	    }
870	
871	    if (!isAgentNotFoundError(error)) {
872	      throw error;
873	    }
874	
875	    const currentWorker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id)).get();
876	    if (isWorkerCancelled(currentWorker)) {
877	      throw error;
878	    }
879	
880	    const resumedWorker = await resumeMissingDirectWorker(run, currentWorker ?? worker);
881	    if (!resumedWorker) {
882	      throw error;
883	    }
884	
885	    // resumeMissingDirectWorker just wrote the resumed runtime agent's
886	    // state ("idle" or "starting") into the DB. The askAgent call on the
887	    // next line drives the runtime to "working", but no other code path
888	    // touches the workers row until askAgent resolves — which for a long
889	    // Claude turn is many minutes. Without re-arming the DB row here the
890	    // frontend sees the worker as idle for the entire turn and never
891	    // shows the "Thinking…" indicator.
892	    await db.update(workers).set({
893	      status: "working",
894	      updatedAt: new Date(),
895	    }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
896	    await db.update(runs).set({
897	      status: "running",
898	      failedAt: null,
899	      lastError: null,
900	      updatedAt: new Date(),
901	    }).where(eq(runs.id, run.id));
902	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
903	
904	    const replayPrompt = resumedWorker.transcriptReplayRequired
905	      ? await buildTranscriptReplayPrompt({
906	        runId: run.id,
907	        workerId: worker.id,
908	        nextUserPrompt: workerPrompt,
909	      })
910	      : null;
911	    try {
912	      return imageAttachments?.length
913	        ? await askAgent(worker.id, replayPrompt ?? workerPrompt, imageAttachments, { expectedTurnGeneration })
914	        : await askAgent(worker.id, replayPrompt ?? workerPrompt, undefined, { expectedTurnGeneration });
915	    } catch (retryError) {
916	      if (isProviderSessionDiagnosticErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(retryError))) {
917	        throw new Error(userFacingProviderSessionErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(retryError)));
918	      }
919	      throw retryError;
920	    }
921	  }
922	}
923	
924	async function continueWorkerConversation({
925	  run,
926	  worker,
927	  content,
928	  userInputText,
929	  userInputId,
930	  attachments,
931	  appendUserInputBeforeAsk = false,
932	  allowCancelledWorkerResume = false,
933	  promptOverride = null,
934	  expectedTurnGeneration,
935	  onUserInputAppended,
936	}: {
937	  run: RunRecord;
938	  worker: WorkerRecord;
939	  content: string;
940	  userInputText: string;
941	  userInputId?: string;
942	  attachments: ChatAttachment[];
943	  appendUserInputBeforeAsk?: boolean;
944	  allowCancelledWorkerResume?: boolean;
945	  promptOverride?: string | null;
946	  expectedTurnGeneration: number;
947	  onUserInputAppended?: () => void;
948	}) {
949	  try {
950	    const currentWorker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id)).get();
951	    if (isWorkerCancelled(currentWorker) && !allowCancelledWorkerResume) {
952	      onUserInputAppended?.();
953	      return;
954	    }
955	
956	    const activatedWorker = await db.update(workers).set({
957	      status: "working",
958	      updatedAt: new Date(),
959	    }).where(and(
960	      eq(workers.id, worker.id),
961	      eq(workers.turnGeneration, expectedTurnGeneration),
962	    )).returning({ id: workers.id }).get();
963	    if (!activatedWorker) {
964	      onUserInputAppended?.();
965	      return;
966	    }
967	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
968	
969	    let userInputAppended = false;
970	    const appendDeliveredUserInput = async (deliveredAt: Date) => {
971	      await appendUserInputOnDelivery({
972	        id: userInputId,
973	        runId: run.id,
974	        workerId: worker.id,
975	        text: userInputText,
976	        deliveredAt,
977	        attachments: attachments.map((attachment) => ({
978	          id: attachment.id,
979	          filename: attachment.name,
980	          mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
981	          sizeBytes: attachment.size,
982	          storagePath: attachment.storagePath,
983	        })),
984	      });
985	      userInputAppended = true;
986	      onUserInputAppended?.();
987	    };
988	
989	    if (appendUserInputBeforeAsk) {
990	      await appendDeliveredUserInput(new Date());
991	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
992	    }
993	
994	    const imageAttachments = resolveImageAttachments(attachments, getAppDataPath);
995	    const response = await askDirectWorkerWithResume(
996	      run,
997	      worker,
998	      content,
999	      imageAttachments,
1000	      promptOverride,
1001	      expectedTurnGeneration,
1002	    );
1003	    if (!userInputAppended) {
1004	      // Append user_input on delivery — `askDirectWorkerWithResume` has
1005	      // resolved successfully, so the prompt definitely reached the
1006	      // worker. Direct fire-and-forget follow-ups opt into pre-ask
1007	      // appending above because the HTTP response returns immediately
1008	      // while the bridge turn is still in flight.
1009	      await appendDeliveredUserInput(new Date());
1010	    }
1011	    const workerAfterResponse = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id)).get();
1012	    if (isWorkerCancelled(workerAfterResponse)) {
1013	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
1014	      return;
1015	    }
1016	
1017	    const snapshot = await Promise.resolve(getAgent(worker.id)).catch(() => null);
1018	    if (snapshot) {
1019	      await persistWorkerSnapshot(worker.id, snapshot);
1020	    }
1021	    await appendAskResponseFallbackEntry({
1022	      runId: run.id,
1023	      workerId: worker.id,
1024	      responseText: response.response,
1025	      snapshot,
1026	    });
1027	
1028	    const workerAfterSnapshot = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id)).get();
1029	    if (isWorkerCancelled(workerAfterSnapshot)) {
1030	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
1031	      return;
1032	    }
1033	
1034	    await db.update(workers).set({
1035	      status: snapshot?.state ?? response.state,
1036	      updatedAt: new Date(),
1037	    }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
1038	
1039	    await resolveRecoveryIncidentsAfterHealthyTurn({
1040	      runId: run.id,
1041	      workerId: worker.id,
1042	      summary: `${worker.id} completed a turn normally after recovery was pending.`,
1043	      reason: "worker_turn_succeeded",
1044	    });
1045	
1046	    // Worker response now lives in the unified worker stream — the
1047	    // bridge entries written by persistWorkerSnapshot above carry the
1048	    // response text. The legacy role:"worker" messages row is gone.
1049	
1050	    if (isPlanningRun(run)) {
1051	      const latestRun = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, run.id)).get();
1052	      const latestWorker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id)).get();
1053	      if (latestRun) {
1054	        await refreshPlanningArtifactsForRun({
1055	          run: latestRun,
1056	          worker: latestWorker,
1057	          snapshot,
1058	          responseText: response.response,
1059	        });
1060	      }
1061	    } else if (isDirectRunMode(run.mode)) {
1062	      await updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput({
1063	        runId: run.id,
1064	        workerId: worker.id,
1065	        workerStatus: snapshot?.state ?? response.state,
1066	        responseText: response.response,
1067	        renderedOutput: snapshot?.renderedOutput,
1068	        currentText: snapshot?.currentText,
1069	        lastText: snapshot?.lastText,
1070	        outputEntries: snapshot?.outputEntries,
1071	        pendingPermissions: snapshot?.pendingPermissions,
1072	        pendingElicitations: snapshot?.pendingElicitations,
1073	      });
1074	    }
1075	
1076	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
1077	  } catch (error) {
1078	    // A stop or steer aborted this turn deliberately. Marking the worker
1079	    // `error` and failing the run here would turn every stop into a red banner.
1080	    if (isWorkerTurnSupersededError(error) || isWorkerTurnAbortedError(error)) {
1081	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
1082	      return;
1083	    }
1084	    const currentWorker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id)).get();
1085	    if (isWorkerCancelled(currentWorker)) {
1086	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
1087	      return;
1088	    }
1089	
1090	    if (isAgentBusyError(error)) {
1091	      const now = new Date();
1092	      await db.update(workers).set({
1093	        status: "working",
1094	        updatedAt: now,
1095	      }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
1096	      await db.update(runs).set({
1097	        status: isPlanningRun(run) ? "working" : "running",
1098	        failedAt: null,
1099	        lastError: null,
1100	        updatedAt: now,
1101	      }).where(eq(runs.id, run.id));
1102	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
1103	      throw Object.assign(error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(formatErrorMessage(error)), { status: 409 });
1104	    }
1105	
1106	    if (await handleDirectWorkerQuotaError({ run, worker, error })) {
1107	      return;
1108	    }
1109	
1110	    const surfacedErrorMessage = await resolveAuthFailureMessage(
1111	      run,
1112	      worker,
1113	      userFacingProviderSessionErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(error)),
1114	    );
1115	    await db.update(workers).set({
1116	      status: "error",
1117	      updatedAt: new Date(),
1118	    }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
1119	    await persistRunFailure(run.id, new Error(surfacedErrorMessage), {
1120	      surface: {
1121	        code: hasVerifiedDeadCredentialMarker(surfacedErrorMessage)
1122	          ? "account.login_required"
1123	          : "conversation.continue.failed",
1124	        workerId: worker.id,
1125	      },
1126	    });
1127	    throw isProviderSessionDiagnosticErrorMessage(formatErrorMessage(error))
1128	      ? new Error(surfacedErrorMessage)
1129	      : error;
1130	  }
1131	}
1132	
1133	type SendConversationMessageArgs = {
1134	  runId: string;
1135	  content: string;
1136	  /**
1137	   * Id the sending client already rendered its own bubble under. Adopting it
1138	   * as the row id means the optimistic row and the persisted row are the same
1139	   * row, so the bubble never changes React key between send and delivery.
1140	   * Rejected unless it is a plain UUID, and ignored when absent.
1141	   */
1142	  clientMessageId?: string | null;
1143	  attachments?: ChatAttachment[];
1144	  busyAction?: BusyMessageAction | null;
1145	  preferredWorkerType?: string | null;
1146	  preferredWorkerModel?: string | null;
1147	  preferredWorkerEffort?: string | null;
1148	  preferredWorkerAccountId?: string | null;
1149	  allowedWorkerTypes?: string[] | string | null;
1150	};
1151	
1152	const CLIENT_MESSAGE_ID_PATTERN = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
1153	
1154	/**
1155	 * A send writes at most one user row — the elicitation-answer, supervised and
1156	 * direct branches are mutually exclusive — so one id covers all three.
1157	 *
1158	 * Falls back to a fresh uuid rather than failing the send when the client id
1159	 * is unusable: malformed, or already taken. The taken case is a resend after
1160	 * a partial failure (the row landed, a later step threw); minting a new id
1161	 * degrades that to a duplicate row instead of a primary-key 500 that would
1162	 * lose the user's text entirely.
1163	 */
1164	export async function resolveUserMessageId(clientMessageId: string | null | undefined) {
1165	  const candidate = typeof clientMessageId === "string" ? clientMessageId.trim().toLowerCase() : "";
1166	  if (!CLIENT_MESSAGE_ID_PATTERN.test(candidate)) {
1167	    return randomUUID();
1168	  }
1169	  const existing = await db.select({ id: messages.id }).from(messages).where(eq(messages.id, candidate)).get();
1170	  return existing ? randomUUID() : candidate;
1171	}
1172	
1173	function parseExplicitWorkerType(value: string | null | undefined) {
1174	  if (!value?.trim()) {
1175	    return null;
1176	  }
1177	  const normalized = normalizeWorkerType(value.replace(/\s+/g, "-"));
1178	  return SUPPORTED_WORKER_TYPES.includes(normalized as SupportedWorkerType)
1179	    ? normalized as SupportedWorkerType
1180	    : null;
1181	}
1182	
1183	function parseWorkerSwitchFromText(content: string) {
1184	  const match = content.match(/\b(?:switch|change|set)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:cli\s+)?(?:worker|workers|agent|agents|worker\s+agent|worker\s+agents)\s+(?:to|as)\s+(codex|claude(?:[-_\s]+code)?|gemini|open[-_\s]*code|opencode)\b/i);
1185	  if (!match?.[1]) {
1186	    return null;
1187	  }
1188	  return parseExplicitWorkerType(match[1]);
1189	}
1190	
1191	async function applyWorkerPreferenceForMessage(args: {
1192	  run: RunRecord;
1193	  content: string;
1194	  preferredWorkerType?: string | null;
1195	  preferredWorkerModel?: string | null;
1196	  preferredWorkerEffort?: string | null;
1197	  preferredWorkerAccountId?: string | null;
1198	  allowedWorkerTypes?: string[] | string | null;
1199	}) {
1200	  const explicitWorkerType = parseExplicitWorkerType(args.preferredWorkerType);
1201	  const textWorkerType = parseWorkerSwitchFromText(args.content);
1202	  const nextWorkerType = explicitWorkerType ?? textWorkerType;
1203	  if (!nextWorkerType) {
1204	    return args.run;
1205	  }
1206	
1207	  const nextAllowedWorkerTypes = [nextWorkerType];
1208	  const nextPreferredWorkerModel = explicitWorkerType
1209	    ? args.preferredWorkerModel?.trim() || null
1210	    : null;
1211	  const nextPreferredWorkerEffort = args.preferredWorkerEffort?.trim() || args.run.preferredWorkerEffort || null;
1212	  const nextPreferredWorkerAccountId = args.preferredWorkerAccountId?.trim() || null;
1213	  const now = new Date();
1214	
1215	  await db.update(runs).set({
1216	    preferredWorkerType: nextWorkerType,
1217	    preferredWorkerModel: nextPreferredWorkerModel,
1218	    preferredWorkerEffort: nextPreferredWorkerEffort,
1219	    preferredWorkerAccountId: nextPreferredWorkerAccountId,
1220	    allowedWorkerTypes: JSON.stringify(nextAllowedWorkerTypes),
1221	    updatedAt: now,
1222	  }).where(eq(runs.id, args.run.id));
1223	  await recordExecutionEvent({
1224	    runId: args.run.id,
1225	    workerId: null,
1226	    planItemId: null,
1227	    eventType: "worker_selection_changed",
1228	    details: {
1229	      summary: `Changed preferred worker selection to ${nextWorkerType}.`,
1230	      preferredWorkerType: nextWorkerType,
1231	      preferredWorkerModel: nextPreferredWorkerModel,
1232	      preferredWorkerEffort: nextPreferredWorkerEffort,
1233	      preferredWorkerAccountId: nextPreferredWorkerAccountId,
1234	      allowedWorkerTypes: nextAllowedWorkerTypes,
1235	      source: explicitWorkerType ? "composer_selection" : "message_text",
1236	    },
1237	    createdAt: now,
1238	  });
1239	
1240	  return {
1241	    ...args.run,
1242	    preferredWorkerType: nextWorkerType,
1243	    preferredWorkerModel: nextPreferredWorkerModel,
1244	    preferredWorkerEffort: nextPreferredWorkerEffort,
1245	    preferredWorkerAccountId: nextPreferredWorkerAccountId,
1246	    allowedWorkerTypes: JSON.stringify(nextAllowedWorkerTypes),
1247	    updatedAt: now,
1248	  };
1249	}
1250	
1251	async function activateRunAndPersistMessage<T>(args: {
1252	  run: RunRecord;
1253	  nextStatus: string;
1254	  activatedAt: Date;
1255	  workerFence?: { workerId: string; expectedTurnGeneration: number; allowCancelled: boolean };
1256	  persist: () => Promise<T>;
1257	}) {
1258	  return runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.run.id, async () => {
1259	    if (args.workerFence) {
1260	      const currentWorker = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, args.workerFence.workerId)).get();
1261	      const workerOwnershipLost = !currentWorker
1262	        || currentWorker.turnGeneration !== args.workerFence.expectedTurnGeneration
1263	        || (isWorkerCancelled(currentWorker) && !args.workerFence.allowCancelled);
1264	      if (workerOwnershipLost) {
1265	        const reason = "Worker ownership changed before the message could be delivered.";
1266	        emitNamedEvent({
1267	          kind: "session.input.refused",
1268	          runId: args.run.id,
1269	          sessionType: args.run.sessionType,
1270	          code: "worker_changed_before_delivery",
1271	          reason,
1272	        });
1273	        emitNamedEvent({
1274	          kind: "error.surfaced",
1275	          code: "conversation.delivery_refused",
1276	          message: reason,
1277	          surface: "toast",
1278	          runId: args.run.id,
1279	          workerId: args.workerFence.workerId,
1280	          conversationId: args.run.id,
1281	        });
1282	        throw Object.assign(new Error(reason), { status: 409 });
1283	      }
1284	    }
1285	
1286	    // The status observed when this send began is its ownership token. Stop
1287	    // writes `cancelled` under this same fence. A send that began before Stop
1288	    // therefore loses this CAS, while a deliberate new send that begins after
1289	    // Stop observes `cancelled` and may explicitly resume the conversation.
1290	    const activatedRun = await db.update(runs).set({
1291	      status: args.nextStatus,
1292	      failedAt: null,
1293	      lastError: null,
1294	      updatedAt: args.activatedAt,
1295	    }).where(and(
1296	      eq(runs.id, args.run.id),
1297	      eq(runs.status, args.run.status),
1298	    )).returning().get();
1299	
1300	    if (!activatedRun) {
1301	      const currentRun = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, args.run.id)).get();
1302	      const reason = currentRun
1303	        ? `Conversation changed from ${args.run.status} to ${currentRun.status} before the message could be delivered.`
1304	        : "Conversation was removed before the message could be delivered.";
1305	      emitNamedEvent({
1306	        kind: "session.input.refused",
1307	        runId: args.run.id,
1308	        sessionType: args.run.sessionType,
1309	        code: "run_changed_before_delivery",
1310	        reason,
1311	      });
1312	      emitNamedEvent({
1313	        kind: "error.surfaced",
1314	        code: "conversation.delivery_refused",
1315	        message: reason,
1316	        surface: "toast",
1317	        runId: args.run.id,
1318	        conversationId: args.run.id,
1319	      });
1320	      throw Object.assign(new Error(reason), { status: 409 });
1321	    }
1322	
1323	    const persisted = await args.persist();
1324	    return { run: activatedRun, persisted };
1325	  });
1326	}
1327	
1328	async function stopConversationFromManualStopCommand(run: RunRecord) {
1329	  if (!isStoppableRunStatus(run.status)) {
1330	    return {
1331	      ok: true as const,
1332	      stopped: false as const,
1333	      ignored: true as const,
1334	      runId: run.id,
1335	      workerId: null,
1336	      runCancelled: false,
1337	      reason: "not_stoppable" as const,
1338	    };
1339	  }
1340	
1341	  if (isSupervisedRun(run)) {
1342	    cancelSupervisorWake(run.id);
1343	    stopRunObserver(run.id);
1344	    await clearSupervisorWakeLease(run.id);
1345	    const runWorkers = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.runId, run.id));
1346	    const activeWorkers = runWorkers.filter((worker) => isStoppableWorkerStatus(worker.status));
1347	    for (const worker of activeWorkers) {
1348	      void cancelAgent(worker.id).catch(() => undefined);
1349	      await db.update(workers).set({
1350	        status: "cancelled",
1351	        updatedAt: new Date(),
1352	      }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
1353	      emitNamedEvent({
1354	        kind: "worker.status",
1355	        runId: run.id,
1356	        workerId: worker.id,
1357	        prev: worker.status,
1358	        next: "cancelled",
1359	      });
1360	      emitNamedEvent({
1361	        kind: "worker.terminal",
1362	        runId: run.id,
1363	        workerId: worker.id,
1364	        status: "cancelled",
1365	      });
1366	    }
1367	
1368	    const now = new Date();
1369	    await db.update(runs).set({
1370	      status: "cancelled",
1371	      updatedAt: now,
1372	    }).where(eq(runs.id, run.id));
1373	    await recordExecutionEvent({
1374	      runId: run.id,

80	  workerId: string;

82	  error: unknown;
83	}) {

99	function timestampMs(value: Date | string | number | null | undefined) {
100	  if (value instanceof Date) {
101	    return value.getTime();
102	  }
103	  const parsed = new Date(value ?? 0).getTime();
104	  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : 0;
105	}

107	async function nextQueuedMessageCreatedAt(runId: string) {
108	  const latest = await db
109	    .select({ createdAt: queuedConversationMessages.createdAt })
110	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
111	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.runId, runId))
112	    .orderBy(desc(queuedConversationMessages.createdAt), desc(queuedConversationMessages.id))
113	    .get();
114	  const previousMs = Math.max(
115	    timestampMs(latest?.createdAt),
116	    lastQueuedMessageCreatedAtByRun.get(runId) ?? 0,
117	  );
118	  // Drizzle's SQLite timestamp mode stores integer seconds in this schema,
119	  // so use a one-second logical tick to preserve FIFO order through DB reads
120	  // that sort only by createdAt.
121	  const nextMs = Math.max(Date.now(), previousMs + 1_000);
122	  lastQueuedMessageCreatedAtByRun.set(runId, nextMs);
123	  return new Date(nextMs);
124	}

153	function workerStreamHasOutputAfterInput(
154	  entries: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof readWorkerOutputEntries>>,
155	  userInputEntryId: string,
156	) {
157	  const inputEntry = entries.find((entry) => entry.id === userInputEntryId);
158	  if (!inputEntry) {
159	    return false;
160	  }
161	
162	  return entries.some((entry) => (
163	    entry.seq > inputEntry.seq
164	    && entry.type !== "user_input"
165	    && entry.text.trim().length > 0
166	  ));
167	}

240	function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
241	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)
242	    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
243	    : null;
244	}
245	
246	function asString(value: unknown): string | null {
247	  return typeof value === "string" ? value : null;
248	}
249	
250	function asRequestedSchema(value: unknown): ElicitationSchema | null {
251	  const schema = asRecord(value);
252	  if (!schema) {
253	    return null;
254	  }
255	  const properties = asRecord(schema.properties) ?? {};
256	  const required = Array.isArray(schema.required)
257	    ? schema.required.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string")
258	    : undefined;
259	  const type = asString(schema.type);
260	  return {
261	    properties,
262	    ...(type ? { type } : {}),
263	    ...(required ? { required } : {}),
264	  };
265	}
266	
267	function isOpenElicitationEntry(entry: NonNullable<WorkerSnapshot["outputEntries"]>[number]) {
268	  if (entry.type !== "elicitation") {
269	    return false;
270	  }
271	  const status = (entry.status ?? "pending").trim().toLowerCase();
272	  return !["answered", "approved", "cancelled", "canceled", "completed", "declined", "denied", "failed", "rejected", "skipped"].includes(status);
273	}
274	
275	function pendingElicitationFromEntry(entry: NonNullable<WorkerSnapshot["outputEntries"]>[number]): PendingElicitation | null {
276	  if (!isOpenElicitationEntry(entry)) {
277	    return null;
278	  }
279	  const raw = asRecord(entry.raw);
280	  const requestId = raw?.requestId;
281	  if (!raw || typeof requestId !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(requestId)) {
282	    return null;
283	  }
284	  return {
285	    requestId,
286	    requestedAt: entry.timestamp,
287	    sessionId: asString(raw.sessionId),
288	    toolCallId: asString(raw.toolCallId ?? entry.toolCallId),
289	    message: asString(raw.message),
290	    requestedSchema: asRequestedSchema(raw.requestedSchema),
291	  };
292	}
293	
294	function selectPendingWorkerElicitation(snapshot: WorkerSnapshot | null): PendingElicitation | null {
295	  const pending = snapshot?.pendingElicitations?.[0] ?? null;
296	  if (pending) {
297	    return pending;
298	  }
299	  const entries = snapshot?.outputEntries ?? [];
300	  for (const entry of entries) {
301	    const elicitation = pendingElicitationFromEntry(entry);
302	    if (elicitation) {
303	      return elicitation;
304	    }
305	  }
306	  return null;
307	}
308	
309	function elicitationAnswerContent(text: string, requestedSchema: ElicitationSchema | null | undefined): ElicitationContent {
310	  const properties = requestedSchema?.properties ?? {};
311	  const propertyNames = Object.keys(properties);
312	  if (propertyNames.includes("customAnswer")) {
313	    return { customAnswer: text };
314	  }
315	
316	  const nonCustomFields = propertyNames.filter((name) => name !== "customAnswer");
317	  if (nonCustomFields.length === 1 && nonCustomFields[0]) {
318	    return { [nonCustomFields[0]]: text };
319	  }
320	
321	  return { response: text };
322	}
323	
324	async function answerPendingWorkerElicitation(args: {
325	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect;
326	  worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect;
327	  snapshot: WorkerSnapshot | null;
328	  content: string;
329	  deliveredAt: Date;
330	}) {
331	  const elicitation = selectPendingWorkerElicitation(args.snapshot);
332	  if (!elicitation) {
333	    return false;
334	  }
335	
336	  try {
337	    await respondElicitation(args.worker.id, {
338	      action: "accept",
339	      content: elicitationAnswerContent(args.content, elicitation.requestedSchema),
340	    });
341	  } catch (error) {
342	    if (!isElicitationNoLongerPendingError(error)) {
343	      throw error;
344	    }
345	    // The question is gone, but the user's answer is not. Close the stale
346	    // stream row so the card stops advertising an open question, then report
347	    // "not delivered as an elicitation" so the caller falls through to normal
348	    // prompt delivery. Rethrowing here would fail the queued row and silently
349	    // discard everything the user typed.
350	    await closeStaleHumanInputEntries({
351	      workerId: args.worker.id,
352	      kind: "elicitation",
353	      requestId: elicitation.requestId,
354	      reason: "the worker stopped waiting for an answer",
355	    });
356	    return false;
357	  }
358	  await db.update(workers).set({
359	    status: "working",
360	    updatedAt: args.deliveredAt,
361	  }).where(eq(workers.id, args.worker.id));
362	  await db.update(runs).set({
363	    status: "running",
364	    failedAt: null,
365	    lastError: null,
366	    updatedAt: args.deliveredAt,
367	  }).where(eq(runs.id, args.run.id));
368	  emitNamedEvent({
369	    kind: "worker.status",
370	    runId: args.run.id,
371	    workerId: args.worker.id,
372	    prev: args.worker.status,
373	    next: "working",
374	  });
375	  return true;
376	}

378	export async function persistDeliveredWorkerResponse({
379	  run,
380	  workerId,
381	  response,
382	  deliveredAt,
383	  userInputEntryId,
384	}: {
385	  run: WorkerResponseRun;
386	  workerId: string;
387	  response: WorkerAskResponse;
388	  deliveredAt: Date;
389	  userInputEntryId: string;
390	}) {
391	  const snapshot = await Promise.resolve(getAgent(workerId)).catch(() => null);
392	  if (snapshot) {
393	    await persistWorkerSnapshot(workerId, snapshot);
394	  }
395	  await appendAskResponseFallbackEntry({
396	    runId: run.id,
397	    workerId,
398	    responseText: response.response,
399	    snapshot,
400	  });
401	  await assertQueuedDeliveryProducedOutput({
402	    runId: run.id,
403	    workerId,
404	    userInputEntryId,
405	    response,
406	    snapshot,
407	  });
408	
409	  await db.update(workers).set({
410	    status: snapshot?.state ?? response.state,
411	    updatedAt: deliveredAt,
412	  }).where(eq(workers.id, workerId));
413	
414	  await resolveRecoveryIncidentsAfterHealthyTurn({
415	    runId: run.id,
416	    workerId,
417	    summary: `${workerId} delivered a queued message normally after recovery was pending.`,
418	    reason: "queued_message_delivered",
419	  });
420	
421	  if (run.mode === "direct" || run.mode === "commit") {
422	    await updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput({
423	      runId: run.id,
424	      workerId,
425	      workerStatus: snapshot?.state ?? response.state,
426	      responseText: response.response,
427	      renderedOutput: snapshot?.renderedOutput,
428	      currentText: snapshot?.currentText,
429	      lastText: snapshot?.lastText,
430	      outputEntries: snapshot?.outputEntries,
431	      pendingPermissions: snapshot?.pendingPermissions,
432	      pendingElicitations: snapshot?.pendingElicitations,
433	    });
434	  }
435	}

451	export async function listPendingQueuedConversationMessages(runId: string) {
452	  const records = await db
453	    .select()
454	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
455	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.runId, runId))
456	    .orderBy(asc(queuedConversationMessages.createdAt), asc(queuedConversationMessages.id));
457	
458	  // `delivering` has left the queue: the message is with the worker and already
459	  // shown in the transcript. Failed dispatches return to `pending`.
460	  return records
461	    .filter((record) => record.status === "pending")
462	    .map(serializeQueuedConversationMessage);
463	}
464	
465	/**
466	 * `delivering` means "an in-process delivery owns this row". No such delivery
467	 * survives a process restart, so any row still marked `delivering` at boot is
468	 * orphaned: its owner died mid-flight. Left alone it dangles forever — and
469	 * since `delivering` rows are no longer listed as queued, it would dangle
470	 * invisibly. Reclaim them as `pending` so the user's message comes back.
471	 */
472	export async function reclaimOrphanedDeliveringMessages() {
473	  const orphaned = await db
474	    .select({ id: queuedConversationMessages.id, runId: queuedConversationMessages.runId })
475	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
476	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.status, "delivering"));
477	  if (orphaned.length === 0) {
478	    return 0;
479	  }
480	
481	  const now = new Date();
482	  await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
483	    status: "pending",
484	    lastError: null,
485	    updatedAt: now,
486	    deliveredAt: null,
487	  }).where(inArray(queuedConversationMessages.id, orphaned.map((record) => record.id)));
488	
489	  for (const record of orphaned) {
490	    await insertQueueExecutionEvent(record.runId, "queued_message_reclaimed", {
491	      summary: "Requeued a message whose delivery was interrupted by a restart.",
492	      queuedMessageId: record.id,
493	    });
494	  }
495	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
496	  return orphaned.length;
497	}
