```text
499	const CLIENT_MESSAGE_ID_PATTERN = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
500	
501	/**
502	 * Falls back to a fresh uuid when the client id is unusable — malformed, or
503	 * already taken by an earlier queue row — so a resend degrades to a duplicate
504	 * row rather than a primary-key failure that would lose the user's text.
505	 */
506	async function resolveQueuedConversationMessageId(clientMessageId: string | null | undefined) {
507	  const candidate = typeof clientMessageId === "string" ? clientMessageId.trim().toLowerCase() : "";
508	  if (!CLIENT_MESSAGE_ID_PATTERN.test(candidate)) {
509	    return randomUUID();
510	  }
511	  const existing = await db
512	    .select({ id: queuedConversationMessages.id })
513	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
514	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, candidate))
515	    .get();
516	  return existing ? randomUUID() : candidate;
517	}
518	
519	export async function createQueuedConversationMessage({
520	  runId,
521	  targetWorkerId = null,
522	  action,
523	  content,
524	  attachments = [],
525	  clientMessageId = null,
526	}: {
527	  runId: string;
528	  targetWorkerId?: string | null;
529	  action: BusyMessageAction;
530	  content: string;
531	  attachments?: ChatAttachment[];
532	  /**
533	   * Id the sending client already rendered its queue row under. Adopting it
534	   * keeps that row and this one the same row, so the queue entry does not
535	   * blink out and back when the event stream catches up.
536	   */
537	  clientMessageId?: string | null;
538	}) {
539	  const trimmedContent = content.trim();
540	  const normalizedAttachments = normalizeChatAttachments(attachments);
541	  if (!trimmedContent && normalizedAttachments.length === 0) {
542	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Message content or attachment is required"), { status: 400 });
543	  }
544	
545	  const now = await nextQueuedMessageCreatedAt(runId);
546	  const record = {
547	    id: await resolveQueuedConversationMessageId(clientMessageId),
548	    runId,
549	    targetWorkerId,
550	    action,
551	    content: trimmedContent,
552	    attachmentsJson: serializeChatAttachments(normalizedAttachments),
553	    status: "pending",
554	    lastError: null,
555	    createdAt: now,
556	    updatedAt: now,
557	    deliveredAt: null,
558	  };
559	
560	  await db.insert(queuedConversationMessages).values(record);
561	  await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_created", {
562	    summary: action === "steer" ? "Steering message was deferred into the queue." : "Message queued for the next safe turn.",
563	    queuedMessageId: record.id,
564	    action,
565	  }, targetWorkerId);
566	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
567	  return serializeQueuedConversationMessage(record);
568	}
569	
570	export async function cancelQueuedConversationMessage({
571	  runId,
572	  messageId,
573	}: {
574	  runId: string;
575	  messageId: string;
576	}) {
577	  const record = await db
578	    .select()
579	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
580	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId))
581	    .get();
582	
583	  if (!record || record.runId !== runId) {
584	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Queued message not found"), { status: 404 });
585	  }
586	
587	  if (record.status === "delivered") {
588	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Queued message was already delivered and cannot be cancelled"), { status: 409 });
589	  }
590	
591	  if (record.status === "cancelled") {
592	    return serializeQueuedConversationMessage(record);
593	  }
594	
595	  const now = new Date();
596	  await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
597	    status: "cancelled",
598	    updatedAt: now,
599	  }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId));
600	  await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_cancelled", {
601	    summary: "Cancelled queued message.",
602	    queuedMessageId: messageId,
603	  }, record.targetWorkerId);
604	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
605	
606	  return serializeQueuedConversationMessage({
607	    ...record,
608	    status: "cancelled",
609	    updatedAt: now,
610	  });
611	}
612	
613	async function deliverQueuedWorkerSteering(args: {
614	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect;
615	  worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect;
616	  messageId: string;
617	  userMessageId?: string | null;
618	  content: string;
619	  userText: string;
620	  attachments: ChatAttachment[];
621	  onUserInputAppended?: () => void;
622	}) {
623	  await runWorkerTurn(args.worker.id, async () => {
624	    const appendQueuedUserInput = async (deliveredAt: Date) => {
625	      await appendUserInputOnDelivery({
626	        id: args.userMessageId ?? args.messageId,
627	        runId: args.run.id,
628	        workerId: args.worker.id,
629	        text: args.userText,
630	        deliveredAt,
631	        attachments: args.attachments.map((attachment) => ({
632	          id: attachment.id,
633	          filename: attachment.name,
634	          mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
635	          sizeBytes: attachment.size,
636	          storagePath: attachment.storagePath,
637	        })),
638	      });
639	      args.onUserInputAppended?.();
640	    };
641	
642	    if (await queuedMessageStatus(args.messageId) !== "delivering") {
643	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
644	      return;
645	    }
646	
647	    const snapshotBeforeAsk = await Promise.resolve(getAgent(args.worker.id)).catch(() => null);
648	    const deliveredAt = new Date();
649	    if (await queuedMessageStatus(args.messageId) !== "delivering") {
650	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
651	      return;
652	    }
653	    if (await answerPendingWorkerElicitation({
654	      run: args.run,
655	      worker: args.worker,
656	      snapshot: snapshotBeforeAsk,
657	      content: args.userText,
658	      deliveredAt,
659	    })) {
660	      await appendQueuedUserInput(deliveredAt);
661	      await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
662	        status: "delivered",
663	        lastError: null,
664	        updatedAt: deliveredAt,
665	        deliveredAt,
666	      }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, args.messageId));
667	      await insertQueueExecutionEvent(args.run.id, "queued_message_delivered", {
668	        summary: `Delivered queued answer to ${args.worker.id}'s pending question.`,
669	        queuedMessageId: args.messageId,
670	        action: "steer",
671	        delivery: "elicitation",
672	      }, args.worker.id);
673	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
674	      return;
675	    }
676	
677	    // Anchor the user's message before the ask: this is the user-initiated
678	    // send-now path, and the bridge starts streaming output during askAgent, so
679	    // appending afterwards would order the reply ahead of the prompt.
680	    await appendQueuedUserInput(deliveredAt);
681	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
682	
683	    if (await queuedMessageStatus(args.messageId) !== "delivering") {
684	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
685	      return;
686	    }
687	
688	    const queuedPrompt = workerPromptForRun(args.run, args.content);
689	    const queuedImages = resolveImageAttachments(args.attachments, getAppDataPath);
690	    const response = queuedImages.length
691	      ? await askAgent(args.worker.id, queuedPrompt, queuedImages)
692	      : await askAgent(args.worker.id, queuedPrompt);
693	    await persistDeliveredWorkerResponse({
694	      run: args.run,
695	      workerId: args.worker.id,
696	      response,
697	      deliveredAt,
698	      userInputEntryId: args.userMessageId ?? args.messageId,
699	    });
700	    // Worker response now lives in the unified worker stream; the
701	    // legacy role:"worker" messages row is no longer written.
702	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
703	      status: "delivered",
704	      lastError: null,
705	      updatedAt: deliveredAt,
706	      deliveredAt,
707	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, args.messageId));
708	    await insertQueueExecutionEvent(args.run.id, "queued_message_delivered", {
709	      summary: `Delivered immediate queued steering to ${args.worker.id}.`,
710	      queuedMessageId: args.messageId,
711	      action: "steer",
712	    }, args.worker.id);
713	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
714	  });
715	}
716	
717	async function continueQueuedWorkerSteering(args: {
718	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect;
719	  worker: typeof workers.$inferSelect;
720	  messageId: string;
721	  userMessageId?: string | null;
722	  content: string;
723	  userText: string;
724	  attachments: ChatAttachment[];
725	}) {
726	  let userInputAppended = false;
727	  try {
728	    await deliverQueuedWorkerSteering({
729	      ...args,
730	      onUserInputAppended: () => {
731	        userInputAppended = true;
732	      },
733	    });
734	    return;
735	  } catch (error) {
736	    if (isAgentNotFoundError(error)) {
737	      const fallbackWorker = await getLatestRunWorker(args.run.id, args.worker.id);
738	      if (fallbackWorker) {
739	        try {
740	          await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
741	            targetWorkerId: fallbackWorker.id,
742	            updatedAt: new Date(),
743	          }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, args.messageId));
744	          await deliverQueuedWorkerSteering({
745	            ...args,
746	            worker: fallbackWorker,
747	            onUserInputAppended: () => {
748	              userInputAppended = true;
749	            },
750	          });
751	          return;
752	        } catch (fallbackError) {
753	          error = fallbackError;
754	        }
755	      }
756	    }
757	
758	    const failedAt = new Date();
759	    if (await queuedMessageStatus(args.messageId) !== "delivering") {
760	      notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
761	      return;
762	    }
763	    if (await handleQueuedWorkerQuotaError({
764	      runId: args.run.id,
765	      workerId: args.worker.id,
766	      workerType: args.worker.type,
767	      error,
768	    })) {
769	      await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
770	        status: "pending",
771	        lastError: null,
772	        updatedAt: new Date(),
773	      }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, args.messageId));
774	      return;
775	    }
776	
777	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
778	      status: isAgentBusyError(error) ? "pending" : "failed",
779	      lastError: errorMessage(error),
780	      updatedAt: failedAt,
781	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, args.messageId));
782	    if (isEmptyQueuedWorkerOutputError(error)) {
783	      await db.update(workers).set({
784	        status: "error",
785	        outputLog: error.message,
786	        updatedAt: failedAt,
787	      }).where(eq(workers.id, args.worker.id));
788	      if (args.run.mode === "direct" || args.run.mode === "commit") {
789	        await persistRunFailure(args.run.id, error, {
790	          surface: { code: "worker.idle.empty_output", workerId: args.worker.id },
791	        });
792	      }
793	    }
794	    if (args.userMessageId && (isAgentBusyError(error) || !userInputAppended)) {
795	      await db.delete(messages).where(eq(messages.id, args.userMessageId));
796	    }
797	    await insertQueueExecutionEvent(args.run.id, isAgentBusyError(error) ? "queued_message_deferred" : "queued_message_failed", {
798	      summary: isAgentBusyError(error)
799	        ? `Worker ${args.worker.id} is still busy; queued message will be retried.`
800	        : `Immediate queued steering failed for ${args.worker.id}.`,
801	      queuedMessageId: args.messageId,
802	      action: "steer",
803	      error: errorMessage(error),
804	    }, args.worker.id);
805	    if (isAgentNotFoundError(error)) {
806	      await insertQueueExecutionEvent(args.run.id, "queued_message_recovery_blocked", {
807	        summary: `Queued message ${args.messageId} is blocked because ${args.worker.id} is missing.`,
808	        queuedMessageId: args.messageId,
809	        action: "steer",
810	        error: errorMessage(error),
811	      }, args.worker.id);
812	      await reconcileRunRecovery({
813	        runId: args.run.id,
814	        liveAgents: [],
815	        source: "queued-message-delivery",
816	      });
817	    }
818	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
819	  }
820	}
821	
822	export async function sendQueuedConversationMessageNow({
823	  runId,
824	  messageId,
825	}: {
826	  runId: string;
827	  messageId: string;
828	}) {
829	  const record = await db
830	    .select()
831	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
832	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId))
833	    .get();
834	
835	  if (!record || record.runId !== runId || record.status !== "pending") {
836	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Queued message not found"), { status: 404 });
837	  }
838	
839	  const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
840	  if (!run) {
841	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Conversation not found"), { status: 404 });
842	  }
843	
844	  const normalizedAttachments = normalizeChatAttachments(record.attachmentsJson ? JSON.parse(record.attachmentsJson) : []);
845	  const workerContent = appendAttachmentContext(record.content, normalizedAttachments, {
846	    resolvePath: (storagePath) => getAppDataPath(storagePath),
847	    imagesInlined: true,
848	  });
849	  const startedAt = new Date();
850	
851	  await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
852	    action: "steer",
853	    status: "delivering",
854	    lastError: null,
855	    updatedAt: startedAt,
856	  }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId));
857	
858	  if (run.mode === "implementation") {
859	    await db.update(runs).set({
860	      status: "running",
861	      failedAt: null,
862	      lastError: null,
863	      updatedAt: startedAt,
864	    }).where(eq(runs.id, runId));
865	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
866	      action: "steer",
867	      status: "pending",
868	      lastError: null,
869	      updatedAt: startedAt,
870	      deliveredAt: null,
871	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId));
872	    await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_sent_now", {
873	      summary: "Accepted queued message for implementation worker steering.",
874	      queuedMessageId: messageId,
875	      action: "steer",
876	    });
877	    startSupervisorRun(runId);
878	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
879	
880	    return {
881	      ok: true,
882	      queuedMessage: serializeQueuedConversationMessage({
883	        ...record,
884	        action: "steer",
885	        status: "pending",
886	        lastError: null,
887	        updatedAt: startedAt,
888	        deliveredAt: null,
889	      }),
890	    };
891	  }
892	
893	  const targetWorker = record.targetWorkerId
894	    ? await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, record.targetWorkerId)).get()
895	    : null;
896	  const worker = targetWorker && targetWorker.runId === runId
897	    ? targetWorker
898	    : await getLatestRunWorker(runId, record.targetWorkerId);
899	  if (!worker || worker.runId !== runId) {
900	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
901	      status: "pending",
902	      updatedAt: new Date(),
903	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId));
904	    throw Object.assign(new Error("Conversation worker not found"), { status: 404 });
905	  }
906	
907	  if (isWorkerClearlyBusy(worker)) {
908	    const error = `Ask failed: Agent is busy: ${worker.id}`;
909	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
910	      action: "steer",
911	      targetWorkerId: worker.id,
912	      status: "pending",
913	      lastError: error,
914	      updatedAt: new Date(),
915	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, messageId));
916	    await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_deferred", {
917	      summary: `Worker ${worker.id} is still busy; queued message will be retried.`,
918	      queuedMessageId: messageId,
919	      action: "steer",
920	      error,
921	    }, worker.id);
922	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
923	
924	    return {
925	      ok: true,
926	      queuedMessage: serializeQueuedConversationMessage({
927	        ...record,
928	        targetWorkerId: worker.id,
929	        action: "steer",
930	        status: "pending",
931	        lastError: error,
932	        updatedAt: startedAt,
933	        deliveredAt: null,
934	      }),
935	    };
936	  }
937	
938	  const userMessage = {
939	    id: messageId,
940	    runId,
941	    role: "user",
942	    kind: "checkpoint",
943	    content: record.content,
944	    attachmentsJson: record.attachmentsJson,
945	    createdAt: startedAt,
946	  };
947	
948	  await db.insert(messages).values(userMessage);
949	  await db.update(runs).set({
950	    status: run.mode === "planning" ? "working" : "running",
951	    failedAt: null,
952	    lastError: null,
953	    updatedAt: startedAt,
954	  }).where(eq(runs.id, runId));
955	  await db.update(workers).set({
956	    status: "working",
957	    updatedAt: startedAt,
958	  }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
959	
960	  await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_sent_now", {
961	    summary: `Accepted queued message for immediate steering to ${worker.id}.`,
962	    queuedMessageId: messageId,
963	    action: "steer",
964	  }, worker.id);
965	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
966	
967	  continueQueuedWorkerSteering({
968	    run,
969	    worker,
970	    messageId,
971	    userMessageId: userMessage.id,
972	    content: workerContent,
973	    userText: record.content,
974	    attachments: normalizedAttachments,
975	  }).catch((error) => {
976	    console.error("Queued message immediate steering failed:", error);
977	  });
978	
979	  return {
980	    ok: true,
981	    message: serializeMessageRecord(userMessage),
982	    queuedMessage: serializeQueuedConversationMessage({
983	      ...record,
984	      targetWorkerId: worker.id,
985	      action: "steer",
986	      status: "delivering",
987	      lastError: null,
988	      updatedAt: startedAt,
989	      deliveredAt: null,
990	    }),
991	  };
992	}
993	
994	async function pendingQueueRecords(runId: string, workerId?: string | null) {
995	  const records = await db
996	    .select()
997	    .from(queuedConversationMessages)
998	    .where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.runId, runId))
999	    .orderBy(asc(queuedConversationMessages.createdAt), asc(queuedConversationMessages.id));
1000	
1001	  return records.filter((record) => {
1002	    if (record.status !== "pending") {
1003	      return false;
1004	    }
1005	
1006	    if (workerId === undefined) {
1007	      return record.targetWorkerId === null;
1008	    }
1009	
1010	    return record.targetWorkerId === workerId;
1011	  });
1012	}
1013	
1014	export async function drainQueuedImplementationMessages(runId: string) {
1015	  const records = await pendingQueueRecords(runId);
1016	  let deliveredCount = 0;
1017	
1018	  for (const record of records) {
1019	    const now = new Date();
1020	    await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1021	      status: "delivering",
1022	      updatedAt: now,
1023	    }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1024	
1025	    if (record.action === "steer") {
1026	      const targetWorker = record.targetWorkerId
1027	        ? await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.id, record.targetWorkerId)).get()
1028	        : null;
1029	      const worker = targetWorker && targetWorker.runId === runId && !isCancelledWorkerStatus(targetWorker.status)
1030	        ? targetWorker
1031	        : await getLatestRunWorker(runId, record.targetWorkerId);
1032	
1033	      if (!worker || worker.runId !== runId || isCancelledWorkerStatus(worker.status)) {
1034	        await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1035	          status: "pending",
1036	          lastError: "Conversation worker not found",
1037	          updatedAt: new Date(),
1038	        }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));
1039	        await insertQueueExecutionEvent(runId, "queued_message_deferred", {
1040	          summary: "No active implementation worker is available; queued steering will be retried.",
1041	          queuedMessageId: record.id,
1042	          action: "steer",
1043	          error: "Conversation worker not found",
1044	        });
1045	        continue;
1046	      }
1047	
1048	      const normalizedAttachments = normalizeChatAttachments(record.attachmentsJson ? JSON.parse(record.attachmentsJson) : []);
1049	      const workerContent = appendAttachmentContext(record.content, normalizedAttachments, {
1050	        resolvePath: (storagePath) => getAppDataPath(storagePath),
1051	      });
1052	      let interventionId: string | null = null;
1053	
1054	      try {
1055	        await runWorkerTurn(worker.id, async () => {
1056	          await db.update(workers).set({
1057	            status: "working",
1058	            updatedAt: now,
1059	          }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
1060	          const intervention = await recordSupervisorIntervention({
1061	            runId,
1062	            workerId: worker.id,
1063	            prompt: workerContent,
1064	            summary: `Sent user steering to ${worker.id}`,
1065	            interventionType: "continue",
1066	          });
1067	          interventionId = intervention.id;
1068	          const steerImages = resolveImageAttachments(normalizedAttachments, getAppDataPath);
1069	          const response = steerImages.length
1070	            ? await askAgent(worker.id, workerContent, steerImages)
1071	            : await askAgent(worker.id, workerContent);
1072	          const deliveredAt = new Date();
1073	          const userMessage = {
1074	            id: randomUUID(),
1075	            runId,
1076	            role: "user" as const,
1077	            kind: "checkpoint" as const,
1078	            content: record.content,
1079	            attachmentsJson: record.attachmentsJson,
1080	            createdAt: record.createdAt,
1081	          };
1082	          await appendUserInputOnDelivery({
1083	            id: userMessage.id,
1084	            runId,
1085	            workerId: worker.id,
1086	            text: record.content,
1087	            deliveredAt,
1088	            attachments: normalizedAttachments.map((attachment) => ({
1089	              id: attachment.id,
1090	              filename: attachment.name,
1091	              mimeType: attachment.mimeType,
1092	              sizeBytes: attachment.size,
1093	              storagePath: attachment.storagePath,
1094	            })),
1095	          });
1096	          await db.insert(messages).values(userMessage);
1097	          await db.insert(messages).values({
1098	            id: randomUUID(),
1099	            runId,
1100	            role: "supervisor",
1101	            kind: "update",
1102	            content: `Got it. I sent that to ${formatWorkerLabel(worker)} and will keep watching the run.`,
1103	            attachmentsJson: null,
1104	            createdAt: deliveredAt,
1105	          });
1106	          await persistDeliveredWorkerResponse({
1107	            run: { id: runId, mode: "implementation" },
1108	            workerId: worker.id,
1109	            response,
1110	            deliveredAt,
1111	            userInputEntryId: userMessage.id,
1112	          });
1113	          // Worker response now lives in the unified worker stream.
1114	          await db.update(queuedConversationMessages).set({
1115	            targetWorkerId: worker.id,
1116	            status: "delivered",
1117	            updatedAt: deliveredAt,
1118	            deliveredAt,
1119	            lastError: null,
1120	          }).where(eq(queuedConversationMessages.id, record.id));

1198	export async function drainQueuedWorkerMessages({
```

```text
1	import { and, eq, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
2	import { db } from "@/server/db";
3	import { recoveryIncidents, runs, workers } from "@/server/db/schema";
4	import { notifyEventStreamSubscribers } from "@/server/events/live-updates";
5	import { emitNamedEvent } from "@/server/events/named-events";
6	import { recordExecutionEvent } from "@/server/events/execution-event-store";
7	import { runMilestoneAutoCommit } from "@/server/git/run-auto-commit";
8	
9	const ACTIVE_QUOTA_INCIDENT_STATUSES = ["open", "recovering"] as const;
10	
11	type OutputEntryLike = {
12	  type?: string | null;
13	  text?: string | null;
14	  status?: string | null;
15	  raw?: unknown;
16	};
17	
18	type WorkerOutputSource = {
19	  workerStatus?: string | null;
20	  responseText?: string | null;
21	  renderedOutput?: string | null;
22	  currentText?: string | null;
23	  lastText?: string | null;
24	  outputLog?: string | null;
25	  outputEntries?: readonly OutputEntryLike[] | null;
26	  outputEntriesJson?: string | null;
27	  pendingPermissions?: readonly unknown[] | null;
28	  pendingElicitations?: readonly unknown[] | null;
29	};
30	
31	const ACTIVE_DIRECT_WORKER_STATUSES = new Set(["starting", "working", "stuck", "recovering"]);
32	
33	function normalizeWorkerStatus(value: string | null | undefined) {
34	  return value?.trim().toLowerCase().split(":")[0]?.trim() ?? "";
35	}
36	
37	function parseOutputEntriesJson(value: string | null | undefined): OutputEntryLike[] {
38	  if (!value?.trim()) {
39	    return [];
40	  }
41	
42	  try {
43	    const parsed = JSON.parse(value) as unknown;
44	    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((entry): entry is OutputEntryLike => {
45	      return typeof entry === "object" && entry !== null;
46	    }) : [];
47	  } catch {
48	    return [];
49	  }
50	}
51	
52	function visibleEntryText(entries: readonly OutputEntryLike[] | null | undefined) {
53	  return (entries ?? [])
54	    .filter((entry) => !entry.type || entry.type === "message")
55	    .map((entry) => entry.text ?? "")
56	    .filter((text) => text.trim().length > 0);
57	}
58	
59	function latestVisibleEntryText(entries: readonly OutputEntryLike[] | null | undefined) {
60	  return visibleEntryText(entries).at(-1) ?? "";
61	}
62	
63	function firstNonEmptyText(values: ReadonlyArray<string | null | undefined>) {
64	  return values.find((value) => typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0) ?? "";
65	}
66	
67	function isOpenHumanInputEntry(entry: OutputEntryLike) {
68	  if (entry.type !== "permission" && entry.type !== "elicitation") {
69	    return false;
70	  }
71	  const status = (entry.status ?? "pending").trim().toLowerCase();
72	  return !["answered", "approved", "cancelled", "canceled", "completed", "declined", "denied", "failed", "rejected", "skipped"].includes(status);
73	}
74	
75	function entryRequestId(entry: OutputEntryLike) {
76	  const raw = entry.raw;
77	  if (typeof raw !== "object" || raw === null) {
78	    return null;
79	  }
80	  const requestId = (raw as { requestId?: unknown }).requestId;
81	  return typeof requestId === "number" && Number.isFinite(requestId) ? requestId : null;
82	}
83	
84	function entriesHaveOpenHumanInput(entries: readonly OutputEntryLike[]) {
85	  const pendingByRequestId = new Map<string, boolean>();
86	
87	  for (const entry of entries) {
88	    if (entry.type !== "permission" && entry.type !== "elicitation") {
89	      continue;
90	    }
91	
92	    const open = isOpenHumanInputEntry(entry);
93	    const requestId = entryRequestId(entry);
94	    if (requestId === null) {
95	      if (open) {
96	        return true;
97	      }
98	      continue;
99	    }
100	
101	    pendingByRequestId.set(`${entry.type}:${requestId}`, open);
102	  }
103	
104	  return [...pendingByRequestId.values()].some(Boolean);
105	}
106	
107	export function directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput(source: WorkerOutputSource) {
108	  return (
109	    (source.pendingPermissions?.length ?? 0) > 0
110	    || (source.pendingElicitations?.length ?? 0) > 0
111	    || entriesHaveOpenHumanInput(source.outputEntries ?? [])
112	    || entriesHaveOpenHumanInput(parseOutputEntriesJson(source.outputEntriesJson))
113	  );
114	}
115	
116	// Trailing "?", tolerating closing quotes/brackets and trailing punctuation.
117	const QUESTION_TAIL_PATTERN = /\?["'”’)\]]*[.\s]*$/;
118	
119	function endsWithQuestion(text: string) {
120	  const lastLine = text
121	    .trim()
122	    .split(/\r?\n/)
123	    .map((line) => line.trim())
124	    .filter(Boolean)
125	    .at(-1);
126	  return Boolean(lastLine && QUESTION_TAIL_PATTERN.test(lastLine));
127	}
128	
129	/**
130	 * A worker can block on the user without ever raising a structured permission
131	 * or elicitation — it just asks in prose and goes idle. Treating that as "done"
132	 * marks the conversation finished in the UI while the worker is in fact waiting
133	 * for an answer, so the question is never surfaced and never answered.
134	 */
135	export function directWorkerOutputAsksBlockingQuestion(source: WorkerOutputSource) {
136	  // Deliberately entry-derived only. `outputLog`/`lastText` on a persisted
137	  // worker row are stale prose that outlive the turn, and reconciling an
138	  // already-finished conversation off them would flip it back to awaiting_user
139	  // forever. Message entries only exist for a turn that actually just spoke.
140	  const latestText = firstNonEmptyText([
141	    latestVisibleEntryText(source.outputEntries),
142	    latestVisibleEntryText(parseOutputEntriesJson(source.outputEntriesJson)),
143	  ]);
144	
145	  return endsWithQuestion(latestText);
146	}
147	
148	export function resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(source: WorkerOutputSource) {
149	  if (directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput(source)) {
150	    return "awaiting_user";
151	  }
152	
153	  // A quota-blocked worker is idle, but the conversation is emphatically not
154	  // finished — it is parked waiting for the provider window to reopen. Letting
155	  // it fall through to "done" below silently cleared the run out of
156	  // `quota_waiting`, which hid the "waiting for quota reset" banner and (until
157	  // recovery stopped keying off `runs.status`) stranded the resume entirely.
158	  if (normalizeWorkerStatus(source.workerStatus) === "cred-exhausted") {
159	    return "quota_waiting";
160	  }
161	
162	  if (ACTIVE_DIRECT_WORKER_STATUSES.has(normalizeWorkerStatus(source.workerStatus))) {
163	    return "running";
164	  }
165	
166	  // Only once the worker has stopped: a question mid-turn is just narration.
167	  return directWorkerOutputAsksBlockingQuestion(source) ? "awaiting_user" : "done";
168	}

170	export async function updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(args: WorkerOutputSource & {
171	  runId: string;
172	  workerId?: string | null;
173	}) {
174	  const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, args.runId)).get();
175	  if (!run || (run.mode !== "direct" && run.mode !== "commit")) {
176	    return null;
177	  }
178	
179	  const quotaIncident = await db.select({ id: recoveryIncidents.id })
180	    .from(recoveryIncidents)

182	      eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, args.runId),
183	      eq(recoveryIncidents.kind, "quota_exhausted"),
184	      inArray(recoveryIncidents.status, [...ACTIVE_QUOTA_INCIDENT_STATUSES]),
185	    ))

237	
238	  const nextStatus = resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(args);
239	  const now = new Date();
240	  if (run.status !== nextStatus || run.failedAt || run.lastError) {
241	    await db.update(runs).set({
242	      status: nextStatus,
243	      failedAt: null,
244	      lastError: null,
245	      updatedAt: now,
246	    }).where(eq(runs.id, args.runId));
247	  }
248	
249	  if (nextStatus === "awaiting_user" && run.status !== "awaiting_user") {
250	    emitNamedEvent({
251	      kind: "conversation.awaiting_user",
252	      runId: args.runId,
253	      workerId: args.workerId ?? undefined,
254	      reason: "worker_requested_input",
255	    });
256	    await recordExecutionEvent({
257	      runId: args.runId,
258	      workerId: args.workerId ?? null,
259	      planItemId: null,
260	      eventType: "direct_worker_awaiting_user",
261	      details: { reason: "worker_requested_input" },
262	      createdAt: now,
263	    });
264	  } else if (run.mode === "direct" && nextStatus === "done" && run.status !== "done") {
265	    await runMilestoneAutoCommit(args.runId, firstNonEmptyText([
266	      args.responseText,
267	      args.currentText,
268	      args.lastText,
269	      latestVisibleEntryText(args.outputEntries),
270	      latestVisibleEntryText(parseOutputEntriesJson(args.outputEntriesJson)),
271	      args.renderedOutput,
272	      args.outputLog,
273	    ]));
274	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
275	  } else if (nextStatus !== run.status || run.failedAt || run.lastError) {
276	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
277	  }
278	
279	  return nextStatus;
280	}
281	
282	export async function updateDirectRunAwaitingUserInputIfRequested(args: WorkerOutputSource & {
283	  runId: string;
284	  workerId?: string | null;
285	}) {
286	  if (!directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput(args)) {
287	    return false;
288	  }
289	
290	  await updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(args);
291	  return true;
292	}
```

```text
1	import { AsyncLocalStorage } from "node:async_hooks";
2	import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
3	import { db } from "@/server/db";
4	import { workers } from "@/server/db/schema";
5	
6	const workerTurnChains = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
7	const conversationMutationChains = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
8	const backgroundTasks = new Set<Promise<void>>();
9	const backgroundTasksByRunId = new Map<string, Set<Promise<void>>>();
10	const conversationDeletionRequests = new Set<string>();
11	const completedConversationDeletionRequests = new Set<string>();
12	
13	export function isWorkerTurnSupersededError(error: unknown): boolean {
14	  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
15	  return /\bworker turn (?:was )?superseded by a newer worker turn\b/i.test(message);
16	}
17	
18	function runOnChain<T>(
19	  chains: Map<string, Promise<void>>,
20	  key: string,
21	  task: () => Promise<T>,
22	): Promise<T> {
23	  const previous = chains.get(key) ?? Promise.resolve();
24	  const next = previous.then(() => task());
25	  const tracked = next.then(() => undefined, () => undefined);
26	  chains.set(key, tracked);
27	  return next.finally(() => {
28	    if (chains.get(key) === tracked) {
29	      chains.delete(key);
30	    }
31	  });
32	}
33	
34	/**
35	 * Stop and steer must be instant. Before this existed, neither could actually
36	 * end a running turn: `askAgent` read its SSE stream to completion with no
37	 * abort path, so a cancel only *asked* the adapter to stop and then waited for
38	 * it, and the next turn sat behind the dead one on the FIFO chain.
39	 *
40	 * Every turn now runs with an AbortSignal published on this async-local store,
41	 * so anything the turn calls — however deeply nested — can pick it up without
42	 * threading a parameter through every call site. `abortWorkerTurn` trips it
43	 * synchronously, which tears down the in-flight fetch locally rather than
44	 * negotiating with an agent that may be wedged.
45	 */
46	const workerTurnSignals = new AsyncLocalStorage<AbortSignal>();
47	const workerTurnAborts = new Map<string, AbortController>();
48	
49	export class WorkerTurnAbortedError extends Error {
50	  readonly workerId: string;
51	
52	  constructor(workerId: string, reason?: string) {
53	    super(`Worker turn aborted for ${workerId}${reason ? `: ${reason}` : ""}`);
54	    this.name = "WorkerTurnAbortedError";
55	    this.workerId = workerId;
56	  }
57	}
58	
59	export function isWorkerTurnAbortedError(error: unknown): boolean {
60	  if (error instanceof WorkerTurnAbortedError) {
61	    return true;
62	  }
63	  const name = (error as { name?: unknown } | null)?.name;
64	  if (name === "AbortError" || name === "WorkerTurnAbortedError") {
65	    return true;
66	  }
67	  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error ?? "");
68	  return /\bworker turn aborted\b|\bthis operation was aborted\b/i.test(message);
69	}
70	
71	/** The AbortSignal of the turn running on this async context, if any. */
72	export function currentWorkerTurnSignal(): AbortSignal | undefined {
73	  return workerTurnSignals.getStore();
74	}
75	
76	export function runWorkerTurn<T>(workerId: string, task: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
77	  return runOnChain(workerTurnChains, workerId, async () => {
78	    const controller = new AbortController();
79	    workerTurnAborts.set(workerId, controller);
80	    try {
81	      return await workerTurnSignals.run(controller.signal, () => task(controller.signal));
82	    } finally {
83	      if (workerTurnAborts.get(workerId) === controller) {
84	        workerTurnAborts.delete(workerId);
85	      }
86	    }
87	  });
88	}
89	
90	/**
91	 * Trip the running turn's signal for `workerId`. Synchronous and local: it does
92	 * not talk to the agent runtime, so a wedged adapter cannot delay it. Returns
93	 * false when there was no live turn to abort.
94	 */
95	export function abortWorkerTurn(workerId: string, reason?: string): boolean {
96	  const controller = workerTurnAborts.get(workerId);
97	  if (!controller || controller.signal.aborted) {
98	    return false;
99	  }
100	  controller.abort(new WorkerTurnAbortedError(workerId, reason));
101	  return true;
102	}
103	
104	export function hasLiveWorkerTurn(workerId: string): boolean {
105	  const controller = workerTurnAborts.get(workerId);
106	  return Boolean(controller && !controller.signal.aborted);
107	}
108	
109	export function __resetWorkerTurnAbortsForTests() {
110	  workerTurnAborts.clear();
111	}
112	
113	/**
114	 * Read the worker's current turn fence value. Returns 0 when the worker
115	 * row is missing (treated as the default generation).
116	 */
117	export async function readWorkerTurnGeneration(workerId: string): Promise<number> {
118	  const record = await db
119	    .select({ turnGeneration: workers.turnGeneration })
120	    .from(workers)
121	    .where(eq(workers.id, workerId))
122	    .get();
123	  return record?.turnGeneration ?? 0;
124	}
125	
126	/**
127	 * Capture a worker turn generation when the worker exists. A null result means
128	 * the bridge request is not backed by a persisted conversation worker and
129	 * therefore cannot participate in the database turn fence.
130	 */
131	export async function captureWorkerTurnGeneration(workerId: string): Promise<number | null> {
132	  const record = await db
133	    .select({ turnGeneration: workers.turnGeneration })
134	    .from(workers)
135	    .where(eq(workers.id, workerId))
136	    .get();
137	  return record?.turnGeneration ?? null;
138	}
139	
140	/**
141	 * Atomically advance the worker turn fence and (optionally) reset persisted
142	 * worker state into a delivery-safe shape in the SAME mutation, so an
143	 * immediate queued delivery cannot observe stale `working` state after a
144	 * successful cancel. Returns the new generation value.
145	 *
146	 * Callers capture the returned generation for the new delivery; any older
147	 * in-flight completion that captured a smaller value must refuse to persist
148	 * terminal updates (see `isWorkerTurnGenerationCurrent`).
149	 */
150	export async function advanceWorkerTurnGeneration(
151	  workerId: string,
152	  reset?: { status?: string; clearCurrentText?: boolean; updatedAt?: Date },
153	): Promise<number> {
154	  await db.update(workers).set({
155	    turnGeneration: sql`${workers.turnGeneration} + 1`,
156	    ...(reset?.status !== undefined ? { status: reset.status } : {}),
157	    ...(reset?.clearCurrentText ? { currentText: "" } : {}),
158	    ...(reset?.updatedAt ? { updatedAt: reset.updatedAt } : {}),
159	  }).where(eq(workers.id, workerId));
160	  return readWorkerTurnGeneration(workerId);
161	}
162	
163	/**
164	 * True when `capturedGeneration` still matches the worker's persisted fence,
165	 * i.e. no newer interrupt delivery has superseded the captured turn. A missing
166	 * worker row reports `false` so stale callers stop persisting.
167	 */
168	export async function isWorkerTurnGenerationCurrent(
169	  workerId: string,
170	  capturedGeneration: number,
171	): Promise<boolean> {
172	  const record = await db
173	    .select({ turnGeneration: workers.turnGeneration })
174	    .from(workers)
175	    .where(eq(workers.id, workerId))
176	    .get();
177	  if (!record) {
178	    return false;
179	  }
180	  return record.turnGeneration === capturedGeneration;
181	}
182	
183	export function runConversationMutation<T>(runId: string, task: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
184	  return runOnChain(conversationMutationChains, runId, task);
185	}
186	
187	export function trackConversationBackgroundTask<T>(
188	  task: Promise<T>,
189	  options: { runId?: string } = {},
190	): Promise<T> {
191	  const runId = options.runId?.trim() || null;
192	  const tracked = task.then(() => undefined, () => undefined);
193	  backgroundTasks.add(tracked);
194	  if (runId) {
195	    const runTasks = backgroundTasksByRunId.get(runId) ?? new Set<Promise<void>>();
196	    runTasks.add(tracked);
197	    backgroundTasksByRunId.set(runId, runTasks);
198	  }
199	  void tracked.finally(() => {
200	    backgroundTasks.delete(tracked);
201	    if (runId) {
202	      const runTasks = backgroundTasksByRunId.get(runId);
203	      runTasks?.delete(tracked);
204	      if (!runTasks || runTasks.size === 0) {
205	        backgroundTasksByRunId.delete(runId);
206	      }
207	      if (completedConversationDeletionRequests.has(runId) && !backgroundTasksByRunId.has(runId)) {
208	        completedConversationDeletionRequests.delete(runId);
209	        conversationDeletionRequests.delete(runId);
210	      }
211	    }
212	  });
213	  return task;
214	}
215	
216	export function requestConversationDeletion(runId: string): void {
217	  completedConversationDeletionRequests.delete(runId);
218	  conversationDeletionRequests.add(runId);
219	}
220	
221	export function isConversationDeletionRequested(runId: string): boolean {
222	  return conversationDeletionRequests.has(runId);
223	}
224	
225	export function completeConversationDeletion(runId: string): void {
226	  if (!backgroundTasksByRunId.has(runId)) {
227	    conversationDeletionRequests.delete(runId);
228	    completedConversationDeletionRequests.delete(runId);
229	    return;
230	  }
231	  completedConversationDeletionRequests.add(runId);
232	}
233	
234	export async function waitForConversationBackgroundTasks(
235	  runId: string,
236	  timeoutMs = 30_000,
237	): Promise<void> {
238	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
239	  while ((backgroundTasksByRunId.get(runId)?.size ?? 0) > 0) {
240	    const remaining = deadline - Date.now();
241	    const runTasks = Array.from(backgroundTasksByRunId.get(runId) ?? []);
242	    if (remaining <= 0) {
243	      throw new Error(
244	        `Timed out waiting for ${runTasks.length} background task(s) for conversation ${runId}`,
245	      );
246	    }
247	    await Promise.race([
248	      Promise.all(runTasks),
249	      new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(remaining, 25))),
250	    ]);
251	  }
252	}
253	
254	export async function waitForConversationBackgroundTasksForTests(timeoutMs = 1_000): Promise<void> {
255	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
256	  while (backgroundTasks.size > 0) {
257	    const remaining = deadline - Date.now();
258	    if (remaining <= 0) {
259	      throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for ${backgroundTasks.size} conversation background task(s)`);
260	    }
261	    await Promise.race([
262	      Promise.all(Array.from(backgroundTasks)),
263	      new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(remaining, 25))),
264	    ]);
265	  }
266	}
267	
268	export function __resetWorkerTurnChainsForTests() {
269	  workerTurnChains.clear();
270	  conversationMutationChains.clear();
271	  backgroundTasks.clear();
272	  backgroundTasksByRunId.clear();
273	  conversationDeletionRequests.clear();
274	  completedConversationDeletionRequests.clear();
275	}
```

```text
1	import { index, integer, primaryKey, real, sqliteTable, text, uniqueIndex } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
2	
3	export const plans = sqliteTable('plans', {
4	  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
5	  path: text('path').notNull(),
6	  status: text('status').notNull(), // 'pending', 'running', 'done', 'failed'
7	  createdAt: integer('created_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }).notNull(),
8	  updatedAt: integer('updated_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }).notNull(),
9	});
10	
11	export const runs = sqliteTable('runs', {
12	  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
13	  planId: text('plan_id').references(() => plans.id).notNull(),
14	  sessionType: text('session_type').notNull().default('omni'),
15	  mode: text('mode').notNull().default('implementation'),
16	  // For Omni runs (mode === 'implementation'): 'planning' = interactive
17	  // planner phase with the supervisor dormant; 'implementing' (or null for
18	  // legacy rows) = supervisor-driven implementation phase.
19	  phase: text('phase'),
20	  projectPath: text('project_path'),
21	  title: text('title'),
22	  preferredWorkerType: text('preferred_worker_type'),
23	  preferredWorkerModel: text('preferred_worker_model'),
24	  preferredWorkerEffort: text('preferred_worker_effort'),
25	  preferredWorkerAccountId: text('preferred_worker_account_id'),
26	  allowedWorkerTypes: text('allowed_worker_types'),
27	  specPath: text('spec_path'),
28	  artifactPlanPath: text('artifact_plan_path'),
29	  plannerArtifactsJson: text('planner_artifacts_json'),
30	  plannerReadinessVerdictJson: text('planner_readiness_verdict_json'),
31	  parentRunId: text('parent_run_id'),
32	  forkedFromMessageId: text('forked_from_message_id'),
33	  autoCommitMilestones: integer('auto_commit_milestones', { mode: 'boolean' }).notNull().default(false),
34	  pushOnCommit: integer('push_on_commit', { mode: 'boolean' }).notNull().default(false),
35	  gitBaselineJson: text('git_baseline_json'),
36	  gitWorkspaceJson: text('git_workspace_json'),
37	  completionCommitSha: text('completion_commit_sha'),
38	  status: text('status').notNull(), // 'running', 'done', 'failed'
39	  failedAt: integer('failed_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }),
40	  lastError: text('last_error'),
41	  archivedAt: integer('archived_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }),
42	  memoryMetadataRevision: integer('memory_metadata_revision').notNull().default(0),
43	  lastMemoryConsolidationAt: integer('last_memory_consolidation_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }),
44	  createdAt: integer('created_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }).notNull(),
45	  // Conversation-visible activity: the last user message or the last agent turn
46	  // that finished. Distinct from `updatedAt`, which every piece of background
47	  // bookkeeping bumps (memory consolidation, watchdog, auto-commit, quota
48	  // recovery, title generation...) and so cannot order the sidebar. Maintained
49	  // by SQL triggers — see `initializeDatabaseSchema`.
50	  lastActivityAt: integer('last_activity_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }),
51	  updatedAt: integer('updated_at', { mode: 'timestamp' }).notNull(),
52	});
53	
54	export const runGoals = sqliteTable('run_goals', {
55	  runId: text('run_id').primaryKey().references(() => runs.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
56	  goalId: text('goal_id').notNull(),
57	  objective: text('objective').notNull(),
58	  status: text('status').notNull(),
59	  revision: integer('revision').notNull(),
60	  leaseGeneration: integer('lease_generation').notNull().default(0),
61	  planJson: text('plan_json').notNull().default('[]'),
62	  planSourceJson: text('plan_source_json').notNull().default('{"kind":"none"}'),
63	  workerId: text('worker_id').references(() => workers.id, { onDelete: 'set null' }),
64	  acpSessionId: text('acp_session_id'),
65	  capabilitiesJson: text('capabilities_json').notNull().default('{}'),
66	  validationStateJson: text('validation_state_json'),
67	  lastError: text('last_error'),
68	  controlMethod: text('control_method'),
69	  transitionSource: text('transition_source').notNull().default('api'),

120	
121	export const workers = sqliteTable('workers', {

126	  cwd: text('cwd').notNull(),

131	  initialPrompt: text('initial_prompt').notNull().default(''),

134	  currentText: text('current_text').notNull().default(''),
135	  lastText: text('last_text').notNull().default(''),
136	  bridgeSessionId: text('bridge_session_id'),
137	  bridgeSessionMode: text('bridge_session_mode'),
138	  effectiveLaunchModel: text('effective_launch_model'),
139	  effectiveLaunchEffort: text('effective_launch_effort'),
140	  launchCredentialSource: text('launch_credential_source'),
141	  // Monotonic fence advanced every time a turn is interrupted. A delivery
142	  // captures the generation it started under; stale completions from an
143	  // interrupted (older) turn compare their captured value and refuse to
144	  // persist terminal status/queue/response updates when it no longer matches.
145	  turnGeneration: integer('turn_generation').notNull().default(0),
146	  // Seq ranges this worker wrote for branches the user discarded via
147	  // retry/edit, as JSON `[{"from":n,"through":m}]`. A rewind to message M
148	  // supersedes everything from M's original delivery through the stream tip at
149	  // recovery time; the re-delivered message and its new answer land after that
150	  // (on a fresh worker, or further down this worker's stream when the ACP
151	  // session is resumed in place). Readers hide superseded entries so the
152	  // conversation shows one coherent thread instead of every abandoned attempt.
```

```text
11	  runMilestoneAutoCommit: mockRunMilestoneAutoCommit,
12	}));

19	
20	describe("directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput", () => {
21	  beforeEach(async () => {
22	    mockRunMilestoneAutoCommit.mockReset();
23	    await db.delete(runs);
24	    await db.delete(plans);
25	  });
26	
27	  it("does not treat quoted product copy as a request for user input", () => {
28	    expect(directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput({
29	      currentText: "I replaced the empty-state heading with a translated \"What shall we build, Network School?\" line and the logo above it.",
30	    })).toBe(false);
31	  });
32	
33	  it("does not infer pending input from prose requests", () => {
34	    expect(directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput({
35	      currentText: "Before I proceed, please confirm which approach you want.",
36	    })).toBe(false);
37	  });
38	
39	  it("does not treat optional post-completion follow-up as blocking input", () => {
40	    expect(directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput({
41	      currentText: [
42	        "Done. The modified files are grouped into three logical commits and pushed to `origin/master`.",
43	        "Let me know if you actually want either committed or added to `.gitignore`.",
44	      ].join("\n"),
45	    })).toBe(false);
46	  });
47	
48	  it("detects structured pending human input only", () => {
49	    expect(directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput({
50	      outputEntries: [
51	        { type: "elicitation", status: "pending", text: "Question for user" },
52	      ],
53	    })).toBe(true);
54	    expect(directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput({
55	      outputEntries: [
56	        { type: "permission", status: "pending", text: "Permission requested", raw: { requestId: 64 } },
57	        { type: "permission", status: "approved", text: "Permission approved", raw: { requestId: 64 } },
58	      ],
59	    })).toBe(false);
60	    expect(directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput({
61	      outputEntries: [
62	        { type: "permission", status: "approved", text: "Permission approved" },
63	      ],
64	    })).toBe(false);
65	    expect(directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput({
66	      pendingElicitations: [{ requestId: 1 }],
67	    })).toBe(true);
68	  });
69	
70	  it("parks a quota-blocked worker instead of marking the conversation done", () => {
71	    // Regression: `cred-exhausted` is idle but not finished. Falling through to
72	    // "done" cleared the run out of `quota_waiting`, which dropped the "waiting
73	    // for quota reset" banner and left the conversation looking complete while
74	    // it was actually blocked on the provider window.
75	    expect(resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput({
76	      workerStatus: "cred-exhausted",
77	      lastText: "You've hit your session limit · resets 4pm (Europe/Madrid)",
78	    })).toBe("quota_waiting");
79	  });
80	
81	  it("still surfaces pending human input ahead of a quota block", () => {

83	      workerStatus: "cred-exhausted",
84	      pendingElicitations: [{ requestId: 1 }],
85	    })).toBe("awaiting_user");
86	  });
87	
88	  it("keeps active direct workers running after structured input is answered", () => {

110	    })).toBe("running");
111	  });
112
```

## Claude subagent agent-aad12317036f0743f: Claude tool result: toolu_01DUNqV2viMABa9LPBudX3y1 · 2026-08-16T19:32:01.872Z

```text
1	const recoveryMutationChains = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
2	
3	/**
4	 * Serialize only the short persisted mutations shared by quota recovery and
5	 * user Stop. This must never wrap bridge spawn/ask work: Stop needs to acquire
6	 * the fence immediately so it can terminalize the run and abort that work.
7	 *
8	 * This deliberately does not reuse the broader conversation mutation chain:
9	 * quota errors can surface while that chain is awaiting an external turn.
10	 * Recovery helpers must keep this fence non-nested and short-lived.
11	 */
12	export function runQuotaRecoveryMutation<T>(runId: string, task: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
13	  const previous = recoveryMutationChains.get(runId) ?? Promise.resolve();
14	  const next = previous.then(() => task());
15	  const tracked = next.then(() => undefined, () => undefined);
16	  recoveryMutationChains.set(runId, tracked);
17	  return next.finally(() => {
18	    if (recoveryMutationChains.get(runId) === tracked) {
19	      recoveryMutationChains.delete(runId);
20	    }
21	  });
22	}
23	
24	export function resetQuotaRecoveryMutationsForTests() {
25	  recoveryMutationChains.clear();
26	}
27
```

```text
70	function isAgentAlreadyExistsError(error: unknown, workerId: string) {
71	  const message = formatErrorMessage(error).toLowerCase();
72	  return message.includes("agent already exists") && message.includes(workerId.toLowerCase());

75	function appendWorkerOutput(existingLog: string | null | undefined, nextChunk: string) {
76	  if (!nextChunk) {
77	    return existingLog ?? "";
78	  }
79	
80	  if (!existingLog) {
81	    return nextChunk;
82	  }
83	
84	  const separator = existingLog.endsWith("\n") || nextChunk.startsWith("\n") ? "" : "\n";
85	  return `${existingLog}${separator}${nextChunk}`;
86	}

521	/**
522	 * Resume a non-implementation (direct/commit) run once its quota window has
523	 * reopened, and settle the run status afterwards.
524	 *
525	 * Callers must decide *whether* to resume from the open `quota_exhausted`
526	 * incident — never from `runs.status`. Direct-run status is rewritten by
527	 * conversation sync and the worker-output resolver, neither of which knows
528	 * about quota, so a run parked as `quota_waiting` can be flipped to
529	 * `running`/`done` before the reset lands.
530	 */
531	export async function resumeDirectRunAfterQuotaReset(args: {
532	  run: typeof runs.$inferSelect;
533	  source: DirectQuotaResumeSource;
534	}): Promise<ResumeQuotaWorkersResult> {
535	  const transitionRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.run.id, async () => {
536	    const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({ runId: args.run.id, now: new Date() });
537	    if (refused) return refused;
538	    await db.update(runs).set({
539	      status: "running",
540	      failedAt: null,
541	      lastError: null,
542	      updatedAt: new Date(),
543	    }).where(eq(runs.id, args.run.id));
544	    return null;
545	  });
546	  if (transitionRefusal) return transitionRefusal;
547	
548	  const result = await resumeQuotaExhaustedWorkers({ run: args.run });
549	  if (result.state !== "none" || result.resumedCount !== 0) {
550	    return result;
551	  }
552	
553	  const reason = MISSING_SESSION_REASONS[args.source];
554	  const missingSessionRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(args.run.id, async () => {
555	    const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({ runId: args.run.id, now: new Date() });
556	    if (refused) return refused;
557	    await db.update(runs).set({
558	      status: "needs_recovery",
559	      lastError: reason,
560	      updatedAt: new Date(),
561	    }).where(eq(runs.id, args.run.id));
562	    await recordExecutionEvent({
563	      runId: args.run.id,
564	      eventType: "quota_resume_missing_session",
565	      details: { summary: reason, reason: MISSING_SESSION_EVENT_REASONS[args.source] },
566	    });
567	    emitNamedEvent({
568	      kind: "error.surfaced",
569	      code: "recovery.needs_user",
570	      message: reason,
571	      surface: "banner",
572	      runId: args.run.id,
573	    });
574	    return null;
575	  });
576	  if (missingSessionRefusal) return missingSessionRefusal;
577	  return result;
578	}
579	
580	function incidentResumeAt(details: string | null | undefined) {
581	  if (!details) {
582	    return null;
583	  }
584	  try {
585	    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(details);
586	    const value = parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed)
587	      ? (parsed as { resumeAt?: unknown }).resumeAt
588	      : null;
589	    if (typeof value !== "string") {
590	      return null;
591	    }
592	    const resumeAt = new Date(value);
593	    return Number.isNaN(resumeAt.getTime()) ? null : resumeAt;
594	  } catch {
595	    return null;
596	  }
597	}
598	
599	export async function hasResumableQuotaIncident(runId: string) {
600	  const incident = await db.select().from(recoveryIncidents).where(and(
601	    eq(recoveryIncidents.runId, runId),
602	    eq(recoveryIncidents.kind, "quota_exhausted"),
603	    inArray(recoveryIncidents.status, [...RESUMABLE_QUOTA_INCIDENT_STATUSES]),
604	  )).limit(1).get();
605	  return Boolean(incident);
606	}
607	
608	/**
609	 * Safety net for direct runs whose durable quota wake never delivered a resume
610	 * — the wake row is consumed before the handler decides what to do, so any bail
611	 * after the claim destroys the only retry record. Sweeps every open
612	 * `quota_exhausted` incident whose reset has already elapsed and resumes it.
613	 *
614	 * Runs with a future durable wake are left alone; that wake is still the
615	 * primary path. `claimRecoveryIncident` debounces re-entry for 60s, and a
616	 * failed resume moves the incident to `needs_user`, so repeated sweeps cannot
617	 * spin on the same run.
618	 */
619	export async function resumeElapsedQuotaWaits(options: { now?: Date } = {}) {
620	  const now = options.now ?? new Date();
621	  const incidents = await db.select().from(recoveryIncidents).where(and(
622	    eq(recoveryIncidents.kind, "quota_exhausted"),
623	    inArray(recoveryIncidents.status, [...RESUMABLE_QUOTA_INCIDENT_STATUSES]),
624	  ));
625	
626	  const elapsedRunIds = Array.from(new Set<string>(incidents
627	    .filter((incident) => {
628	      const resumeAt = incidentResumeAt(incident.details);
629	      return resumeAt !== null && resumeAt.getTime() <= now.getTime();
630	    })
631	    .map((incident) => incident.runId)));
632	
633	  let sweptCount = 0;
634	  let clearedCount = 0;
635	  for (const runId of elapsedRunIds) {
636	    const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
637	    if (!run || run.archivedAt) {
638	      continue;
639	    }
640	    if (normalizeRunStatus(run.status) === "cancelled" || normalizeRunStatus(run.status) === "canceled") {
641	      continue;
642	    }
643	    if (await hasFutureDurableSupervisorWake(runId, now.getTime())) {
644	      continue;
645	    }
646	
647	    // Implementation runs park at `quota_waiting`, which the watchdog loop
648	    // below deliberately skips — so a lost wake strands them exactly like a
649	    // direct conversation. Re-arm a due wake rather than resuming here, so the
650	    // normal (well-tested) wake handler does the work. This converges: the
651	    // handler either resolves the incident or moves the run out of
652	    // `quota_waiting`, and either outcome stops matching on the next sweep.
653	    if (run.mode === "implementation") {
654	      if (isRunnableImplementationRun(run) && normalizeRunStatus(run.status) === "quota_waiting") {
655	        const sweepRefusal = await runQuotaRecoveryMutation(runId, async () => {
656	          const refused = await refuseLateQuotaRecovery({ runId, now });
657	          if (refused) return refused;
658	          await scheduleDurableSupervisorWakeAt({
659	            runId,
660	            wakeAt: now,
661	            reason: "quota_wait",
662	            source: "watchdog-sweep",
663	            force: true,
664	          });
665	          emitNamedEvent({ kind: "supervisor.quota_wake_swept", runId, status: run.status, action: "rescheduled" });
666	          return null;
667	        });
668	        if (!sweepRefusal) sweptCount += 1;
669	      }
670	      continue;
671	    }
672	
673	    const runWorkers = await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.runId, runId));
674	    const blockedWorker = runWorkers.find((worker) => (
675	      worker.status === "cred-exhausted" && Boolean(worker.bridgeSessionId?.trim())
676	    ));
677	    if (!blockedWorker) {
678	      // The worker already came back on its own (or has no resumable session),
679	      // so the incident is stale bookkeeping that only keeps the "waiting for
680	      // quota reset" banner up. Close it without touching the agent — resuming
681	      // a conversation the user considers finished would start unrequested work.
682	      const { resolvedCount } = await clearResolvedQuotaIncidents(runId, { now });
683	      if (resolvedCount > 0) {
684	        emitNamedEvent({ kind: "supervisor.quota_wake_swept", runId, status: run.status, action: "cleared" });
685	        clearedCount += 1;
686	      }
687	      continue;
688	    }
689	
690	    const resumeResult = await resumeDirectRunAfterQuotaReset({ run, source: "watchdog_sweep" });
691	    if (resumeResult.state === "ignored") {
692	      continue;
693	    }
694	    emitNamedEvent({ kind: "supervisor.quota_wake_swept", runId, status: run.status, action: "resumed" });
695	    await recordExecutionEvent({
696	      runId,
697	      eventType: "quota_wait_swept",
698	      details: {
699	        summary: "Quota reset had elapsed with no scheduled wake; resumed from the open incident.",
700	        runStatus: run.status,
701	        workerId: blockedWorker.id,
702	        reason: "watchdog_sweep",
703	      },
704	    });
705	    sweptCount += 1;
706	  }
707	
708	  return { sweptCount, clearedCount };
709	}
710
```

## Summary

I've completed a thorough investigation of the worker status patterns and the correct ordering for marking workers as busy/working around `askAgent` calls. Here is the precise writeup with file:line citations:

---

### 1. **send-message.ts - Normal User-Message Delivery Path**

**File**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/send-message.ts`

**The precise sequence for direct/planning runs:**

1. **Activate run status and persist message** (lines 1662–1691)
   - Calls `activateRunAndPersistMessage()` which updates `runs.status` to "running" or "working"
   - Persists user message to DB

2. **Capture expected turn generation** (line 1704)
   - `const expectedTurnGeneration = worker.turnGeneration;`

3. **Wrap in runWorkerTurn** (lines 1708, 1749, 1777)
   - `await runWorkerTurn(worker.id, () => continueWorkerConversation(...))`
   - This acquires the per-worker turn chain BEFORE calling continueWorkerConversation

4. **Inside continueWorkerConversation → activate worker status to "working"** (lines 956–962)
   - Inside continueWorkerConversation (line 956):
     ```typescript
     const activatedWorker = await db.update(workers).set({
       status: "working",
       updatedAt: new Date(),
     }).where(and(
       eq(workers.id, worker.id),
       eq(workers.turnGeneration, expectedTurnGeneration),
     )).returning({ id: workers.id }).get();
     ```
   - If this fails (worker superseded), returns early

5. **Call askDirectWorkerWithResume** (lines 995–1002)
   - No explicit status update immediately before askAgent
   - Worker already marked "working" above

6. **After askAgent returns, update worker status** (lines 1034–1037)
   - ```typescript
     await db.update(workers).set({
       status: snapshot?.state ?? response.state,
       updatedAt: new Date(),
     }).where(eq(workers.id, worker.id));
     ```

7. **Call updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput** (lines 1062–1074)
   - This reconciles run status based on final worker status

---

### 2. **queued-messages.ts - Queue Drain Path**

**File**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/queued-messages.ts`

**The sequence in drainQueuedWorkerMessages:**

1. **Claim the queue row** (lines 1240–1250)
   - Atomically CAS the message status from "pending" to "delivering"

2. **Update run status** (lines 1251–1256)
   - Sets run status to "working" or "running"

3. **Wrap in runWorkerTurn** (line 1304)
   - `await runWorkerTurn(workerId, async () => { ... })`
   - Again, this acquires the turn chain BEFORE updating worker status

4. **Inside the turn callback, update worker status to "working"** (lines 1305–1308)
   - ```typescript
     await db.update(workers).set({
       status: "working",
       updatedAt: startedAt,
     }).where(eq(workers.id, workerId));
     ```

5. **Call askAgent** (lines 1372–1375)
   - Only after status is "working"

6. **Call persistDeliveredWorkerResponse** (lines 1377–1383)
   - Inside this (line 409–412), updates worker status to final state:
     ```typescript
     await db.update(workers).set({
       status: snapshot?.state ?? response.state,
       updatedAt: deliveredAt,
     }).where(eq(workers.id, workerId));
     ```

7. **Call updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput** (inside persistDeliveredWorkerResponse, lines 422–434)

---

### 3. **runWorkerTurn - Definition and Behavior**

**File**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/worker-turn-gate.ts` (lines 76–88)

```typescript
export function runWorkerTurn<T>(workerId: string, task: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  return runOnChain(workerTurnChains, workerId, async () => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    workerTurnAborts.set(workerId, controller);
    try {
      return await workerTurnSignals.run(controller.signal, () => task(controller.signal));
    } finally {
      if (workerTurnAborts.get(workerId) === controller) {
        workerTurnAborts.delete(workerId);
      }
    }
  });
}
```

**What it does:**
- Acquires a FIFO **per-worker turn chain** (line 77, via `runOnChain`)
- Serializes all turns for a single worker
- Creates an `AbortController` and publishes it for early cancellation
- **Does NOT itself set any worker status** — that's up to the caller
- **Does NOT bump `workers.turn_generation`** — callers do that (line 155, in `advanceWorkerTurnGeneration`)
- Used to prevent concurrent `askAgent` calls and ensure Stop/Steer can interrupt

**Key:** The turn gate is purely about FIFO ordering and abort signals. Status management and turn generation are caller responsibilities.

---

### 4. **direct-run-status.ts - resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput & updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput**

**File**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/conversations/direct-run-status.ts`

**resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput (lines 148–168):**

This function examines worker output and decides what the run's status should be:

```typescript
export function resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput(source: WorkerOutputSource) {
  if (directWorkerOutputHasPendingHumanInput(source)) {
    return "awaiting_user";  // Structured permission or elicitation pending
  }

  // Quota-blocked worker is idle but not finished
  if (normalizeWorkerStatus(source.workerStatus) === "cred-exhausted") {
    return "quota_waiting";
  }

  if (ACTIVE_DIRECT_WORKER_STATUSES.has(normalizeWorkerStatus(source.workerStatus))) {
    return "running";  // "starting", "working", "stuck", "recovering"
  }

  // Only once the worker has stopped: a question mid-turn is just narration.
  return directWorkerOutputAsksBlockingQuestion(source) ? "awaiting_user" : "done";
}
```

**Worker statuses that cause run to resolve to "done":** (implicit from code)
- Worker status is NOT in `ACTIVE_DIRECT_WORKER_STATUSES` (which are "starting", "working", "stuck", "recovering")
- Worker status is NOT "cred-exhausted"
- Worker does NOT have pending human input
- Worker does NOT end with a blocking question

**Worker statuses that protect from being marked "done":**
- `"starting"` – ACTIVE
- `"working"` – ACTIVE
- `"stuck"` – ACTIVE
- `"recovering"` – ACTIVE
- `"cred-exhausted"` – Special case: returns "quota_waiting", not "done"

**updateDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput (lines 170–280):**

- Reads the current run and any active quota incident
- If a quota incident is active and run is in "quota_waiting", preserves that state (lines 191–235)
- Otherwise calls `resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput` and persists the result (lines 238–246)
- Emits named events for status transitions (lines 249–277)

---

### 5. **Valid Worker Status Values**

**File**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/db/schema.ts` (lines 121–158)

The schema comment (line 125) documents the enum as a text column:
```typescript
status: text('status').notNull(), // 'idle', 'working', 'stuck', 'cred-exhausted'
```

**Observed valid values across codebase:**
- `"idle"` – Worker is idle and ready
- `"working"` – Worker is actively processing a turn
- `"stuck"` – Worker is stuck (watchdog detected)
- `"cred-exhausted"` / `"cred-exhausted"` – Worker's quota/credentials exhausted (normalized in queries)
- `"starting"` – Worker is starting up
- `"cancelled"` / `"canceled"` – User stopped the worker
- `"done"` – Worker finished naturally
- `"error"` – Worker encountered an error
- `"failed"` – Worker failed
- `"recovering"` – Worker is in recovery (from direct-run-status.ts line 31)

**Normalization helper** (send-message.ts, lines 114–116):
```typescript
function normalizeWorkerStatus(status: string | null | undefined) {
  return status?.trim().toLowerCase().split(":")[0]?.trim() ?? "";
}
```
This strips colons and trailing qualifiers, e.g., `"working:foo"` → `"working"`.

---

### 6. **Existing Tests for Quota Resume**

**Found test file**: `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/tests/conversations/direct-run-status.test.ts`

**Key test: "parks a quota-blocked worker instead of marking the conversation done"** (lines 70–79)
```typescript
it("parks a quota-blocked worker instead of marking the conversation done", () => {
  // Regression: `cred-exhausted` is idle but not finished. Falling through to
  // "done" cleared the run out of `quota_waiting`, which dropped the "waiting
  // for quota reset" banner and left the conversation looking complete while
  // it was actually blocked on the provider window.
  expect(resolveDirectRunStatusFromWorkerOutput({
    workerStatus: "cred-exhausted",
    lastText: "You've hit your session limit · resets 4pm (Europe/Madrid)",
  })).toBe("quota_waiting");
});
```

**Another test: "still surfaces pending human input ahead of a quota block"** (lines 81–86)
- Confirms that pending elicitations override the quota wait status

**Additional test files mentioning quota/resume:**
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/tests/ui/conversation-actions.test.ts`
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/tests/app/use-run-recovery-state.test.ts`
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/tests/server/conversations-sync.test.ts`
- `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/tests/api/conversation-messages-route.test.ts`

The quota resume logic itself is well-integrated into the recovery control planes but does not have a dedicated standalone test suite for quota resumption specifically.

---

### **Key Pattern for Quota Resume Implementation**

Based on `/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/quota/worker-resume.ts` (resumeQuotaExhaustedWorkers):

The pattern mirrors the normal send-message flow:

1. **Resume the worker session** (lines 279–295): spawn/getAgent
2. **Wrap the prompt in runWorkerTurn** (line 132 in promptResumedQuotaWorker):
   ```typescript
   const response = await runWorkerTurn(args.worker.id, () => askAgent(...))
   ```
3. **Update worker status inside the quota recovery mutation** (lines 305–308):
   ```typescript
   await db.update(workers).set({
     status: resumedWorker.state,  // from spawn response
     ...
   }).where(and(
     eq(workers.id, worker.id),
     eq(workers.turnGeneration, turnGeneration),
   ))
   ```
4. **Mark recovery incident resolved** (lines 332–342): Only after successful status persistence
5. **If idle, prompt the worker** (line 352): Call `promptResumedQuotaWorker`, which again wraps in `runWorkerTurn` and updates status to "working" before askAgent

---

This establishes the correct pattern: **status update → runWorkerTurn → askAgent inside turn callback → update status with response state → resolve recovery.**

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-16T19:32:56.928Z

This confirms the pattern: both send-message.ts and queued-messages.ts set status to "working" inside runWorkerTurn before calling askAgent, but the quota resume path skips straight to askAgent without that step, leaving the worker's idle state resolving to "done" instead of an active status. The fix for promptResumedQuotaWorker is to add that same CAS update to "working" guarded by turnGeneration before askAgent runs.

I also need to think through edge cases: if askAgent throws or gets refused, the worker status needs to reset from "working" to something sane rather than getting stuck, and the existing error handling via handleWorkerQuotaExhaustion should cover that. Additionally, moving the supervisor intervention recording and input appending to happen before the ask (rather than after) would let users see the resume prompt in the stream while work is happening, fixing the issue where the conversation looks empty during resume.

Now I want to check the full function and catch block more closely, along with how worker_prompted is used in wake.ts's completion reset event types, so I'm going to read through the relevant line ranges in both files.

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-16T19:33:15.813Z

Now I see the fix clearly: in `promptResumedQuotaWorker`, I need to move the logic that marks the worker busy, records the prompt, and appends supervisor input to happen before the askAgent call, inside the turn itself, so the state updates occur prior to awaiting the response.

I'm updating the worker's status to "working" conditioned on matching the expected turn generation, and if that update affects no rows, I need to throw a superseded-turn error — I should check how `isWorkerTurnSupersededError` is constructed elsewhere, like in send-message.ts, to match the right error shape.

I also realize I should move the `worker_prompted` event recording and supervisor input append to happen before the ask call rather than after, since that's when the prompt is actually delivered — though I need to think through what happens if the ask then fails, and make sure the worker status gets reset properly rather than staying stuck at "working" in non-quota, non-transient failure cases. I want to trace through the catch block's handling of quota exhaustion versus other errors, and also check the superseded-turn predicate logic in worker-turn-gate before moving on to the second bug.

```text
135	export function directWorkerOutputAsksBlockingQuestion(source: WorkerOutputSource) {
```

## Claude thinking · 2026-08-16T19:33:42.506Z

Now I see the key detail: quotaRecoveryOwnsPersistedState only applies while the quota incident is active, and that incident resolved at 18:00:03, removing the protection so the worker's idle→done transition could take effect. The storm happens because while the incident was open and the worker was cred-exhausted, something kept repeatedly CAS-ing run.status back to quota_waiting, meaning another process was continuously overriding the status.

I'm tracing that secondary bug — some writer sets status to "running" every second, likely an SSE-driven sync loop, which this code then resets back to quota_waiting. I'll focus the fix on the primary bug the user reported, flag the storm as a separate issue, and now start drafting the fix for promptResumedQuotaWorker, building the prompt for the resumed worker.

Writing worker activation logic...

Then I need to move the supervisor intervention recording, input appending, and event logging inside the activation block before calling askAgent, and strip those same calls out of the post-ask code. I'm also reconsidering whether the refuseLateQuotaRecovery guard should still fire now that the run shouldn't go terminal mid-turn — it should stay as a safety check, but I want to make sure it doesn't trigger spuriously.

If the user genuinely cancels mid-turn, the response gets discarded but I need to verify the worker status gets properly reset rather than left stuck in "working." I want to check whether refuseLateQuotaRecovery already handles resetting worker status, so I should look at that function's implementation before deciding how to wire this up, and I'll also need to add a test following the project's existing conventions.

A unit test for promptResumedQuotaWorker would require heavy mocking, and the lifecycle scenario tests drive HTTP/SSE with real quota blocks which seems hard to set up here. I want to check how existing tests mock the bridge client for quota-related paths, so I'll search for similar patterns in the server test directory while also reading through recovery.ts.
